== @ISA

[.book-title]
The Book of the Prophet Isaiah

=== @1

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judah’s Rebellion

[.parallel]
(<<2 Chronicles 28:5–15>>)

@ The vision of Isaiah the son of Amoz, which he saw concerning Judah and Jerusalem in ##the days##note:x[<<2 Chr 26:1–23>>] of Uzziah, Jotham, Ahaz, __and__ Hezekiah, kings of Judah.

@ ##Hear##note:x[<<Deut 30:19; 32:1; Mic 1:2>>], O heavens, and give ear, O earth: for the LORD hath spoken, I have nourished and brought up children, and they have rebelled against me.
@ The ##ox##note:x[<<Jer 8:7>>] knoweth his owner, and the ass his master’s crib: ##__but__ Israel##note:x[<<Isa 44:18; Jer 4:22>>] doth not know, my people doth not consider.
@ Ah sinful nation, a people ##laden##note:tn[Heb. of heaviness] with iniquity, a seed of evildoers, children that are corrupters: they have ##forsaken##note:x[<<Jer 2:13, 17>>] the LORD, they have provoked ##the Holy##note:x[<<Isa 5:24>>] One of Israel unto anger, they are gone away backward.note:tn[__gone…__: Heb. alienated, or, separated]

@ ¶ Why ##should##note:x[<<Jer 5:3; Ezek 24:13>>] ye be stricken any more? ye will revolt more and more: ##the whole##note:x[<<Dan 9:8–11>>] head is sick, and the whole heart faint.note:tn[__revolt…__: Heb. increase revolt]
@ From the sole of the foot even unto the head __there is__ no soundness in it; __but__ wounds, and ##bruises##note:x[<<Jer 6:14>>], and putrifying sores: ##they have##note:x[<<Jer 6:14; 33:6>>] not been closed, neither bound up, neither mollified with ##ointment##note:tn[or, oil].
@ Your country __is__ desolate, your cities __are__ burned with fire: your land, ##strangers##note:x[<<Deut 28:33, 43; Lam 5:2>>] devour it in your presence, and __it is__ desolate, as overthrown by strangers.note:tn[__overthrown…__: Heb. the overthrow of]
@ And the ##daughter##note:x[<<Zech 2:10; 9:9; John 12:15>>] of Zion is left as a cottage in a vineyard, as a lodge in a garden of cucumbers, as a besieged city.
@ Except the LORD of hosts had ##left##note:x[<<Rom 9:29>>] unto us ##a very##note:x[<<Isa 37:31–32>>] small remnant, ##we should##note:x[<<Gen 19:24>>] have been as Sodom, __and__ we should have been like unto Gomorrah.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Meaningless Offerings

@ ¶ ##Hear##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:19–23>>] the word of the LORD, ye rulers of ##Sodom##note:x[<<Deut 32:32; Rev 11:8>>]; give ear unto the law of our God, ye people of Gomorrah.
@ To ##what purpose##note:x[<<1 Sam 15:22; Jer 6:20; Amos 5:21>>] __is__ the multitude of your sacrifices unto me? saith the LORD: I am full of the burnt offerings of rams, and the fat of fed beasts; and I delight not in the blood of bullocks, or of lambs, or of he goats.note:tn[__he goats__: Heb. great he goats]
@ ##When##note:x[<<Exod 23:17; 34:23; Isa 58:1–2>>] ye come to appear before me, who hath required this at your hand, to tread my courts?note:tn[__to appear__: Heb. to be seen]
@ Bring no more ##vain##note:x[<<Mal 1:10; Matt 15:9>>] oblations; ##incense##note:x[<<Isa 66:3>>] is an abomination unto me; the new moons and sabbaths, the calling of assemblies, I cannot away with; __it is__ ##iniquity##note:tn[or, grief], even the solemn meeting.
@ Your new moons and your appointed feasts ##my soul##note:x[<<Amos 5:21>>] hateth: they are a trouble unto me; ##I am weary##note:x[<<Isa 43:24>>] to bear __them__.
@ And ##when##note:x[<<Isa 59:2; Mic 3:4>>] ye spread forth ##your hands##note:x[<<Isa 59:2–3>>], I will hide mine eyes from you: yea, when ye make many prayers, I will not hear: your hands are full of ##blood##note:tn[Heb. bloods].note:tn[__make…__: Heb. multiply prayer]

@ ¶ Wash you, make you clean; ##put away##note:x[<<Isa 55:6–7; 1 Pet 2:1>>] the evil of your doings from before mine eyes; ##cease##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:11>>] to do evil;
@ Learn to do well; ##seek##note:x[<<Ps 82:3–4; Jer 22:3; Mic 6:8>>] judgment, ##relieve##note:tn[or, righten] the oppressed, judge the fatherless, plead for the widow.
@ Come now, and let us reason together, saith the LORD: ##though your##note:x[<<Ps 51:7; Isa 44:22; Rev 7:14>>] sins be as scarlet, they shall be as white as snow; though they be red like crimson, they shall be as wool.
@ ##If ye be willing and obedient, ye shall eat the good of the land:##note:x[<<Isa 3:10; Joel 2:26; Heb 5:9>>]
@ But if ye refuse and rebel, ye shall be devoured with the sword: ##for the mouth##note:x[<<Num 23:19; Isa 40:5; 58:14>>] of the LORD hath spoken __it__.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Corruption of Zion

@ ¶ How is the faithful city become an harlot! it was full of judgment; righteousness lodged in it; but now murderers.
@ Thy silver is become dross, thy wine mixed with water:
@ Thy princes __are__ rebellious, and companions of thieves: ##every##note:x[<<Exod 23:8; Mic 7:3>>] one loveth gifts, and followeth after rewards: ##they judge##note:x[<<Zech 7:10>>] not the fatherless, neither doth the cause of the widow come unto them.
@ Therefore saith the Lord, the LORD of hosts, ##the mighty##note:x[<<Isa 49:26; 60:16>>] One of Israel, ##Ah##note:x[<<Deut 28:63>>], I will ease me of mine adversaries, and avenge me of mine enemies:

@ ¶ And I will turn my hand upon thee, and ##purely##note:tn[Heb. according to pureness] ##purge##note:x[<<Isa 1:22; 4:4; Mal 3:3>>] away thy dross, and take away all thy tin:
@ ##And I will##note:x[<<Jer 33:7>>] restore thy judges as at the first, and thy counsellors as at the beginning: afterward ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Isa 62:1>>] be called, The city of righteousness, the faithful city.
@ Zion shall be redeemed with judgment, and her converts with righteousness.note:tn[__her…__: or, they that return of her]

@ ¶ And note:x[<<Ps 1:6; 2 Thess 1:8–9>>]the ##destruction##note:tn[Heb. breaking] of the transgressors and of the sinners __shall be__ together, and they that forsake the LORD shall be consumed.
@ For they shall be ashamed of ##the oaks##note:x[<<Isa 57:5>>] which ye have desired, and ye shall be confounded for ##the gardens##note:x[<<Isa 65:3; 66:17>>] that ye have chosen.
@ For ye shall be as an oak whose leaf fadeth, and as a garden that hath no water.
@ And the strong shall be as tow, and the maker of it as a spark, ##and they##note:x[<<Isa 66:24>>] shall both burn together, and none shall quench __them__.note:tn[__maker…__: or, and his work]

=== @2

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Mountain of the House of the LORD

[.parallel]
(<<Micah 4:1–5>>)

@ The word that Isaiah the son of Amoz ##saw##note:x[<<Isa 1:1; 13:1>>] concerning Judah and Jerusalem.
@ ##And it shall##note:x[<<Mic 4:1–3>>] come to pass in the last days, __that__ ##the mountain##note:x[<<Zech 8:3>>] of the LORD’s house shall be ##established##note:tn[or, prepared] in the top of the mountains, and shall be exalted above the hills; ##and all##note:x[<<Isa 27:13>>] nations shall flow unto it.
@ And many people shall go and say, ##Come ye##note:x[<<Jer 50:4–5; Zech 8:20–23>>], and let us go up to the mountain of the LORD, to the house of the God of Jacob; and he will teach us of his ways, and we will walk in his paths: ##for out##note:x[<<Luke 24:47>>] of Zion shall go forth the law, and the word of the LORD from Jerusalem.
@ And he shall judge among the nations, and shall rebuke many people: ##and they##note:x[<<Isa 11:6–9; Hos 2:18; Mic 4:3>>] shall beat their swords into plowshares, and their spears into ##pruninghooks##note:tn[or, scythes]: nation shall not lift up sword against nation, neither shall they learn war any more.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Day of Reckoning

@ O house of Jacob, ##come ye##note:x[<<Isa 60:19; Luke 1:79; Eph 5:8>>], and let us walk in the light of the LORD.

@ ¶ ##Therefore##note:x[<<Rom 11:20>>] thou hast forsaken thy people the house of Jacob, because they be replenished from the east, ##and __are__##note:x[<<Deut 18:10–14>>] soothsayers like the Philistines, and they please themselves in the children of strangers.note:tn[__from…__: or, more than the]note:tn[__please…__: or, abound with the]
@ Their ##land##note:x[<<Deut 17:16–17>>] also is full of silver and gold, neither __is there any__ end of their treasures; ##their land is##note:x[<<Isa 30:16; 31:1>>] also full of horses, neither __is there any__ end of their chariots:
@ Their land also is full of idols; they ##worship##note:x[<<Ps 115:4–8; Rev 9:20>>] the work of their own hands, that which their own fingers have made:
@ And ##the mean##note:x[<<Isa 5:15>>] man boweth down, and the great man humbleth himself: therefore forgive them not.

@ ¶ ##Enter##note:x[<<Isa 2:19–21; Hos 10:8; Rev 6:15–16>>] into the rock, and hide thee in the dust, for fear of the LORD, and for the glory of his majesty.
@ The ##lofty##note:x[<<Ps 18:27; Isa 2:17; 5:15–16>>] looks of man shall be humbled, and the haughtiness of men shall be bowed down, and the LORD alone shall be exalted in that day.
@ For the day of the LORD of hosts __shall be__ ##upon##note:x[<<Isa 23:9>>] every __one that is__ proud and lofty, and upon every __one that is__ lifted up; and he shall be brought low:
@ And upon all the cedars of Lebanon, __that are__ high and lifted up, and upon all the oaks of Bashan,
@ ##And upon all the high mountains, and upon all the hills __that are__ lifted up,##note:x[<<Isa 30:25; 40:4>>]
@ And upon every high tower, and upon every fenced wall,
@ And upon all ##the ships##note:x[<<1 Kgs 10:22>>] of Tarshish, and upon all pleasant pictures.note:tn[__pleasant…__: Heb. pictures of desire]
@ And ##the loftiness##note:x[<<Isa 2:11; 13:11; Jer 48:29–30>>] of man shall be bowed down, and the haughtiness of men shall be made low: and the LORD alone shall be exalted in that day.
@ And the idols he shall utterly abolish.note:tn[__he…__: or, shall utterly pass away]
@ ##And they##note:x[<<Isa 2:10; Luke 23:30>>] shall go into the holes of the rocks, and into the caves of the earth, ##for fear##note:x[<<Isa 2:10>>] of the LORD, and for the glory of his majesty, when he ariseth to shake terribly the earth.note:tn[__of the earth__: Heb. of the dust]
@ In that day a man shall ##cast##note:x[<<Isa 30:22; 31:7>>] his idols of silver, and his idols of gold, which they made __each one__ for himself to worship, to the moles and to the bats;note:tn[__his idols of silver…__: Heb. the idols of his silver, etc]note:tn[__each…__: or, for him]
@ To ##go##note:x[<<Isa 2:19>>] into the clefts of the rocks, and into the tops of the ragged rocks, for fear of the LORD, and for the glory of his majesty, when he ariseth to shake terribly the earth.
@ ##Cease##note:x[<<Ps 146:3; Jer 17:5>>] ye from man, whose breath __is__ in his nostrils: ##for wherein##note:x[<<Ps 144:3–4>>] is he to be accounted of?

=== @3

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Jerusalem and Judah

@ For, behold, ##the Lord##note:x[<<Isa 1:24>>], the LORD of hosts, doth take away from Jerusalem and from Judah ##the stay##note:x[<<Lev 26:26; Ezek 4:16–17>>] and the staff, the whole stay of bread, and the whole stay of water,
@ The mighty man, and the man of war, the judge, and the prophet, and the prudent, and the ancient,
@ The captain of fifty, and the honourable man, and the counsellor, and the cunning artificer, and the eloquent orator.note:tn[__the honourable…__: Heb. a man eminent in countenance]note:tn[__eloquent…__: or, skilful of speech]
@ And I will give ##children##note:x[<<Eccl 10:16>>] __to be__ their princes, and babes shall rule over them.
@ And the people shall be oppressed, every one by another, and every one by his neighbour: the child shall behave himself proudly against the ancient, and the base against the honourable.
@ When ##a man##note:x[<<Isa 4:1>>] shall take hold of his brother of the house of his father, __saying__, Thou hast clothing, be thou our ruler, and __let__ this ruin __be__ under thy hand:
@ In that day shall he ##swear##note:tn[Heb. lift up the hand], saying, I will not be an note:x[<<Hos 5:13>>]##healer##note:tn[Heb. binder up]; for in my house __is__ neither bread nor clothing: make me not a ruler of the people.
@ For Jerusalem is ruined, and Judah is fallen: ##because##note:x[<<Ezek 9:9>>] their tongue and their doings __are__ against the LORD, ##to provoke##note:x[<<1 Cor 10:22>>] the eyes of his glory.

@ ¶ The shew of their countenance doth witness against them; ##and they declare##note:x[<<Gen 13:13>>] their sin as Sodom, they hide __it__ not. Woe unto their soul! for they have rewarded evil unto themselves.
@ Say ye to the righteous, that __it shall be__ well __with him:__ for ##they shall eat##note:x[<<Ps 128:1–2; Gal 6:7–8; Heb 6:10>>] the fruit of their doings.
@ ##Woe##note:x[<<Eccl 8:13>>] unto the wicked! __it shall be__ ill __with him:__ ##for the reward##note:x[<<Jas 2:13>>] of his hands shall be ##given him##note:x[<<Ps 120:3–4>>].note:tn[__given…__: Heb. done to him]

@ ¶ __As for__ my people, ##children##note:x[<<Isa 3:4; Nah 3:13>>] __are__ their oppressors, and women rule over them. O my people, they which lead thee cause __thee__ to err, and ##destroy##note:tn[Heb. swallow up] the way of thy paths.note:tn[__lead…__: or, call thee blessed]
@ The LORD ##standeth up##note:x[<<Mic 6:2>>] to plead, and standeth to judge the people.
@ The LORD will ##enter##note:x[<<Job 22:4>>] into judgment with the ancients of his people, and the princes thereof: for ye have ##eaten##note:tn[or, burnt] up the vineyard; the spoil of the poor __is__ in your houses.
@ What mean ye __that__ ##ye beat##note:x[<<Mic 3:2–3>>] my people to pieces, and grind the faces of the poor? saith the Lord GOD of hosts.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Warning to the Daughters of Zion

@ ¶ Moreover the LORD saith, Because the daughters of Zion ##are haughty##note:x[<<Prov 30:13; Isa 32:9–11; Ezek 16:49–50>>], and walk with stretched forth necks and wanton eyes, walking and ##mincing##note:tn[or, tripping nicely] __as__ they go, and making a tinkling with their feet&#58;note:tn[__wanton…__: Heb. deceiving with their eyes]
@ Therefore the Lord will smite with a scab the crown of the head of the daughters of Zion, and the LORD will note:x[<<Jer 13:22>>]##discover##note:tn[Heb. make naked] their secret parts.
@ In that day the Lord will take away the bravery of __their__ tinkling ornaments __about their feet__, and __their__ ##cauls##note:tn[or, networks], and __their__ ##round tires##note:x[<<Judg 8:21>>] like the moon,
@ The ##chains##note:tn[or, sweet balls], and the bracelets, and the ##mufflers##note:tn[or, spangled ornaments],
@ The bonnets, and the ornaments of the legs, and the headbands, and the ##tablets##note:tn[Heb. houses of the soul], and the earrings,
@ The rings, and ##nose jewels##note:x[<<Gen 24:47>>],
@ The changeable suits of apparel, and the mantles, and the wimples, and the crisping pins,
@ The glasses, and the fine linen, and the hoods, and the vails.
@ And it shall come to pass, __that__ instead of sweet smell there shall be stink; and instead of a girdle a rent; and instead of well set hair ##baldness##note:x[<<Isa 22:12>>]; and instead of a stomacher ##a girding##note:x[<<Lam 2:10; Ezek 27:31>>] of sackcloth; __and__ burning instead of beauty.
@ Thy men shall fall by the sword, and thy ##mighty##note:tn[Heb. might] in the war.
@ And ##her gates##note:x[<<Jer 14:2; Lam 1:4>>] shall lament and mourn; and she __being__ ##desolate##note:tn[or, emptied: Heb. cleansed] ##shall sit##note:x[<<Lam 2:10>>] upon the ground.

=== @4

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Remnant in Zion

@ ##And in##note:x[<<Isa 2:17; Luke 21:22>>] that day seven women shall take hold of one man, saying, We will eat our own bread, and wear our own apparel: only ##let us be called by thy name##note:x[<<Gen 30:23>>], to take away our reproach.note:tn[__let…__: Heb. let thy name be called upon us]note:tn[__to take…__: or, take thou away]

@ In that day shall ##the branch##note:x[<<Jer 23:5; Zech 3:8; 6:12>>] of the LORD be beautiful and glorious, and the fruit of the earth __shall be__ excellent and comely for them that are escaped of Israel.note:tn[__beautiful…__: Heb. beauty and glory]note:tn[__them…__: Heb. the escaping]
@ And it shall come to pass, __that he that is__ left in Zion, and __he that__ remaineth in Jerusalem, ##shall be##note:x[<<Isa 52:1; 60:21>>] called holy, __even__ every one that is ##written##note:x[<<Luke 10:20>>] among the living in Jerusalem&#58;note:tn[__among…__: or, to life]
@ When the Lord shall have ##washed away##note:x[<<Mal 3:2–3>>] the filth of the daughters of Zion, and shall have purged the blood of Jerusalem from the midst thereof ##by the spirit##note:x[<<Ezek 22:18–22; Mal 3:2–3>>] of judgment, and by the spirit of burning.
@ And the LORD will create upon every dwelling place of mount Zion, and upon her assemblies, ##a cloud##note:x[<<Exod 13:21–22; 40:34–38; Neh 9:12>>] and smoke by day, and the shining of a flaming fire by night: for ##upon all##note:tn[or, above all] the glory __shall be__ a ##defence##note:tn[Heb. covering].
@ And there shall be a ##tabernacle##note:x[<<Ps 27:5; Isa 25:4>>] for a shadow in the daytime from the heat, and for a place of refuge, and ##for a covert##note:x[<<Isa 32:2>>] from storm and from rain.

=== @5

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Song of the Vineyard

[.parallel]
(<<Luke 13:6–9>>)

@ Now will I sing to my wellbeloved a song of my beloved ##touching##note:x[<<Mark 12:1; Luke 20:9; John 15:1>>] his vineyard. My wellbeloved hath a vineyard in a very fruitful hill&#58;note:tn[__a very…__: Heb. the horn of the son of oil]
@ And he ##fenced##note:tn[or, made a wall about] it, and gathered out the stones thereof, and ##planted##note:x[<<Jer 2:21>>] it with the choicest vine, and built a tower in the midst of it, and also ##made##note:tn[Heb. hewed] a winepress therein: and ##he looked##note:x[<<Isa 1:2–4>>] that it should bring forth grapes, and it brought forth ##wild grapes##note:x[<<Deut 32:32–33>>].
@ And now, O inhabitants of Jerusalem, and men of Judah, judge, I pray you, betwixt me and my vineyard.
@ ##What could have been done more to my vineyard, that I have not done in it? wherefore, when I looked that it should bring forth grapes, brought it forth wild grapes?##note:x[<<Matt 23:37; Acts 7:51–60>>]
@ And now go to; I will tell you what I will do to my vineyard: I will take away the hedge thereof, and it shall be eaten up; __and__ break down the wall thereof, and it shall be ##trodden down##note:x[<<Isa 28:18; Lam 1:15; Luke 21:24>>]:note:tn[__trodden…__: Heb. for a treading]
@ And ##I will lay##note:x[<<Jer 25:11>>] it waste: ##it shall##note:x[<<Isa 7:23–25>>] not be pruned, nor digged; but there shall come up briers and thorns: I will also command the clouds that they rain no rain upon it.
@ For ##the vineyard##note:x[<<Ps 80:8–11; Jer 12:10>>] of the LORD of hosts __is__ the house of Israel, and the men of Judah his pleasant plant: and ##he looked##note:x[<<Matt 3:8–10>>] for judgment, but behold ##oppression##note:tn[Heb. a scab]; for righteousness, but behold a cry.note:tn[__his…__: Heb. plant of his pleasures]

[discrete.pericope]
==== Woes to the Wicked

@ ¶ Woe unto ##them##note:x[<<Jer 22:13–17; Mic 2:2; Hab 2:9–12>>] that join house to house, __that__ lay field to field, till __there be__ no place, that ##they##note:tn[Heb. ye] may be placed alone in the midst of the earth!
@ In mine ears __said__ the LORD of hosts, Of a truth many houses shall be ##desolate##note:x[<<Matt 23:38>>], __even__ great and fair, without inhabitant.note:tn[__In…__: or, This is in mine ears, saith the LORD, etc]note:tn[__Of a…__: Heb. If not, etc]
@ Yea, ten acres of vineyard shall yield one bath, and the seed of an homer shall yield an ephah.

@ ¶ Woe unto them that ##rise##note:x[<<Prov 23:29–30; Rom 13:13>>] up early in the morning, __that__ they may follow strong drink; that continue until night, __till__ wine note:x[<<Prov 20:1>>]##inflame##note:tn[of, pursue] them!
@ And ##the harp##note:x[<<Job 21:11–14; Amos 6:4–6>>], and the viol, the tabret, and pipe, and wine, are in their feasts: but ##they regard##note:x[<<Ps 28:5>>] not the work of the LORD, neither consider the operation of his hands.

@ ¶ Therefore ##my people##note:x[<<Isa 1:7>>] are gone into captivity, ##because##note:x[<<Isa 1:3; Hos 4:6>>] __they have__ no knowledge: and their honourable men __are__ famished, and their multitude dried up with thirst.note:tn[__honourable…__: Heb. glory are men of famine]
@ Therefore ##hell##note:x[<<Hab 2:5>>] hath enlarged herself, and opened her mouth without measure: and their glory, and their multitude, and their pomp, and ##he that rejoiceth##note:x[<<Ps 55:15; Luke 21:34>>], shall descend into it.
@ And ##the mean##note:x[<<Isa 2:9, 11>>] man shall be brought down, and the mighty man shall be humbled, and the eyes of the lofty shall be humbled:
@ But ##the LORD##note:x[<<1 Chr 29:11; Ps 46:10; Isa 12:4>>] of hosts shall be exalted in judgment, and God that is holy shall be sanctified in righteousness.note:tn[__God…__: or, the holy God: Heb. the God the holy]
@ Then ##shall the lambs##note:x[<<Isa 7:25; Zeph 2:6, 14>>] feed after their manner, and the waste places of the fat ones shall strangers eat.

@ Woe unto them that ##draw##note:x[<<Jer 23:14>>] iniquity with cords of vanity, and sin as it were with a cart rope:
@ That say, ##Let him##note:x[<<Jer 17:15; Ezek 12:22>>] make speed, __and__ hasten his work, that we may see __it:__ and let the counsel of the Holy One of Israel draw nigh and come, that we may know __it__!

@ ¶ Woe unto ##them##note:x[<<Prov 17:15; Luke 16:15; 2 Tim 3:1–5>>] that call evil good, and good evil; that put darkness for light, and light for darkness; that put bitter for sweet, and sweet for bitter!note:tn[__call…__: Heb. say concerning evil, It is good, etc]
@ Woe unto __them that are__ ##wise##note:x[<<Prov 3:7; Rom 12:16; 1 Cor 3:18–20>>] in their own eyes, and prudent in their own sight!note:tn[__in their own sight__: Heb. before their face]
@ Woe unto __them that are__ ##mighty##note:x[<<Isa 5:11; 28:7; Hab 2:15>>] to drink wine, and men of strength to mingle strong drink:
@ Which ##justify##note:x[<<Prov 17:15>>] the wicked for reward, and ##take##note:x[<<Ps 94:21; Isa 10:2>>] away the righteousness of the righteous from him!
@ Therefore as the ##fire##note:tn[Heb. tongue of fire] ##devoureth##note:x[<<Joel 2:5>>] the stubble, and ##the flame##note:x[<<Luke 3:17>>] consumeth the chaff, __so__ ##their root##note:x[<<Job 18:16>>] shall be as rottenness, and their blossom shall go up as dust: because they have cast away the law of the LORD of hosts, and despised the word of the Holy One of Israel.
@ Therefore is the anger of the LORD kindled against his people, and he hath stretched forth his hand against them, and hath smitten them: and the hills did tremble, and their carcases __were__ note:x[<<2 Kgs 9:37>>]##torn##note:tn[or, as dung] in the midst of the streets. ##For all##note:x[<<Isa 9:21; 10:4>>] this his anger is not turned away, but his hand __is__ stretched out still.

@ ¶ And he will lift up an ensign to the nations from far, and will ##hiss##note:x[<<Isa 7:18; Zech 10:8>>] unto them from the ##end##note:x[<<Deut 28:49>>] of the earth: and, behold, they shall come with speed swiftly:
@ None ##shall be##note:x[<<Joel 2:7–8>>] weary nor stumble among them; none shall slumber nor sleep; ##neither##note:x[<<Job 12:18>>] shall the girdle of their loins be loosed, nor the latchet of their shoes be broken:
@ Whose ##arrows##note:x[<<Ps 45:5>>] __are__ sharp, and all their bows bent, their horses’ hoofs shall be counted like flint, and their wheels like a whirlwind:
@ Their ##roaring##note:x[<<Zech 11:3>>] __shall be__ like a lion, they shall roar like young lions: yea, they shall roar, and ##lay hold##note:x[<<Isa 42:22; 49:24–25>>] of the prey, and shall carry __it__ away safe, and none shall deliver __it__.
@ And in that day they shall roar against them like the roaring of the sea: and ##if __one__ look##note:x[<<Isa 8:22; Jer 4:23–28; Joel 2:10>>] unto the land, behold darkness __and__ ##sorrow##note:tn[or, distress], and the light is darkened in the heavens thereof.note:tn[__and the light…__: or, when it is light, it shall be dark in the destructions thereof]

=== @6

[discrete.pericope]
==== Isaiah’s Commission

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 13:10–17; Mark 4:10–12; Acts 28:16–31>>)

@ In the year that king Uzziah died ##I saw also##note:x[<<John 12:41>>] the Lord ##sitting##note:x[<<Dan 7:9>>] upon a throne, high and lifted up, and his train ##filled##note:x[<<Rev 15:8>>] the temple.note:tn[__his…__: or, the skirts thereof]
@ Above it stood the ##seraphims##note:x[<<Ps 104:4>>]: each one had six ##wings##note:x[<<Rev 4:8>>]; with twain he covered his face, and with twain he covered ##his feet##note:x[<<Ezek 1:11>>], and with twain he did fly.
@ And one cried unto another, and said, ##Holy##note:x[<<Rev 4:8–9>>], holy, holy, __is__ the LORD of hosts: ##the whole earth##note:x[<<Num 14:21; Ps 72:19>>] __is__ full of his glory.note:tn[__one…__: Heb. this cried to this]note:tn[__the whole…__: Heb. his glory is the fulness of the whole earth]
@ And the posts of the ##door##note:tn[Heb. thresholds] moved at the voice of him that cried, and ##the house##note:x[<<Exod 40:34; 1 Kgs 8:10–12; Rev 15:8>>] was filled with smoke.

@ ¶ Then ##said I##note:x[<<Luke 5:8–9>>], Woe __is__ me! for I am ##undone##note:tn[Heb. cut off]; because I __am__ ##a man##note:x[<<Jer 1:6>>] of unclean lips, and ##I dwell##note:x[<<Jas 3:6–10>>] in the midst of a people of unclean lips: for mine eyes have seen the King, the LORD of hosts.
@ Then flew one of the seraphims unto me, ##having##note:x[<<Rev 8:3–5>>] a live coal in his hand, ##__which__##note:x[<<Heb 9:22–26; Rev 8:3–5>>] he had taken with the tongs from off the altar&#58;note:tn[__having…__: Heb. and in his hand a live coal]
@ And ##he laid __it__ upon##note:x[<<Jer 1:9>>] my mouth, and said, Lo, this hath touched thy lips; and ##thine iniquity##note:x[<<Isa 43:25; 1 John 1:7>>] is taken away, and thy sin purged.note:tn[__laid…__: Heb. caused it to touch]
@ Also I heard the voice of the Lord, saying, ##Whom##note:x[<<Exod 4:10–13; Acts 26:16–17>>] shall I send, and who will go for us? ##Then##note:x[<<Eph 3:8>>] said I, Here __am__ I; send me.note:tn[__Here…__: Heb. behold me]

@ ¶ And he said, Go, and tell this people, ##Hear ye##note:x[<<Matt 13:14–15; Mark 4:12; Luke 8:10>>] indeed, but understand not; and see ye indeed, but perceive not.note:tn[__indeed, but understand__: or, without ceasing, etc: Heb. in hearing, etc]
@ Make the heart of this people fat, and make their ears heavy, and shut their eyes; ##lest##note:x[<<Jer 5:21; Acts 28:27>>] they see with their eyes, and hear with their ears, and understand with their heart, and ##convert##note:x[<<Matt 13:15>>], and be healed.
@ Then said I, Lord, how long? And he answered, Until the cities be wasted without inhabitant, and the houses without man, and the land be utterly desolate,note:tn[__utterly…__: Heb. desolate with desolation]
@ And the LORD have removed men far away, and __there be__ ##a great##note:x[<<Jer 4:29>>] forsaking in the midst of the land.

@ ¶ ##But yet##note:x[<<Rom 11:5–6>>] in it __shall be__ a tenth, and __it__ shall return, and shall be eaten: as a teil tree, and as an oak, whose note:x[<<Job 14:7–9>>]##substance##note:tn[or, stock, or, stem] __is__ in them, when they cast __their leaves:__ ##__so__ the holy##note:x[<<Rom 11:5>>] seed __shall be__ the substance thereof.note:tn[__and it…__: or, when it is returned, and hath been broused]

=== @7

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Message to Ahaz

@ And it came to pass in ##the days##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:1>>] of Ahaz the son of Jotham, the son of Uzziah, king of Judah, __that__ ##Rezin##note:x[<<2 Kgs 15:37>>] the king of Syria, and Pekah the son of Remaliah, king of Israel, went up toward Jerusalem to war against it, but could not prevail against it.
@ And it was told ##the house##note:x[<<Isa 7:13>>] of David, saying, Syria is confederate with Ephraim. ##And his heart##note:x[<<Isa 8:12>>] was moved, and the heart of his people, as the trees of the wood are moved with the wind.note:tn[__is confederate…__: Heb. resteth on]
@ Then said the LORD unto Isaiah, Go forth now to meet Ahaz, thou, and note:x[<<Rom 9:27>>]##Shear-jashub##note:tn[that is, The remnant shall return] thy son, at ##the end##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:17; Isa 36:2>>] of the conduit of the upper pool in the ##highway##note:tn[or, causeway] of the fuller’s field;
@ note:x[<<Deut 20:3>>]And say unto him, ##Take heed##note:x[<<Lam 3:26>>], and be quiet; ##fear not##note:x[<<Isa 35:4>>], neither be fainthearted for the two tails of these smoking firebrands, for the fierce anger of Rezin with Syria, and of the son of Remaliah.note:tn[__neither…__: Heb. let not thy heart be tender]
@ Because ##Syria##note:x[<<Ps 83:3–4>>], Ephraim, and the son of Remaliah, have taken evil counsel against thee, saying,
@ Let us go up against Judah, and ##vex##note:tn[or, waken] it, and let us make a breach therein for us, and set a king in the midst of it, __even__ the son of Tabeal:
@ ##Thus saith the Lord GOD, It shall not stand, neither shall it come to pass.##note:x[<<Isa 8:10; 46:10–11; Lam 3:37>>]
@ For the head of Syria __is__ Damascus, and the head of Damascus __is__ Rezin; and ##within##note:x[<<Isa 17:1–3>>] threescore and five years shall Ephraim be broken, that it be not a people.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. from a people]
@ And the head of Ephraim __is__ Samaria, and the head of Samaria __is__ Remaliah’s son. ##If ye##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:20; Rom 11:20; Heb 11:6>>] will not believe, surely ye shall not be established.note:tn[__If…__: or, Do ye not believe? it is because ye are not stable]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Sign of Immanuel

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 1:18–25>>)

@ ¶ Moreover the LORD spake again unto Ahaz, saying,note:tn[__Moreover…__: Heb. And the LORD added to speak]
@ Ask thee ##a sign##note:x[<<Isa 37:30; 38:7–8, 22>>] of the LORD thy God; ask it either in the depth, or in the height above.note:tn[__ask it…__: or, make thy petition deep]
@ But Ahaz said, I will not ask, neither will I tempt the LORD.
@ And he said, Hear ye now, O house of David; __Is it__ a small thing for you to weary men, but ##will ye##note:x[<<Isa 43:24; Mal 2:17>>] weary my God also?
@ Therefore the Lord himself shall give you a sign; ##Behold##note:x[<<Matt 1:23>>], a virgin shall conceive, and bear a son, and shall call his name ##Immanuel##note:x[<<Isa 9:6; John 1:1–2>>].note:tn[__shall call__: or, thou, O virgin, shalt call]
@ ##Butter##note:x[<<Isa 7:22>>] and honey shall he eat, that he may ##know##note:x[<<Amos 5:15>>] to refuse the evil, and choose the good.
@ For ##before##note:x[<<Deut 1:39>>] the child shall know to refuse the evil, and choose the good, ##the land##note:x[<<Isa 8:4>>] that thou abhorrest shall be forsaken of both her kings.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment to Come

[.parallel]
(<<Micah 1:1–7>>)

@ ¶ The LORD shall bring upon thee, and upon thy people, and upon thy father’s house, days that have not come, from the day that Ephraim departed from Judah; __even__ the king of Assyria.
@ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ the LORD shall ##hiss##note:x[<<Isa 5:26>>] for the fly that __is__ in the uttermost part of the rivers of Egypt, and for the bee that __is__ in the land of Assyria.
@ And they shall come, and shall rest all of them in the desolate valleys, and ##in the holes##note:x[<<Isa 2:19; Jer 16:16>>] of the rocks, and upon all thorns, and upon all ##bushes##note:tn[or, commendable trees].
@ In the same day shall the Lord shave with a razor that is hired, __namely__, by them beyond the river, by the king of Assyria, the head, and the hair of the feet: and it shall also consume the beard.
@ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ a man shall nourish a young cow, and two sheep;
@ And it shall come to pass, for the abundance of milk __that__ they shall give he shall eat butter: for butter and honey shall every one eat that is left in the land.note:tn[__the land__: Heb. the midst of the land]
@ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ every place shall be, where there were a thousand vines at a thousand silverlings, it shall __even__ ##be for briers##note:x[<<Isa 5:6>>] and thorns.
@ With arrows and with bows shall __men__ come thither; because all the land shall become briers and thorns.
@ And __on__ all hills that shall be digged with the mattock, there shall not come thither the fear of briers and thorns: but it shall be for the sending forth of oxen, and for the treading of lesser cattle.

=== @8

[discrete.pericope]
==== Assyrian Invasion Prophesied

@ Moreover the LORD said unto me, Take thee a great roll, and ##write##note:x[<<Job 19:23–24; Isa 30:8; Hab 2:2–3>>] in it with a man’s pen concerning ##Maher-shalal-hash-baz##note:tn[Heb. In making speed to the spoil he hasteneth the prey, or, Make speed, etc].
@ And I took unto me faithful witnesses to record, ##Uria##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:10–11>>]h the priest, and Zechariah the son of Jeberechiah.
@ And I ##went##note:tn[Heb. approached] unto the prophetess; and she conceived, and bare a son. Then said the LORD to me, Call his name Maher-shalal-hash-baz.
@ For ##before##note:x[<<Rom 9:11>>] the child shall have knowledge to cry, My father, and my mother, the riches of Damascus and the spoil of Samaria shall be taken away before the king of Assyria.note:tn[__the riches…__: or, he that is before the king of Assyria shall take away the riches, etc]

@ ¶ The LORD spake also unto me again, saying,
@ Forasmuch as this people refuseth ##the waters of Shiloah##note:x[<<John 9:7>>] that go softly, and rejoice in Rezin and Remaliah’s son;
@ Now therefore, behold, ##the Lord bringeth##note:x[<<Isa 17:12–13>>] up upon them the waters of the river, strong and many, __even__ the king of Assyria, and all his glory: and he shall come up over all his channels, and go over all his banks:
@ And he shall pass through Judah; he shall overflow and go over, he shall ##reach##note:x[<<Isa 30:28>>] __even__ to the neck; and the stretching out of his wings shall fill the breadth of thy land, ##O Immanuel##note:x[<<Isa 7:14; Matt 1:23>>].note:tn[__stretching…__: Heb. fulness of the breadth of thy land shall be the stretchings out of his wings]

@ ¶ Associate yourselves, O ye ##people, note:x[<<Ps 37:14–15; Prov 11:21; Isa 14:5–6>>]and##note:tn[or, people, yet] ye shall be broken in pieces; and give ear, all ye of far countries: gird yourselves, and ye shall be broken in pieces; gird yourselves, and ye shall be broken in pieces.
@ Take ##counsel##note:x[<<Job 5:12; Lam 3:37>>] together, and it shall come to nought; speak the word, and it shall not stand: ##for God##note:x[<<Rom 8:31>>] __is__ with us.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Call to Fear God

[.parallel]
(<<Ecclesiastes 8:10–13>>)

@ ¶ For the LORD spake thus to me ##with a strong hand##note:x[<<Ezek 3:14>>], and instructed me that I should not walk in the way of this people, saying,note:tn[__with…__: Heb. in strength of hand]
@ Say ye ##not, A confederacy##note:x[<<Isa 51:12–13>>], to all __them to__ whom this people shall say, A confederacy; neither ##fear ye##note:x[<<Luke 21:9; 1 Pet 3:14–15>>] their fear, nor be afraid.

@ And he shall be for a sanctuary; but for ##a stone##note:x[<<Isa 28:16; Rom 9:32–33; 1 Pet 2:8>>] of stumbling and for a rock of offence to both the houses of Israel, for a gin and for a snare to the inhabitants of Jerusalem.

@ ##Bind up##note:x[<<Dan 12:4>>] ##the testimony##note:x[<<Heb 3:5>>], seal the law among my disciples.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Lam 3:25–26; Hab 2:3; Heb 10:36–39>>] wait upon the LORD, that hideth his face from the house of Jacob, and I will look for him.
@ Behold, ##I and the##note:x[<<Heb 2:13–14>>] children whom the LORD hath given me __are__ ##for signs##note:x[<<Luke 2:34>>] and for wonders in Israel from the LORD of hosts, ##which##note:x[<<Zech 8:3>>] dwelleth in mount Zion.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Darkness and Light

@ ¶ And when they shall say unto you, ##Seek##note:x[<<Lev 20:6; 1 Chr 10:13; 2 Chr 33:6>>] unto them that have familiar spirits, and unto wizards that peep, and that mutter: should not a people seek unto their God? for the living to the dead?

=== @9

[discrete.pericope]
==== Unto Us a Child Is Born

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 4:12–17; Mark 1:14–15; Luke 4:14–15>>)

@ Nevertheless ##the dimness##note:x[<<Isa 8:22>>] __shall__ not __be__ such as __was__ in her vexation, ##when##note:x[<<2 Kgs 15:29>>] at the first he lightly afflicted the land of Zebulun and the land of Naphtali, and afterward did more grievously afflict __her__ ##__by__ the way##note:x[<<Matt 4:15>>] of the sea, beyond Jordan, in Galilee of the nations.note:tn[__of the nations__: or, populous]

@ Thou ##hast multiplied##note:x[<<Isa 26:15>>] the nation, __and__ ##not##note:tn[or, to him] increased the joy: ##they joy##note:x[<<Isa 35:10; 65:18>>] before thee according to the joy in harvest, __and__ as __men__ rejoice when they divide the spoil.
@ ##For thou hast broken##note:x[<<Isa 14:25; Jer 30:8>>] the yoke of his burden, and the staff of his shoulder, the rod of his oppressor, ##as in the day##note:x[<<Isa 10:26>>] of Midian.note:tn[__For…__: or, When thou brakest]
@ For every battle of the warrior __is__ with confused noise, and garments rolled in blood; but __this__ shall be with ##burning##note:x[<<Ps 46:9; Isa 30:33; 2 Thess 1:8>>] __and__ ##fuel##note:tn[Heb. meat] of fire.note:tn[__For…__: or, When the whole battle of the warrior was, etc]note:tn[__but…__: or, and it was, etc]
@ ##For unto##note:x[<<Luke 2:11>>] us a child is born, unto us a son is given: and ##the government##note:x[<<Jer 23:5–6; Matt 28:18>>] shall be upon his shoulder: and his name shall be called Wonderful, Counsellor, The mighty God, The everlasting Father, The Prince of Peace.
@ Of ##the increase##note:x[<<Dan 2:44; 7:14; Luke 1:32–33>>] of __his__ government and peace __there shall be__ no end, upon the throne of David, and upon his kingdom, to order it, and to establish it with judgment and with justice from henceforth even for ever. The zeal of the LORD of hosts will perform this.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment against Israel’s Pride

@ ¶ The Lord sent a word into Jacob, and it hath lighted upon Israel.
@ And all the people shall know, __even__ Ephraim and the inhabitant of Samaria, that say ##in the pride##note:x[<<Isa 46:12>>] and stoutness of heart,
@ The ##bricks##note:x[<<Mal 1:4>>] are fallen down, but we will build with hewn stones: the sycomores are cut down, but we will change __them into__ cedars.
@ Therefore the LORD shall set up the adversaries of Rezin against him, and ##join##note:tn[Heb. mingle] his enemies together;
@ The ##Syrians##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:6; 2 Chr 28:18>>] before, and the Philistines behind; and they shall devour Israel with ##open##note:tn[Heb. whole] mouth. ##For all##note:x[<<Isa 5:25>>] this his anger is not turned away, but his hand __is__ stretched out still.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment against Israel’s Hypocrisy

@ ¶ For ##the people##note:x[<<Jer 5:3; Hos 7:10>>] turneth not unto him that smiteth them, ##neither##note:x[<<Isa 31:1>>] do they seek the LORD of hosts.
@ Therefore the LORD ##will cut##note:x[<<Isa 19:15>>] off from Israel head and tail, branch and rush, ##in one day##note:x[<<Rev 18:8>>].
@ The ancient and honourable, he __is__ the head; and ##the prophet##note:x[<<Isa 29:10; Matt 24:24>>] that teacheth lies, he __is__ the tail.
@ For ##the leaders##note:x[<<Isa 3:12; Matt 15:14>>] of this people cause __them__ to err; and __they that are__ ##led of##note:tn[or, called blessed of] them __are__ ##destroyed##note:tn[Heb. swallowed up].note:tn[__the leaders__: or, they that call them blessed]
@ Therefore the Lord shall ##have no joy##note:x[<<Jer 18:21>>] in their young men, neither shall have mercy on their fatherless and widows: ##for every##note:x[<<Isa 10:6>>] one __is__ an hypocrite and an evildoer, and every mouth speaketh ##folly##note:tn[or, villany]. ##For all##note:x[<<Isa 5:25>>] this his anger is not turned away, but his hand __is__ stretched out still.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment against Israel’s Unrepentance

@ ¶ For ##wickedness##note:x[<<Mal 4:1>>] burneth as the fire: it shall devour the briers and thorns, and shall kindle in the thickets of the forest, and they shall mount up __like__ the lifting up of smoke.
@ Through the wrath of the LORD of hosts is the land darkened, and the people shall be as the ##fuel##note:tn[Heb. meat] of the fire: ##no man##note:x[<<Mic 7:2>>] shall spare his brother.
@ ##And he##note:x[<<Isa 49:26>>] shall ##snatch##note:tn[Heb cut] on the right hand, and be hungry; and he shall eat on the left hand, and they shall not be satisfied: they shall eat every man the flesh of his own arm:
@ Manasseh, Ephraim; and Ephraim, Manasseh: __and__ they together __shall be__ against Judah. ##For all this##note:x[<<Isa 5:25>>] his anger is not turned away, but his hand __is__ stretched out still.

=== @10

[discrete.pericope]
==== Woe to Tyrants

@ ##Woe##note:x[<<Matt 23:23; Luke 11:52>>] unto ##them##note:x[<<Mic 3:1–4>>] that decree unrighteous decrees, and that write grievousness __which__ they have prescribed;note:tn[__that write…__: or, to the writers that write grievousness]
@ To ##turn aside##note:x[<<Mal 3:5>>] the needy from judgment, and to take away the right from the poor of my people, ##that widows##note:x[<<Isa 3:14>>] may be their prey, and __that__ they may rob the fatherless!
@ ##And what##note:x[<<Job 31:14>>] will ye do in ##the day##note:x[<<Hos 9:7; Luke 19:44>>] of visitation, and in the desolation __which__ shall come from far? to whom will ye flee for help? and where will ye leave your glory?
@ Without me they shall bow down under the prisoners, and they shall fall under the slain. ##For all this##note:x[<<Isa 5:25>>] his anger is not turned away, but his hand __is__ stretched out still.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Assyria

@ ¶ O Assyrian, ##the rod##note:x[<<Isa 10:15; Jer 51:20–24>>] of mine anger, ##and##note:tn[or, though] the staff in their hand is mine indignation.note:tn[__O…__: or, Woe to the Assyrian: Heb. Asshur]
@ I will send him ##against##note:x[<<Isa 9:17>>] an hypocritical nation, and against the people of my wrath will I give him a charge, to take the spoil, and to take the prey, and to tread them down like the mire of the streets.note:tn[__tread…__: Heb. lay them a treading]
@ Howbeit ##he meaneth##note:x[<<Gen 50:20; Mic 4:11–12; Acts 2:23>>] not so, neither doth his heart think so; but __it is__ in his heart to destroy and cut off nations not a few.
@ ##For he saith, __Are__ not my princes altogether kings?##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:24>>]
@ __Is__ not Calno as ##Carchemish##note:x[<<2 Chr 35:20>>]? __is__ not Hamath as Arpad? __is__ not ##Samaria##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:9>>] as Damascus?
@ As my hand hath found the kingdoms of the idols, and whose graven images did excel them of Jerusalem and of Samaria;
@ Shall I not, as I have done unto Samaria and her idols, so do to Jerusalem and her idols?
@ Wherefore it shall come to pass, __that__ when the Lord hath performed his whole work upon mount Zion and on Jerusalem, ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 50:18>>] ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] the fruit of the stout heart of the king of Assyria, and the glory of his high looks.note:tn[__stout…__: Heb. greatness of the heart]
@ ##For he saith##note:x[<<Deut 8:17; Dan 4:30>>], By the strength of my hand I have done __it__, and by my wisdom; for I am prudent: and I have removed the bounds of the people, and have robbed their treasures, and I have put down the inhabitants like a valiant __man:__note:tn[__a valiant…__: or, many people]
@ And my hand hath found as a nest the riches of the people: and as one gathereth eggs __that are__ left, have I gathered all the earth; and there was none that moved the wing, or opened the mouth, or peeped.
@ Shall ##the axe##note:x[<<Isa 10:5; Jer 51:20–23; Rom 9:20–21>>] boast itself against him that heweth therewith? __or__ shall the saw magnify itself against him that shaketh it? as if the rod should shake __itself__ against them that lift it up, __or__ as if the staff should lift up __itself, as if it were__ no wood.note:tn[__the rod…__: or, a rod should shake them that lift it up]note:tn[__itself, as if…__: or, that which is not wood]
@ Therefore shall the Lord, ##the Lord of hosts##note:x[<<2 Chr 32:21>>], send among his fat ones leanness; and under his glory he shall kindle a burning like the burning of a fire.
@ And the light of Israel shall be for a fire, and his Holy One for a flame: and it shall burn and ##devour##note:x[<<Isa 27:4>>] his thorns and his briers in one day;
@ And shall consume the glory of his forest, and of his fruitful field, both soul and body: and they shall be as when a standardbearer fainteth.note:tn[__both…__: Heb. from the soul, and even to the flesh]
@ And the rest of the trees of his forest shall be ##few##note:tn[Heb. number], that a child may write them.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Remnant Shall Return

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ ##the remnant##note:x[<<Isa 37:31–32>>] of Israel, and such as are escaped of the house of Jacob, shall no more again stay upon him that smote them; but shall stay upon the LORD, the Holy One of Israel, in truth.

@ For the Lord GOD of hosts shall make a consumption, even determined, in the midst of all the land.

@ ¶ Therefore thus saith the Lord GOD of hosts, ##O my people##note:x[<<Heb 12:22–24>>] that dwellest in Zion, be not afraid of the Assyrian: he shall smite thee with a rod, and shall lift up his staff against thee, after the manner of Egypt.note:tn[__and shall…__: or, but he shall lift up his staff for]

@ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ ##his burden##note:x[<<Isa 9:4; 14:25>>] shall be taken away from off thy shoulder, and his yoke from off thy neck, and the yoke shall be destroyed ##because##note:x[<<Luke 4:18>>] of the anointing.note:tn[__be taken…__: Heb. remove]

@ Lift up thy voice, O daughter of ##Gallim##note:x[<<1 Sam 25:44>>]: cause it to be heard unto Laish, O poor ##Anathoth##note:x[<<Jer 1:1>>].note:tn[__Lift…__: Heb. Cry shrill with]
@ Madmenah is removed; the inhabitants of Gebim gather themselves to flee.
@ As yet shall he remain at ##Nob##note:x[<<1 Sam 21:1>>] that day: he shall shake his hand __against__ the mount of the daughter of Zion, the hill of Jerusalem.
@ Behold, the Lord, the LORD of hosts, shall lop the bough with terror: and ##the high ones##note:x[<<Amos 2:9>>] of stature __shall be__ hewn down, and the haughty shall be humbled.
@ And he shall cut down the thickets of the forest with iron, and Lebanon shall fall by a mighty one.note:tn[__by…__: or, mightily]

=== @11

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Root of Jesse

@ ##And there shall##note:x[<<Zech 6:12; Rev 22:16>>] come forth a rod out of the stem of Jesse, and ##a Branch##note:x[<<Jer 23:5>>] shall grow out of his roots:
@ And ##the spirit##note:x[<<Isa 61:1>>] of the LORD shall rest upon him, ##the spirit of wisdom##note:x[<<John 16:13; Eph 1:17–18>>] and understanding, the spirit of counsel and might, the spirit of knowledge and of the fear of the LORD;
@ And ##shall make him##note:x[<<Luke 2:52>>] of quick understanding in the fear of the LORD: ##and he shall not##note:x[<<1 Sam 16:7; John 7:24>>] judge after the sight of his eyes, neither reprove after the hearing of his ears&#58;note:tn[__quick…__: Heb. scent or, smell]
@ But with righteousness shall he judge the poor, and note:x[<<Prov 31:8–9>>]##reprove##note:tn[or, argue] with equity for the meek of the earth: ##and he shall##note:x[<<2 Thess 2:8; Rev 19:15>>] smite the earth with the rod of his mouth, and with the breath of his lips shall he slay the wicked.
@ And ##righteousness##note:x[<<Isa 59:17; Eph 6:14>>] shall be the girdle of his loins, ##and faithfulness##note:x[<<1 John 1:9>>] the girdle of his reins.
@ ##The wolf also shall dwell with the lamb, and the leopard shall lie down with the kid; and the calf and the young lion and the fatling together; and a little child shall lead them.##note:x[<<Isa 65:25; Ezek 34:25; Hos 2:18>>]
@ And the cow and the bear shall feed; their young ones shall lie down together: and the lion shall eat straw like the ox.
@ And the sucking child shall play on the hole of the asp, and the weaned child shall put his hand on the ##cockatrice’##note:tn[or, adder’s] den.
@ They shall ##not hurt##note:x[<<Mic 4:2–4>>] nor destroy in all my holy mountain: ##for the##note:x[<<Ps 98:2–3; Hab 2:14>>] earth shall be full of the knowledge of the LORD, as the waters cover the sea.

@ ¶ And ##in that day##note:x[<<Isa 11:1>>] there shall be a root of Jesse, which shall stand for an ensign of the people; ##to it shall##note:x[<<Luke 2:32; Rom 15:9–12>>] the Gentiles seek: and his rest shall be ##glorious##note:tn[Heb. glory].
@ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ the Lord shall ##set his hand##note:x[<<Hos 3:4–5; Zech 10:8–12>>] again the second time to recover the remnant of his people, which shall be left, ##from Assyria##note:x[<<Mic 7:12>>], and from Egypt, and from Pathros, and from Cush, and from Elam, and from Shinar, and from Hamath, and from the islands of the sea.
@ And he shall set up an ensign for the nations, and ##shall assemble##note:x[<<Ps 147:2; Isa 43:6; 56:8>>] the outcasts of Israel, and gather together the dispersed of Judah from the four ##corners##note:tn[Heb. wings] of the earth.
@ ##The envy##note:x[<<Jer 3:18; Ezek 37:16–24; Hos 1:11>>] also of Ephraim shall depart, and the adversaries of Judah shall be cut off: Ephraim shall not envy Judah, and Judah shall not vex Ephraim.
@ But they shall fly upon the shoulders of the Philistines toward the west; they shall ##spoil##note:x[<<Jer 49:28>>] note:x[<<Isa 25:10; Joel 3:19>>]##them of##note:tn[Heb. the children of] the east together: they shall lay their hand upon Edom and Moab; and the children of Ammon shall obey them.note:tn[__they shall lay…__: Heb. Edom and Moab shall be the laying on of their hand]note:tn[__shall obey…__: Heb. their obedience]
@ And the LORD shall ##utterly##note:x[<<Isa 50:2; Zech 10:11>>] destroy the tongue of the Egyptian sea; and with his mighty wind shall he shake his hand over the river, and ##shall smite##note:x[<<Rev 16:12>>] it in the seven streams, and make __men__ go over ##dryshod##note:tn[Heb. in shoes].
@ ##And there shall##note:x[<<Isa 11:11; 19:23; 35:8–10>>] be an highway for the remnant of his people, which shall be left, from Assyria; like as it was to Israel in the day that he came up out of the land of Egypt.

=== @12

[discrete.pericope]
==== Joyful Thanksgiving

@ And in that day thou shalt say, ##O LORD##note:x[<<Isa 25:1>>], I will praise thee: ##though##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; Isa 54:8>>] thou wast angry with me, thine anger is turned away, and thou comfortedst me.
@ Behold, ##God##note:x[<<Ps 27:1>>] __is__ my salvation; I will trust, and not be afraid: for ##the LORD##note:x[<<Exod 15:2; Ps 118:14>>] JEHOVAH __is__ my strength and __my__ song; he also is become my salvation.
@ Therefore ##with joy##note:x[<<Jer 2:13; Rev 22:1, 17>>] shall ye draw water out of the wells of salvation.

@ And in that day shall ye say, Praise the LORD, ##call upon note:x[<<1 Chr 29:11>>]his name##note:x[<<Ps 105:1>>], ##declare##note:x[<<Ps 145:4–6>>] his doings among the people, make mention that his name is exalted.note:tn[__call…__: or, proclaim]
@ ##Sing##note:x[<<Exod 15:1; Ps 98:1>>] unto the LORD; for he hath done excellent things: ##this __is__ known##note:x[<<Hab 2:14>>] in all the earth.
@ ##Cry out##note:x[<<Zeph 3:14>>] and shout, thou ##inhabitant##note:tn[Heb. inhabitress] of Zion: for ##great##note:x[<<Zech 2:5, 10–11>>] __is__ the Holy One of Israel in the midst of thee.

=== @13

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Burden against Babylon

@ The burden ##of Babylon##note:x[<<Isa 47:1–15; Jer 50:1–46>>], ##which Isaiah##note:x[<<Isa 1:1>>] the son of Amoz did see.
@ ##Lift ye up##note:x[<<Isa 5:26; Jer 50:2>>] a banner upon the high mountain, exalt the voice unto them, shake the hand, that they may ##go into##note:x[<<Jer 51:58>>] the gates of the nobles.
@ I have commanded my sanctified ones, I have also called my ##mighty ones##note:x[<<Joel 3:11>>] for mine anger, __even__ them that rejoice in my highness.
@ The ##noise##note:x[<<Joel 3:14>>] of a multitude in the mountains, like as of a great people; a tumultuous noise of the kingdoms of nations gathered together: the LORD of hosts mustereth the host of the battle.note:tn[__like…__: Heb. the likeness of]
@ They come from a far country, from the end of heaven, __even__ the LORD, and the weapons of his indignation, to destroy the whole land.

@ ¶ ##Howl ye##note:x[<<Ezek 30:2>>]; ##for the day##note:x[<<Amos 5:18>>] of the LORD __is__ at hand; it shall come ##as a##note:x[<<Joel 1:15>>] destruction from the Almighty.
@ Therefore ##shall all##note:x[<<Ezek 21:7>>] hands be faint, and every man’s heart shall melt&#58;note:tn[__be faint__: or, fall down]
@ And they shall be afraid: ##pangs##note:x[<<Isa 26:17; 1 Thess 5:3>>] and sorrows shall take hold of them; they shall be in pain as a woman that travaileth: they shall ##be amazed one at another##note:x[<<Nah 2:10>>]; their faces __shall be as__ ##flames##note:tn[Heb. faces of the flames].note:tn[__be amazed__: Heb. wonder]note:tn[__one…__: Heb. every man at his neighbour]
@ Behold, the day of the LORD cometh, ##cruel##note:x[<<Isa 13:15–18; Nah 1:6; Mal 4:1>>] both with wrath and fierce anger, to lay the land desolate: and he shall destroy the sinners thereof out of it.
@ ##For the stars of heaven and the constellations thereof shall not give their light: the sun shall be darkened in his going forth, and the moon shall not cause her light to shine.##note:x[<<Ezek 32:7–8; Joel 2:31; Matt 24:29>>]
@ And I will punish the world for __their__ evil, and the wicked for their iniquity; ##and I will cause##note:x[<<Isa 5:15>>] the arrogancy of the proud to cease, and will lay low the haughtiness of the terrible.
@ ##I will make a man more precious than fine gold; even a man than the golden wedge of Ophir.##note:x[<<Isa 4:1; 24:6>>]
@ Therefore ##I will##note:x[<<Joel 3:16; Matt 24:29; Heb 12:26–27>>] shake the heavens, and the earth shall remove out of her place, in the wrath of the LORD of hosts, and in the day of his fierce anger.
@ And it shall be ##as the##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:17, 36>>] chased roe, and as a sheep that no man taketh up: ##they shall##note:x[<<Jer 50:16>>] every man turn to his own people, and flee every one into his own land.
@ Every one that is found shall be thrust through; and every one that is joined __unto them__ shall fall by the sword.
@ Their ##children##note:x[<<Hos 10:14; Nah 3:10>>] also shall be dashed to pieces before their eyes; their houses shall be spoiled, and their wives ravished.
@ Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 41:25; Jer 51:11; Dan 5:28–31>>] stir up the Medes against them, which shall not regard silver; and __as for__ gold, they shall not delight in it.
@ __Their__ bows also ##shall dash##note:x[<<2 Kgs 8:12>>] the young men to pieces; and they shall have no pity on the fruit of the womb; ##their eye##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:17>>] shall not spare children.

@ ¶ note:x[<<Gen 19:24>>]And ##Babylon##note:x[<<Jer 51:41; Dan 4:30>>], the glory of kingdoms, the beauty of the Chaldees’ excellency, shall be as when God overthrew Sodom and Gomorrah.note:tn[__as…__: Heb. as the overthrowing]
@ ##It shall never be inhabited, neither shall it be dwelt in from generation to generation: neither shall the Arabian pitch tent there; neither shall the shepherds make their fold there.##note:x[<<Isa 14:23; Jer 51:43>>]
@ But wild beasts of the desert shall lie there; and their houses shall be full of doleful creatures; and ##owls##note:tn[or, ostriches: Heb. daughters of the owl] shall dwell there, and satyrs shall dance there.note:tn[__wild…__: Heb. Ziim]note:tn[__doleful…__: Heb. Ochim]
@ And the wild beasts of the islands shall cry in their desolate houses, and dragons in __their__ pleasant palaces: and her time __is__ near to come, and her days shall not be prolonged.note:tn[__the wild…__: Heb. Iim]note:tn[__desolate…__: or, palaces]

=== @14

[discrete.pericope]
==== Restoration for Israel

@ For ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 102:13; Isa 54:7–8>>] will have mercy on Jacob, and will yet ##choose##note:x[<<Zech 1:17>>] Israel, and set them in their own land: and the strangers shall be joined with them, and they shall cleave to the house of Jacob.
@ And the people shall take them, and bring them to their place: ##and the house##note:x[<<Isa 61:5>>] of Israel shall possess them in the land of the LORD for servants and handmaids: and they shall take them captives, whose captives they were; and ##they shall rule over##note:x[<<Isa 60:14; Jer 30:16>>] their oppressors.note:tn[__whose…__: Heb. that had taken them captives]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Fall of the King of Babylon

@ ##And it shall come to pass in the day that the LORD shall give thee rest from thy sorrow, and from thy fear, and from the hard bondage wherein thou wast made to serve,##note:x[<<Ezra 9:8–9; Isa 32:18; Jer 30:10>>]

@ ¶ That thou shalt take up this ##proverb##note:tn[or, taunting speech] against the king of Babylon, and say, How hath the oppressor ceased! the golden city ceased!note:tn[__golden…__: or, exactress of gold]
@ ##The LORD hath broken the staff of the wicked, __and__ the sceptre of the rulers.##note:x[<<Ps 125:3>>]
@ He ##who smote##note:x[<<Dan 7:19–21>>] the people in wrath with a continual stroke, he that ruled the nations in anger, is persecuted, __and__ none hindereth. note:tn[__a continual…__: Heb. a stroke without removing]
@ The whole earth is at rest, __and__ is quiet: ##they##note:x[<<Ps 96:11–13; 126:1–3; Isa 49:13>>] break forth into singing.
@ ##Yea, the fir trees rejoice at thee, __and__ the cedars of Lebanon, __saying__, Since thou art laid down, no feller is come up against us.##note:x[<<Ezek 31:16>>]
@ ##Hell##note:tn[or, The grave] from beneath ##is moved##note:x[<<Ezek 32:21–32>>] for thee to meet __thee__ at thy coming: it stirreth up the dead for thee, __even__ all the chief ones of the earth; it hath raised up from their thrones all the kings of the nations.note:tn[__chief…__: Heb. leaders, or, great goats]
@ All they shall speak and say unto thee, Art thou also become weak as we? art thou become like unto us?
@ Thy pomp is brought down to the grave, __and__ the noise of thy viols: the worm is spread under thee, and the worms cover thee.
@ ##How art thou fallen##note:x[<<Ezek 28:13–17; Luke 10:18; Rev 12:7–10>>] from heaven, O Lucifer, son of the morning! __how__ art thou cut down to the ground, which didst weaken the nations!note:tn[__O Lucifer__: or, O day star]
@ For ##thou##note:x[<<Ezek 28:2>>] hast said in thine heart, ##I will ascend##note:x[<<Ezek 28:12–16; Dan 8:10–12>>] into heaven, I will exalt my throne above the stars of God: I will sit also upon the mount of the congregation, in the sides of the north:
@ I will ascend above the heights of the clouds; ##I will be##note:x[<<Gen 3:5; Isa 47:8; 2 Thess 2:4>>] like the most High.
@ Yet ##thou##note:x[<<Matt 11:23; Acts 12:22–23>>] shalt be brought down to hell, ##to the##note:x[<<Ezek 32:23>>] sides of the pit.
@ They that see thee ##shall narrowly##note:x[<<Ps 58:10–11>>] look upon thee, __and__ consider thee, __saying,__ ##__Is__ this##note:x[<<Jer 50:23; 51:20–23>>] the man that made the earth to tremble, that did shake kingdoms;
@ __That__ made the world as a wilderness, and destroyed the cities thereof; __that__ opened not the house of his prisoners?note:tn[__opened…__: or, did not let his prisoners loose homeward?]
@ All the kings of the nations, __even__ all of them, lie in glory, every one in his own house.
@ But thou art cast out of thy grave like an abominable branch, __and as__ the raiment of those that are slain, thrust through with a sword, that ##go##note:x[<<Jer 41:7, 9>>] down to the stones of the pit; as a carcase trodden under feet.
@ Thou shalt not be joined with them in burial, because thou hast destroyed thy land, __and__ slain thy people: ##the seed##note:x[<<Job 18:19; Ps 21:10; 37:28>>] of evildoers shall never be renowned.
@ Prepare slaughter for his children for the iniquity of their fathers; that they do not rise, nor possess the land, nor fill the face of the world with cities.
@ For ##I will##note:x[<<Jer 50:29–35>>] rise up against them, saith the LORD of hosts, and cut off from Babylon ##the name##note:x[<<Prov 10:7>>], and ##remnant##note:x[<<1 Kgs 14:10>>], and son, and nephew, saith the LORD.
@ I will also ##make##note:x[<<Isa 13:21–22; 34:11–15; Zeph 2:14>>] it a possession for the bittern, and pools of water: and I will sweep it with the besom of destruction, saith the LORD of hosts.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Purpose against Assyria

@ ¶ The LORD of hosts hath sworn, saying, ##Surely##note:x[<<Job 23:13; Prov 19:21; Isa 46:10–11>>] as I have thought, so shall it come to pass; and as I have purposed, __so__ shall it stand:
@ That ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 9:4; 37:36–38>>] break the Assyrian in my land, and upon my mountains tread him under foot: ##then##note:x[<<Nah 1:13>>] shall his yoke depart from off them, and his burden depart from off their shoulders.
@ This __is__ the purpose that is purposed upon the whole earth: and this __is__ the hand that is stretched out upon all the nations.
@ For ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 43:13>>] of hosts hath purposed, and who shall disannul __it__? and ##his##note:x[<<2 Chr 20:6; Job 23:13>>] hand __is__ stretched out, and who shall turn it back?

[discrete.pericope]
==== Philistia Will Be Destroyed

@ ##In the year that king Ahaz died was this burden.##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:20>>]

@ ¶ Rejoice not thou, whole Palestina, ##because##note:x[<<2 Chr 26:6>>] the rod of him that smote thee is broken: for out of the serpent’s root shall come forth a note:x[<<Isa 11:8>>]##cockatrice##note:tn[or, adder], and his fruit __shall be__ ##a fiery##note:x[<<Isa 30:6>>] flying serpent.
@ And the firstborn of ##the poor##note:x[<<Isa 7:21–22>>] shall feed, and the needy shall lie down in safety: and I will kill thy root with famine, and he shall slay thy remnant.

@ What shall __one__ then answer the messengers of the nation? That ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 87:1, 5; 102:16>>] hath founded Zion, and the poor of his people shall trust in it.note:tn[__trust…__: or, betake themselves unto it]

=== @15

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Burden against Moab

[.parallel]
(<<Jeremiah 48:1–47>>)

@ The burden of ##Moab##note:x[<<Isa 11:14; Jer 48:1–47; Amos 2:1–3>>]. Because in the night Ar of Moab is laid waste, __and__ brought to silence; because in the night Kir of Moab is laid waste, __and__ brought to silence;note:tn[__brought…__: or, cut off]
@ He is gone up to Bajith, and to Dibon, the high places, to weep: Moab shall howl over ##Nebo##note:x[<<Jer 48:1>>], and over Medeba: on ##all##note:x[<<Lev 21:5; Jer 48:1>>] their heads __shall be__ baldness, __and__ every beard cut off.
@ In ##their streets##note:x[<<Jonah 3:6–8>>] they shall gird themselves with sackcloth: on the tops of their houses, and in their streets, every one shall howl, weeping abundantly.note:tn[__weeping…__: Heb. descending into weeping, or, coming down with weeping]
@ And ##Heshbon##note:x[<<Jer 48:34>>] shall cry, and Elealeh: their voice shall be heard __even__ unto Jahaz: therefore the armed soldiers of Moab shall cry out; his life shall be grievous unto him.
@ My heart shall cry out for Moab; his fugitives __shall flee__ unto Zoar, an heifer of three years old: for by ##the mounting##note:x[<<Jer 48:5>>] up of Luhith with weeping shall they go it up; for in the way of Horonaim they shall raise up a cry of note:x[<<Jer 4:20>>]##destruction##note:tn[Heb. breaking].note:tn[__his…__: or, to the borders thereof, even to Zoar, as an heifer]

@ For the waters of Nimrim shall be ##desolate##note:tn[Heb. desolations]: for the hay is withered away, the grass faileth, there is no green thing.
@ Therefore the abundance they have gotten, and that which they have laid up, shall they carry away to the brook of the willows.note:tn[__brook…__: or, valley of the Arabians]
@ For the cry is gone round about the borders of Moab; the howling thereof unto Eglaim, and the howling thereof unto Beer-elim.
@ For the waters of Dimon shall be full of blood: for I will bring ##more##note:tn[Heb. additions] upon Dimon, ##lions##note:x[<<2 Kgs 17:25>>] upon him that escapeth of Moab, and upon the remnant of the land.

=== @16

[discrete.pericope]
==== Moab’s Destruction

[.parallel]
(<<Zephaniah 2:8–11>>)

@ Send ye ##the lamb##note:x[<<2 Kgs 3:4>>] to the ruler of the land ##from##note:x[<<2 Kgs 14:7>>] ##Sela##note:tn[or, Petra: Heb. A rock] to the wilderness, unto ##the mount##note:x[<<Isa 10:32>>] of the daughter of Zion.
@ For it shall be, __that__, ##as##note:x[<<Prov 27:8>>] a wandering bird cast out of the nest, __so__ the daughters of Moab shall be at the fords of Arnon.note:tn[__cast…__: or, a nest forsaken]
@ ##Take##note:tn[Heb. Bring] counsel, execute judgment; make thy shadow as the night in the midst of the noonday; hide the outcasts; bewray not him that wandereth.
@ Let mine outcasts dwell with thee, Moab; be thou a covert to them from the face of the spoiler: ##for##note:x[<<Isa 14:4>>] the ##extortioner##note:tn[Heb. wringer] is at an end, the spoiler ceaseth, the oppressors are consumed out of the land.note:tn[__the oppressors__: Heb. the treaders down]
@ And in mercy shall the throne be ##established##note:tn[or, prepared]: and he shall sit upon it in truth ##in the##note:x[<<Isa 9:6–7; Dan 7:14; Mic 4:7>>] tabernacle of David, judging, and seeking judgment, and hasting righteousness.

@ ¶ We ##have##note:x[<<Jer 48:29–30; Amos 2:1; Obad 1:3–4>>] heard of the pride of Moab; __he is__ very proud: __even__ of his haughtiness, and his pride, and his wrath: __but__ his lies __shall__ not __be__ so.
@ Therefore shall Moab howl for Moab, every one shall howl: for the foundations of ##Kir-hareseth##note:x[<<2 Kgs 3:25>>] shall ye ##mourn##note:tn[or, mutter]; surely __they are__ stricken.
@ For the fields of Heshbon languish, __and__ the vine of Sibmah: the lords of the heathen have broken down the principal plants thereof, they are come __even__ unto Jazer, they wandered __through__ the wilderness: her branches are stretched out, they are gone over the sea.note:tn[__stretched…__: or, plucked up]

@ ¶ Therefore I will bewail with the weeping of Jazer the vine of Sibmah: I will water thee with my tears, O Heshbon, and Elealeh: for the shouting for thy summer fruits and for thy harvest is fallen.note:tn[__the shouting…__: or, the alarm is fallen upon, etc]
@ ##And gladness is taken away, and joy out of the plentiful field; and in the vineyards there shall be no singing, neither shall there be shouting: the treaders shall tread out no wine in __their__ presses; I have made __their vintage__ shouting to cease.##note:x[<<Jer 48:33>>]
@ Wherefore ##my##note:x[<<Isa 15:5; 63:15; Jer 48:36>>] bowels shall sound like an harp for Moab, and mine inward parts for Kir-haresh.

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass, ##when##note:x[<<Isa 15:2; Jer 48:35>>] it is seen that Moab is weary on the high place, that he shall come to his sanctuary to pray; but he shall not prevail.
@ This __is__ the word that the LORD hath spoken concerning Moab since that time.
@ But now the LORD hath spoken, saying, Within three years, as the years of an hireling, and the glory of Moab shall be contemned, with all that great multitude; and the remnant __shall be__ very small __and__ ##feeble##note:tn[or, not many].

=== @17

[discrete.pericope]
