[.parallel]
(<<Jeremiah 49:23–27>>)

@ The burden of ##Damascus##note:x[<<Amos 1:3–5; Zech 9:1>>]. Behold, Damascus is taken away from __being__ a city, and it shall be ##a ruinous##note:x[<<Isa 25:2>>] heap.
@ The cities of ##Aroer##note:x[<<Deut 2:36>>] __are__ forsaken: ##they shall##note:x[<<Ezek 25:5; Zeph 2:6>>] be for flocks, which shall lie down, and none shall make __them__ afraid.
@ The ##fortress##note:x[<<Isa 7:16; 8:4>>] also shall cease from Ephraim, and the kingdom from Damascus, and the remnant of Syria: ##they shall##note:x[<<Hos 9:11>>] be as the glory of the children of Israel, saith the LORD of hosts.
@ And in that day it shall come to pass, __that__ the glory of Jacob shall be made thin, and ##the fatness##note:x[<<Isa 10:16>>] of his flesh shall wax lean.
@ And it shall be ##as when##note:x[<<Jer 51:33; Joel 3:13; Matt 13:30>>] the harvestman gathereth the corn, and reapeth the ears with his arm; and it shall be as he that gathereth ears in the valley of Rephaim.

@ ¶ ##Yet gleaning grapes shall be left in it, as the shaking of an olive tree, two __or__ three berries in the top of the uppermost bough, four __or__ five in the outmost fruitful branches thereof, saith the LORD God of Israel.##note:x[<<Deut 4:27; Isa 24:13>>]
@ ##At that day shall a man look to his Maker, and his eyes shall have respect to the Holy One of Israel.##note:x[<<Hos 6:1; Mic 7:7>>]
@ And ##he shall##note:x[<<Isa 27:9; 30:22>>] not look to the altars, the work of his hands, neither shall respect __that__ which his fingers have made, either the groves, or the ##images##note:tn[or, sun images].

@ ¶ In that day shall his strong cities be as a forsaken bough, and an uppermost branch, which they left because of the children of Israel: and there shall be desolation.
@ Because ##thou hast##note:x[<<Ps 106:21; Jer 2:32>>] forgotten the God of thy salvation, and hast not been mindful of ##the rock##note:x[<<Isa 26:4>>] of thy strength, therefore shalt thou plant pleasant plants, and shalt set it with strange slips:
@ In the day shalt thou make thy plant to grow, and in the morning shalt thou make thy seed to flourish: __but__ ##the harvest##note:x[<<Job 4:8; Hos 8:7>>] __shall be__ a heap in the day of grief and of desperate sorrow.note:tn[__a heap…__: or, removed in the day of inheritance, and there shall be deadly sorrow]

@ ¶ Woe to the ##multitude##note:tn[or, noise] of many people, __which__ ##make a noise##note:x[<<Ps 18:4; Jer 6:23; Luke 21:25>>] like the noise of the seas; and to the rushing of nations, __that__ make a rushing like the rushing of ##mighty##note:tn[or, many] waters!
@ The nations shall rush like the rushing of many waters: ##but##note:x[<<Ps 9:5>>] __God__ shall rebuke them, and they shall flee far off, and ##shall be##note:x[<<Job 21:18; Isa 41:15–16>>] chased as the chaff of the mountains before the wind, and like a rolling thing before the whirlwind.note:tn[__a rolling…__: or, thistledown]
@ note:x[<<2 Kgs 19:35>>]And behold at eveningtide trouble; __and__ before the morning he __is__ not. This __is__ the portion of them that spoil us, and the lot of them that rob us.

=== @18

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Message to Cush

@ Woe to the land shadowing with wings, ##which##note:x[<<2 Kgs 19:9; Ezek 30:4–5; Zeph 3:10>>] __is__ beyond the rivers of Ethiopia:
@ That sendeth ambassadors by the sea, even in vessels of bulrushes upon the waters, __saying__, Go, ye swift messengers, to a nation scattered and peeled, ##to a people##note:x[<<Gen 10:8–9>>] terrible from their beginning hitherto; a nation meted out and trodden down, whose land the rivers have spoiled!note:tn[__scattered…__: or, outspread and polished]note:tn[__meted…__: or, that meteth out and treadeth down: Heb. of line, line, and treading under foot]note:tn[__have…__: or, despise]
@ All ye inhabitants of the world, and dwellers on the earth, ##see ye##note:x[<<Isa 5:26>>], when he lifteth up an ensign on the mountains; and when he bloweth a trumpet, hear ye.
@ For so the LORD said unto me, ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 26:21; Hos 5:15>>] take my rest, and I will ##consider in my dwelling place##note:x[<<Isa 18:7>>] like a clear heat upon herbs, __and__ like a cloud of dew in the heat of harvest.note:tn[__consider…__: or, regard my set dwelling]note:tn[__upon…__: or, after rain]
@ For afore the harvest, when the bud is perfect, and the sour grape is ripening in the flower, he shall both cut off the sprigs with pruning hooks, and take away __and__ cut down the branches.
@ ##They shall be left together unto the fowls of the mountains, and to the beasts of the earth: and the fowls shall summer upon them, and all the beasts of the earth shall winter upon them.##note:x[<<Jer 7:33>>]

@ ¶ In that time ##shall the##note:x[<<Zeph 3:10; Acts 8:27–28>>] present be brought unto the LORD of hosts of a people scattered and peeled, and from a people terrible from their beginning hitherto; a nation meted out and trodden under foot, whose land the rivers have spoiled, ##to the##note:x[<<Zech 14:16–17>>] place of the name of the LORD of hosts, the mount Zion.note:tn[__scattered…__: or, outspread and polished]

=== @19

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Burden against Egypt

@ The burden of ##Egypt##note:x[<<Joel 3:19>>]. Behold, the LORD ##rideth##note:x[<<Rev 1:7>>] upon a swift cloud, and shall come into Egypt: and ##the idols##note:x[<<Exod 12:12>>] of Egypt shall be moved at his presence, and the heart of Egypt shall melt in the midst of it.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Judg 7:22; 1 Sam 14:20; Ezek 38:21>>] ##set##note:tn[Heb. mingle] the Egyptians against the Egyptians: and they shall fight every one against his brother, and every one against his neighbour; city against city, __and__ kingdom against kingdom.
@ And the spirit of Egypt shall ##fail##note:tn[Heb. be emptied] in the midst thereof; and I will ##destroy##note:tn[Heb. swallow up] the counsel thereof: ##and they##note:x[<<1 Chr 10:13; Isa 8:19; Dan 2:2>>] shall seek to the idols, and to the charmers, and to them that have familiar spirits, and to the wizards.
@ And the Egyptians will I give over into the hand of ##a cruel lord##note:x[<<Isa 20:4; Jer 46:26; Ezek 29:19>>]; and a fierce king shall rule over them, saith the Lord, the LORD of hosts.note:tn[__give…__: or, shut up]
@ ##And the waters shall fail from the sea, and the river shall be wasted and dried up.##note:x[<<Jer 51:36; Ezek 30:12>>]
@ And they shall turn the rivers far away; ##__and__ the##note:x[<<Isa 37:25>>] brooks of defence shall be emptied and dried up: the reeds and flags shall wither.
@ The paper reeds by the brooks, by the mouth of the brooks, and every thing sown by the brooks, shall wither, be driven away, and be no __more__.note:tn[__and be…__: Heb. and shall not be]
@ ##The fishers also shall mourn, and all they that cast angle into the brooks shall lament, and they that spread nets upon the waters shall languish.##note:x[<<Ezek 47:10; Hab 1:15>>]
@ Moreover they that ##work##note:x[<<Prov 7:16; Ezek 27:7>>] in fine flax, and they that weave ##networks##note:tn[or, white works], shall be confounded.
@ And they shall be broken in the ##purposes##note:tn[Heb. foundations] thereof, all that make sluices __and__ ponds for fish.note:tn[__for fish__: Heb. of living things]

@ ¶ Surely the princes of ##Zoan##note:x[<<Num 13:22; Ps 78:43; Isa 30:4>>] __are__ fools, the counsel of the wise counsellors of Pharaoh is become brutish: how say ye unto Pharaoh, I __am__ the son of the wise, the son of ancient kings?
@ Where __are__ they? where __are__ thy wise __men__? and let them tell thee now, and let them know what the LORD of hosts hath purposed upon Egypt.
@ The princes of Zoan are become fools, the princes of ##Noph##note:x[<<Jer 2:16; 46:14; Ezek 30:13>>] are deceived; they have also seduced Egypt, __even they that are__ the stay of the tribes thereof.note:tn[__they that…__: or, governors: Heb. corners]
@ The LORD ##hath mingled##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:20–23; Ezek 14:7–9; 2 Thess 2:11>>] a perverse spirit in the midst thereof: and they have caused Egypt to err in every work thereof, as a drunken __man__ staggereth in his vomit.note:tn[__a perverse…__: Heb. a spirit of perversities]
@ Neither shall there be __any__ work for Egypt, which the head or tail, branch or rush, may do.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Blessing upon the Earth

@ In that day shall Egypt be ##like##note:x[<<Jer 30:5–7; 51:30; Nah 3:13>>] unto women: and it shall be afraid and fear because of the shaking of the hand of the LORD of hosts, which he shaketh over it.
@ And the land of Judah shall be a terror unto Egypt, every one that maketh mention thereof shall be afraid in himself, ##because##note:x[<<Isa 14:24>>] of the counsel of the LORD of hosts, which he hath determined against it.

@ ¶ In ##that day##note:x[<<Isa 19:21; Zech 2:11>>] shall five cities in the land of Egypt ##speak##note:x[<<Zeph 3:9>>] the language of Canaan, and swear to the LORD of hosts; one shall be called, The city of destruction.note:tn[__the language__: Heb. the lip]note:tn[__of destruction__: or, of Heres, or, of the sun]
@ ##In##note:x[<<Gen 28:18; Josh 22:10>>] that day shall there be an altar to the LORD in the midst of the land of Egypt, and a pillar at the border thereof to the LORD.
@ And it shall be for a sign and for a witness unto the LORD of hosts in the land of Egypt: for ##they shall##note:x[<<Ps 50:15>>] cry unto the LORD because of the oppressors, and ##he shall send##note:x[<<Titus 2:13>>] them a saviour, and a great one, and he shall deliver them.
@ And the LORD shall be known to Egypt, and the Egyptians shall know the LORD in that day, and shall do sacrifice and oblation; yea, they shall vow a vow unto the LORD, and perform __it__.
@ And the LORD shall smite Egypt: ##he shall smite##note:x[<<Deut 32:39; Heb 12:11>>] and heal __it:__ and ##they shall##note:x[<<Hos 14:1>>] return __even__ to the LORD, and he shall be intreated of them, and shall heal them.

@ ¶ ##In that day shall there be a highway out of Egypt to Assyria, and the Assyrian shall come into Egypt, and the Egyptian into Assyria, and the Egyptians shall serve with the Assyrians.##note:x[<<Isa 11:16>>]
@ In that day ##shall##note:x[<<Isa 65:8>>] Israel be the third with Egypt and with Assyria, __even__ ##a blessing##note:x[<<Gen 12:2; Isa 65:8>>] in the midst of the land:
@ Whom the LORD of hosts shall bless, saying, ##Blessed##note:x[<<Isa 29:23; Hos 2:23; Rom 9:24–25>>] __be__ Egypt my people, and Assyria the work of my hands, and Israel mine inheritance.

=== @20

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Sign against Egypt and Cush

@ In the year that ##Tartan##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:17>>] came unto Ashdod, (when Sargon the king of Assyria sent him,) and fought against Ashdod, and took it;
@ At the same time spake the LORD ##by##note:tn[Heb. by the hand of] Isaiah the son of Amoz, saying, Go and loose ##the sackcloth##note:x[<<Matt 3:4>>] from off thy loins, and put off thy shoe from thy foot. And he did so, walking ##naked##note:x[<<1 Sam 19:24; Mic 1:8>>] and barefoot.
@ And the LORD said, Like as my servant Isaiah hath walked naked and barefoot three years __for__ ##a sign##note:x[<<Isa 8:18>>] and wonder upon Egypt and upon Ethiopia;
@ So ##shall##note:x[<<Isa 19:4>>] the king of Assyria lead away the Egyptians prisoners, and the Ethiopians captives, young and old, naked and barefoot, even with __their__ buttocks uncovered, to the ##shame##note:tn[Heb. nakedness] of Egypt.note:tn[__the Egyptians…__: Heb. the captivity of Egypt]
@ And they shall be ##afraid##note:x[<<Isa 30:3, 5>>] and ashamed of Ethiopia their expectation, and of Egypt their glory.
@ And the inhabitant of this ##isle##note:tn[or, country] shall say in that day, Behold, such __is__ our expectation, whither we flee for help to be delivered from the king of Assyria: ##and how##note:x[<<Matt 23:33; 1 Thess 5:3; Heb 2:3>>] shall we escape?

=== @21

[discrete.pericope]
==== Babylon Is Fallen

[.parallel]
(<<Revelation 18:1–8>>)

@ ##The burden##note:x[<<Isa 13:1>>] of ##the desert##note:x[<<Isa 14:23; Jer 51:42>>] of the sea. As whirlwinds in the south pass through; __so__ it cometh from the desert, from a terrible land.
@ A note:x[<<Ps 60:3>>]##grievous##note:tn[Heb. hard] vision is declared unto me; ##the treacherous##note:x[<<Isa 33:1>>] dealer dealeth treacherously, and the spoiler spoileth. ##Go up##note:x[<<Jer 49:34>>], O Elam: besiege, O Media; all the sighing thereof have I made to cease.
@ Therefore are my loins filled with pain: ##pangs have##note:x[<<Ps 48:6; Isa 13:8; 26:17>>] taken hold upon me, as the pangs of a woman that travaileth: I was bowed down at the hearing __of it;__ I was dismayed at the seeing __of it__.
@ My heart panted, fearfulness affrighted me: the night of my pleasure hath he ##turned##note:tn[Heb. put] into fear unto me.note:tn[__heart…__: or, mind wandered]
@ Prepare the table, watch in the watchtower, eat, drink: ##arise##note:x[<<Jer 51:11>>], ye princes, __and__ anoint the shield.
@ For thus hath the Lord said unto me, ##Go##note:x[<<Ezek 3:17; 33:2–7; Hab 2:1–2>>], set a watchman, let him declare what he seeth.
@ ##And he saw##note:x[<<Isa 21:9>>] a chariot __with__ a couple of horsemen, a chariot of asses, __and__ a chariot of camels; and he hearkened diligently with much heed:
@ And he cried, A lion: My lord, I stand continually upon the watchtower in the daytime, and I am set in my ward whole nights&#58;note:tn[__he…__: or, cried as a lion]note:tn[__whole…__: or, every night]
@ And, behold, here cometh a chariot of men, __with__ a couple of horsemen. And he answered and said, ##Babylon##note:x[<<Jer 51:8; Rev 14:8; 18:2>>] is fallen, is fallen; and all the graven images of her gods he hath broken unto the ground.
@ O ##my threshing##note:x[<<Isa 41:15–16; Jer 51:33; Mic 4:13>>], and the ##corn##note:tn[Heb. son] of my floor: that which I have heard of the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, have I declared unto you.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Burden against Edom

[.parallel]
(<<Isaiah 34:5–17>>)

@ ¶ The burden of ##Dumah##note:x[<<Gen 25:14>>]. He calleth to ##me out##note:x[<<Amos 1:6; Obad 1:1–16>>] of Seir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night?
@ The watchman said, The morning cometh, and also the night: ##if##note:x[<<Acts 17:30–32>>] ye will enquire, enquire ye: return, come.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Burden against Arabia

@ ¶ The burden upon ##Arabia##note:x[<<Jer 25:23–24>>]. In the forest in Arabia shall ye lodge, O ye travelling companies of Dedanim.
@ The inhabitants of the land of ##Tema##note:x[<<Gen 25:15; Job 6:19>>] note:x[<<Rom 12:20>>]##brought##note:tn[or, bring ye] water to him that was thirsty, they prevented with their bread him that fled.
@ For they fled from the swords, from the drawn sword, and from the bent bow, and from the grievousness of war.note:tn[__from the swords__: or, for fear, etc: Heb. from the face, etc]
@ For thus hath the Lord said unto me, Within a year, ##according##note:x[<<Isa 16:14>>] to the years of an hireling, and all the glory of ##Kedar##note:x[<<Ps 120:5; Isa 60:7>>] shall fail:
@ And the residue of the number of ##archers##note:tn[Heb. bows], the mighty men of the children of Kedar, shall be diminished: ##for##note:x[<<Num 23:19; Zech 1:6>>] the LORD God of Israel hath spoken __it__.

=== @22

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Valley of Vision

@ The burden of ##the valley##note:x[<<Ps 125:2; Jer 21:13>>] of vision. What aileth thee now, that thou art wholly gone up to the housetops?
@ Thou ##that art##note:x[<<Isa 32:13>>] full of stirs, a tumultuous city, a joyous city: thy slain __men are__ not slain with the sword, nor dead in battle.
@ All thy rulers are fled together, they are bound by the archers: all that are found in thee are bound together, __which__ have fled from far.note:tn[__by…__: Heb. of the bow]
@ Therefore said I, ##Look##note:x[<<Jer 9:1>>] away from me; I will weep bitterly, ##labour##note:x[<<Matt 2:18>>] not to comfort me, because of the spoiling of the daughter of my people.note:tn[__weep…__: Heb. be bitter in weeping]
@ For __it is__ ##a day##note:x[<<Isa 37:3>>] of trouble, and of ##treading##note:x[<<Isa 10:6>>] down, and of ##perplexity##note:x[<<Mic 7:4>>] by the Lord GOD of hosts in the valley of vision, breaking down the walls, and of crying to the mountains.
@ And ##Elam##note:x[<<Isa 21:2>>] bare the quiver with chariots of men __and__ horsemen, and ##Kir##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:9>>] ##uncovered##note:tn[Heb. made naked] the shield.
@ And it shall come to pass, __that__ thy choicest valleys shall be full of chariots, and the horsemen shall set themselves in array ##at##note:tn[or, toward] the gate.note:tn[__thy…__: Heb. the choice of thy valleys]

@ ¶ And he discovered the covering of Judah, and thou didst look in that day to ##the armour##note:x[<<1 Kgs 7:2; 10:17>>] of the house of the forest.
@ Ye have seen also the breaches of the city of David, that they are many: and ye gathered together the waters of the lower pool.
@ And ye have numbered the houses of Jerusalem, and the houses have ye broken down to fortify the wall.
@ Ye made also a ditch between the two walls for the water of the old pool: but ##ye have##note:x[<<Jer 33:2–3; Mic 7:7>>] not looked unto the maker thereof, neither had respect unto him that fashioned it long ago.
@ And in that day did the Lord GOD of hosts ##call##note:x[<<Joel 1:13; 2:17>>] to weeping, and to mourning, and ##to baldness##note:x[<<Mic 1:16>>], and to girding with sackcloth:
@ And ##behold##note:x[<<Isa 56:12; Luke 17:26–29>>] joy and gladness, slaying oxen, and killing sheep, eating flesh, and drinking wine: ##let##note:x[<<Isa 56:12>>] us eat and drink; for to morrow we shall die.
@ And ##it was##note:x[<<Isa 5:9>>] revealed in mine ears by the LORD of hosts, ##Surely##note:x[<<1 Sam 3:14; Ezek 24:13>>] this iniquity shall not be purged from you till ye die, saith the Lord GOD of hosts.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Message for Shebna

@ ¶ Thus saith the Lord GOD of hosts, Go, get thee unto this treasurer, __even__ unto ##Shebna##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:18; Isa 36:3; 37:2>>], which __is__ over the house, __and say__,
@ What hast thou here? and whom hast thou here, that thou hast ##hewed##note:x[<<2 Sam 18:18; 2 Chr 16:14; Matt 27:60>>] thee out a sepulchre here, __as__ he that heweth him out a sepulchre on high, __and__ that graveth an habitation for himself in a rock?note:tn[__as…__: or, O he]
@ Behold, the LORD will carry thee away with a mighty captivity, and will surely cover thee.note:tn[__will carry…__: or, who covered thee with an excellent covering, and clothed thee gorgeously, (next verse) shall surely, etc]note:tn[__a mighty…__: Heb. the captivity of a man]
@ He will ##surely##note:x[<<Isa 17:13>>] violently turn and toss thee __like__ a ball into a ##large##note:tn[Heb. large of spaces] country: there shalt thou die, and there the chariots of thy glory __shall be__ the shame of thy lord’s house.
@ ##And I will drive thee from thy station, and from thy state shall he pull thee down.##note:x[<<Ezek 17:24>>]

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass in that day, that I will call my servant ##Eliakim##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:18; Isa 36:3; 37:2>>] the son of Hilkiah:
@ And I will clothe him with thy robe, and strengthen him with thy girdle, and I will commit thy government into his hand: and he shall be ##a father##note:x[<<Gen 45:8>>] to the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and to the house of Judah.
@ ##And the key##note:x[<<Matt 16:18–19>>] of the house of David will I lay upon his shoulder; ##so he##note:x[<<Job 12:14; Rev 3:7>>] shall open, and none shall shut; and he shall shut, and none shall open.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Ezra 9:8; Zech 10:4>>] fasten him __as__ a nail in a sure place; and he shall be for ##a glorious##note:x[<<Job 36:7>>] throne to his father’s house.
@ And they shall hang upon him all the glory of his father’s house, the offspring and the issue, all vessels of small quantity, from the vessels of cups, even to all the vessels of flagons.note:tn[__vessels of flagons__: or, instruments of viols]
@ In that day, saith the LORD of hosts, shall the nail that is fastened in the sure place be removed, and be cut down, and fall; and the burden that __was__ upon it shall be cut off: ##for the##note:x[<<Isa 46:11>>] LORD hath spoken __it__.

=== @23

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Burden against Tyre

[.parallel]
(<<Ezekiel 26:1–21>>)

@ The ##burden##note:x[<<Jer 25:22; Joel 3:4–8; Amos 1:9–10>>] of Tyre. Howl, ye ships of Tarshish; for it is laid waste, so that there is no house, no entering in: from the land of Chittim it is revealed to them.
@ Be ##still##note:tn[Heb. silent], ye inhabitants of the isle; thou whom the merchants of Zidon, that pass over the sea, have replenished.
@ And by great waters the seed of Sihor, the harvest of the river, __is__ her revenue; and she is a mart of nations.
@ Be thou ashamed, O Zidon: for the sea hath spoken, __even__ the strength of the sea, saying, I travail not, nor bring forth children, neither do I nourish up young men, __nor__ bring up virgins.
@ As at the report concerning Egypt, __so__ shall they be sorely pained at the report of Tyre.
@ Pass ye over to Tarshish; howl, ye inhabitants of the isle.
@ __Is__ this ##your##note:x[<<Isa 22:2>>] joyous __city__, whose antiquity __is__ of ancient days? her own feet shall carry her afar off to sojourn.note:tn[__afar…__: Heb. from afar off]
@ Who hath taken this counsel against Tyre, the crowning __city__, whose merchants __are__ princes, whose traffickers __are__ the honourable of the earth?
@ The LORD of hosts hath purposed it, ##to stain##note:x[<<Job 40:11–12; Isa 13:11; Jas 4:6>>] the pride of all glory, __and__ to bring into contempt all the honourable of the earth.note:tn[__to stain__: Heb. to pollute]
@ Pass through thy land as a river, O daughter of Tarshish: __there is__ no more ##strength##note:tn[Heb. girdle].
@ He stretched out his hand over the sea, he shook the kingdoms: the LORD hath given a commandment against the merchant __city__, to destroy the ##strong holds##note:x[<<Zech 9:3–4>>] thereof.note:tn[__against…__: or, concerning a merchantman]note:tn[__the merchant city__: Heb. Canaan]note:tn[__strong…__: or, strengths]
@ And he said, ##Thou shalt##note:x[<<Rev 18:22>>] no more rejoice, O thou oppressed virgin, daughter of Zidon: arise, pass over to Chittim; there also shalt thou have no rest.
@ Behold the land of the Chaldeans; this people was not, __till__ the Assyrian founded it for them that dwell in the wilderness: they set up the towers thereof, they raised up the palaces thereof; __and__ he brought it to ruin.
@ Howl, ye ships of Tarshish: for your strength is laid waste.

@ And it shall come to pass in that day, that ##Tyre shall##note:x[<<Jer 25:22>>] be forgotten seventy years, according to the days of one king: after the end of seventy years shall Tyre sing as an harlot.note:tn[__shall Tyre…__: Heb. it shall be unto Tyre as the song of an harlot]
@ Take an harp, go about the city, thou harlot that hast been forgotten; make sweet melody, sing many songs, that thou mayest be remembered.

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass after the end of seventy years, that the LORD will visit Tyre, ##and she shall##note:x[<<Ezek 27:6–36>>] turn to her hire, and ##shall commit##note:x[<<Nah 3:4>>] fornication with all the kingdoms of the world upon the face of the earth.
@ And ##her merchandise##note:x[<<Ps 72:10>>] and her hire shall be holiness to the LORD: it shall not be treasured nor laid up; for her merchandise shall be ##for them##note:x[<<Deut 26:12–14; Phil 4:17–18>>] that dwell before the LORD, to eat sufficiently, and for ##durable##note:tn[Heb. old] clothing.

=== @24

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Judgment on the Earth

@ Behold, the LORD ##maketh the##note:x[<<Isa 6:11–12; 42:15; Nah 2:10>>] earth empty, and maketh it waste, and turneth it upside down, and scattereth abroad the inhabitants thereof.note:tn[__turneth…__: Heb. perverteth the face thereof]
@ And it shall be, ##as with the people##note:x[<<Hos 4:9>>], so with the ##priest##note:tn[or, prince]; as with the servant, so with his master; as with the maid, so with her mistress; as with the buyer, so with the seller; as with the lender, so with the borrower; as with the taker of usury, so with the giver of usury to him.
@ The land ##shall##note:x[<<Isa 6:11; 24:1>>] be utterly emptied, and utterly spoiled: for the LORD hath spoken this word.
@ The earth ##mourneth##note:x[<<Hos 4:3>>] __and__ fadeth away, the world languisheth __and__ fadeth away, the haughty people of the earth do languish.note:tn[__the haughty…__: Heb. the height of the people]
@ The earth also is ##defiled##note:x[<<Rom 8:20–21>>] under the inhabitants thereof; because they have transgressed the laws, ##changed##note:x[<<Dan 7:25>>] the ordinance, ##broken##note:x[<<Heb 13:20>>] the everlasting covenant.
@ Therefore ##hath##note:x[<<Zech 5:3–4; Mal 4:1>>] the curse devoured the earth, and they that dwell therein are desolate: therefore the inhabitants of the earth are burned, ##and few##note:x[<<Matt 7:14>>] men left.
@ ##The new wine mourneth, the vine languisheth, all the merryhearted do sigh.##note:x[<<Isa 16:10>>]
@ ##The mirth of tabrets ceaseth, the noise of them that rejoice endeth, the joy of the harp ceaseth.##note:x[<<Jer 7:34; Ezek 26:13; Hos 2:11>>]
@ They shall not drink wine with a song; strong drink shall be bitter to them that drink it.
@ The city ##of confusion##note:x[<<Rev 18:2>>] is broken down: every house is shut up, that no man may come in.
@ __There is__ a crying for wine in the streets; all joy is darkened, the mirth of the land is gone.
@ In the city is left desolation, and the gate is smitten with destruction.

@ ¶ When thus it shall be in the midst of the land among the people, ##__there__##note:x[<<Isa 1:9>>] __shall be__ as the shaking of an olive tree, __and__ as the gleaning grapes when the vintage is done.
@ They shall lift up their voice, they shall sing for the majesty of the LORD, they shall cry aloud from the sea.
@ Wherefore glorify ye the LORD in the ##fires##note:tn[or, valleys], __even__ the name of the LORD God of Israel in the ##isles##note:x[<<Mal 1:11>>] of the sea.

@ ¶ From the uttermost part of the earth have we heard songs, __even__ glory to the righteous. But I said, ##My leanness##note:x[<<Isa 21:2; 33:1>>], my leanness, woe unto me! the treacherous dealers have dealt treacherously; yea, the treacherous dealers have dealt very treacherously.note:tn[__uttermost…__: Heb. wing]note:tn[__My leanness__: Heb. Leanness to me, or, My secret to me]
@ Fear, ##and the pit##note:x[<<Jer 48:43–44>>], and the snare, __are__ upon thee, O inhabitant of the earth.
@ And it shall come to pass, __that__ he who fleeth from the noise of the fear shall fall into the pit; and he that cometh up out of the midst of the pit shall be taken in the snare: ##for the##note:x[<<Gen 7:11>>] windows from on high are open, and ##the foundations##note:x[<<Ps 18:7>>] of the earth do shake.
@ ##The earth is utterly broken down, the earth is clean dissolved, the earth is moved exceedingly.##note:x[<<Isa 34:4–10>>]
@ The earth shall ##reel##note:x[<<Isa 19:14; 29:9>>] to and fro like a drunkard, and shall be removed like a cottage; and the transgression thereof shall be heavy upon it; ##and it##note:x[<<Dan 11:19>>] shall fall, and not rise again.
@ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 76:12; Isa 34:2–17; Zech 14:12–19>>] shall ##punish##note:tn[Heb. visit upon] the host of the high ones __that are__ on high, and the kings of the earth upon the earth.
@ And they shall be gathered together, ##__as__ prisoners are gathered##note:x[<<Zech 9:11>>] in the ##pit##note:tn[or, dungeon], and shall be shut up in the prison, and after many days shall they be ##visited##note:tn[or, found wanting].note:tn[__as prisoners…__: Heb. with the gathering of prisoners]
@ Then ##the moon##note:x[<<Isa 13:10; 60:19; Rev 21:23>>] shall be confounded, and the sun ashamed, when the LORD of hosts shall reign in mount Zion, and in Jerusalem, and before his ancients gloriously.note:tn[__before…__: or, there shall be glory before his ancients]

=== @25

[discrete.pericope]
==== Praise to the Victorious God

[.parallel]
(<<Jeremiah 51:15–19>>)

@ ##O LORD, thou __art__ my God; I will exalt thee, I will praise thy name; for thou hast done wonderful __things;__ note:x[<<Num 23:19>>]__thy__ counsels of old __are__ faithfulness __and__ truth.##note:x[<<Exod 15:2; Ps 118:28>>]
@ ##For##note:x[<<Isa 17:1>>] thou hast made of a city an heap; __of__ a defenced city a ruin: a ##palace##note:x[<<Isa 13:22>>] of strangers to be no city; it shall never be built.
@ Therefore shall the strong people glorify thee, the city of the terrible nations shall fear thee.
@ For ##thou hast##note:x[<<Zeph 3:12; Jas 2:5>>] been a strength to the poor, a strength to the needy in his distress, ##a refuge##note:x[<<Isa 32:2>>] from the storm, a shadow from the heat, when the blast of the terrible ones __is__ as a storm __against__ the wall.
@ Thou shalt bring down the noise of strangers, as the heat in a dry place; __even__ the heat with the shadow of a cloud: the branch of the terrible ones shall be brought low.

@ ¶ And in this mountain shall the LORD of hosts ##make##note:x[<<Ps 63:5; Jer 31:12–13>>] unto ##all people##note:x[<<Matt 8:11>>] a feast of fat things, a feast of wines on the lees, of fat things full of marrow, of wines on the lees well refined.
@ And ##he will##note:x[<<Isa 60:1–3; Matt 27:51; Eph 4:18>>] ##destroy##note:tn[Heb. swallow up] in this mountain the face of the covering ##cast##note:tn[Heb. covered] over all people, and the vail that is spread over all nations.
@ ##He##note:x[<<Rev 21:4>>] will swallow up death in victory; and the Lord ##GOD##note:x[<<Rev 7:17; 21:4>>] will wipe away tears from off all faces; and the rebuke of his people shall he take away from off all the earth: for the LORD hath spoken __it__.

@ ¶ And it shall be said in that day, ##Lo##note:x[<<Gen 49:18; Ps 27:14; Mic 7:7>>], this __is__ our God; we have waited for him, and he will save us: this __is__ the LORD; we have waited for him, we will be glad and rejoice in his salvation.
@ For in this mountain shall the hand of the LORD rest, and Moab shall be trodden down under him, even as straw is trodden down for the dunghill.note:tn[__trodden down under__: or, threshed, etc]note:tn[__trodden down for…__: or, threshed in Madmenah]
@ And ##he shall spread##note:x[<<Isa 5:25; 14:26>>] forth his hands in the midst of them, as he that swimmeth spreadeth forth __his hands__ to swim: and ##he shall bring##note:x[<<Isa 2:11>>] down their pride together with the spoils of their hands.
@ And the fortress of the high fort of thy walls shall he bring down, lay low, __and__ bring to the ground, __even__ to the dust.

=== @26

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Song of Salvation

@ In that day shall this song be sung in the land of Judah; We have a strong city; ##salvation##note:x[<<Isa 60:18; 62:11; Zech 2:5>>] will __God__ appoint __for__ walls and bulwarks.
@ ##Open##note:x[<<Ps 118:20; Isa 60:11>>] ye the gates, that the ##righteous##note:x[<<Rev 5:9>>] nation which keepeth the ##truth##note:tn[Heb. truths] may enter in.
@ Thou ##wilt##note:x[<<John 14:27; 16:33; Phil 4:7>>] keep __him__ in perfect peace, __whose__ ##mind##note:tn[or, thought, or, imagination] __is__ stayed __on thee:__ because he trusteth in thee.note:tn[__perfect…__: Heb. peace, peace]
@ ##Trust##note:x[<<Ps 62:8; Isa 12:2>>] ye ##in the##note:x[<<Phil 4:13>>] LORD for ever: for in the LORD JEHOVAH __is__ everlasting strength&#58;note:tn[__everlasting…__: Heb. the rock of ages]

@ ¶ For he ##bringeth##note:x[<<Job 40:11–13>>] down them that dwell on high; ##the lofty##note:x[<<Jer 51:37; Rev 18:2>>] city, he layeth it low; he layeth it low, __even__ to the ground; he bringeth it __even__ to the dust.
@ ##The foot shall tread it down, __even__ the feet of the poor, __and__ the steps of the needy.##note:x[<<Luke 10:19; Rom 16:20; Rev 2:26>>]
@ The ##way##note:x[<<2 Cor 1:12; Eph 2:10; 1 John 3:7>>] of the just __is__ uprightness: thou, most upright, dost weigh the path of the just.
@ Yea, in the way of thy judgments, O LORD, have ##we##note:x[<<Ps 37:3–7; Isa 33:2; Jas 5:7–11>>] waited for thee; the desire of __our__ soul __is__ to thy name, and to the remembrance of thee.
@ With my soul ##have I##note:x[<<Ps 119:62>>] desired thee in the night; yea, with ##my spirit##note:x[<<Ps 63:1; Matt 6:33>>] within me will I seek thee early: for when thy judgments __are__ in the earth, the inhabitants of the world will learn righteousness.
@ Let ##favour##note:x[<<Rom 2:4–5>>] be shewed to the wicked, __yet__ will he not learn righteousness: in the land of uprightness will he deal unjustly, ##and will not##note:x[<<Hos 11:7; John 5:37–38>>] behold the majesty of the LORD.
@ LORD, __when__ thy hand is lifted up, they will not see: __but__ they shall see, and be ashamed for __their__ envy at the people; yea, the fire of thine enemies shall devour them.note:tn[__at…__: or, toward thy people]

@ ¶ LORD, thou wilt ##ordain##note:x[<<Ps 29:11>>] peace ##for##note:x[<<Eph 2:10>>] us: for thou also hast wrought all our works note:x[<<Ps 57:2>>]##in us##note:tn[or, for us].
@ O LORD our God, __other__ lords beside thee have had dominion over us: __but__ by thee only will we make mention of thy name.
@ __They are__ ##dead##note:x[<<Isa 8:19; Rev 18:2–3>>], they shall not live; __they are__ deceased, they shall not rise: therefore hast thou visited and destroyed them, ##and made##note:x[<<Prov 10:7>>] all their memory to perish.
@ Thou hast ##increased##note:x[<<Isa 9:3>>] the nation, O LORD, thou hast increased the nation: thou art glorified: thou hadst removed __it__ far __unto__ all the ends of the earth.
@ LORD, ##in trouble##note:x[<<Hos 5:15>>] have they visited thee, they poured out a ##prayer##note:tn[Heb. secret speech] __when__ thy chastening __was__ upon them.
@ ##Like as a woman with child, __that__ draweth near the time of her delivery, is in pain, __and__ crieth out in her pangs; so have we been in thy sight, O LORD.##note:x[<<Isa 13:8; 21:3; John 16:21>>]
@ We have been with child, ##we have been in##note:x[<<Isa 37:3; Hos 13:13>>] pain, we have as it were brought forth wind; we have not wrought any deliverance in the earth; neither have ##the inhabitants##note:x[<<Ps 17:14>>] of the world fallen.
@ Thy ##dead __men__##note:x[<<Ezek 37:1–14>>] shall live, __together with__ ##my dead##note:x[<<Dan 12:2; Matt 27:52>>] body shall they arise. Awake and sing, ye that dwell in dust: for thy dew __is as__ the dew of herbs, and the earth shall cast out the dead.

@ ¶ Come, my people, enter thou into thy chambers, and shut thy doors about thee: ##hide##note:x[<<Ps 27:5>>] thyself as it were ##for a##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; Isa 54:7–8>>] little moment, until the indignation be overpast.
@ For, behold, the ##LORD##note:x[<<Isa 18:4; Jude 1:14–15>>] cometh out of his place to punish the inhabitants of the earth for their iniquity: the earth ##also##note:x[<<Rev 6:9–11>>] shall disclose her ##blood##note:tn[Heb. bloods], and shall no more cover her slain.

=== @27

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD’s Vineyard

[.parallel]
(<<John 15:1–8>>)

@ In that day the LORD ##with his##note:x[<<Isa 66:16>>] sore and great and strong sword shall punish leviathan the ##piercing##note:tn[or, crossing like a bar] serpent, even leviathan that crooked serpent; and he shall slay ##the dragon##note:x[<<Isa 51:9; Ezek 29:3>>] that __is__ in the sea.
@ In that day sing ye unto her, ##A vineyard##note:x[<<Jer 2:21>>] of red wine.
@ I the LORD ##do keep##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:9; John 15:1–2>>] it; I will ##water##note:x[<<Isa 58:11>>] it every moment: lest __any__ hurt it, I will keep it night and day.
@ Fury __is__ not in me: ##who would##note:x[<<Isa 10:17; Matt 3:12; Heb 6:8>>] set the briers __and__ thorns against me in battle? I would go through them, I would burn them together.note:tn[__go…__: or, march against]
@ Or ##let him##note:x[<<Job 22:21; Isa 25:4>>] take hold of my strength, __that__ he may make peace with me; ##__and__ he##note:x[<<Isa 57:19>>] shall make peace with me.
@ ##He shall cause them that come of Jacob to take root: Israel shall blossom and bud, and fill the face of the world with fruit.##note:x[<<Isa 37:31; Hos 14:5–6; Rom 11:16–26>>]

@ ¶ Hath he smitten him, as he smote those that smote him? __or__ is he slain according to the slaughter of them that are slain by him?note:tn[__as…__: Heb. according to the stroke of]
@ In ##measure##note:x[<<Jer 10:24>>], when it shooteth forth, ##thou wilt##note:x[<<Hos 4:1>>] debate with it: he stayeth ##his rough##note:x[<<Jer 4:11>>] wind in the day of the east wind.note:tn[__it shooteth…__: or, thou sendest it forth]note:tn[__he…__: or, when he removeth it with]
@ By ##this therefore##note:x[<<Isa 48:10>>] shall the iniquity of Jacob be purged; and this __is__ all the fruit to take away his sin; when he maketh all the stones of the altar as chalkstones that are beaten in sunder, the groves and ##images##note:tn[or, sun images] shall not stand up.
@ Yet the defenced city __shall be__ desolate, __and__ the habitation forsaken, and left like a wilderness: ##there shall the##note:x[<<Isa 17:2>>] calf feed, and there shall he lie down, and consume the branches thereof.
@ When the boughs thereof are withered, they shall be broken off: the women come, __and__ set them on fire: ##for it __is__##note:x[<<Isa 1:3; Jer 8:7>>] a people of no understanding: ##therefore##note:x[<<Isa 43:1>>] he that made them will not have mercy on them, and he that formed them will shew them no favour.

@ ¶ And it shall come to pass in that day, __that__ the LORD shall ##beat off##note:x[<<Gen 15:18; Isa 56:8>>] from the channel of the river unto the stream of Egypt, and ##ye shall be##note:x[<<Deut 30:3–4>>] gathered one by one, O ye children of Israel.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Captivity of Ephraim

@ Woe to the crown of pride, to the ##drunkards##note:x[<<Isa 28:7; Hos 7:5>>] of Ephraim, whose glorious beauty __is__ a fading flower, which __are__ on the head of the fat valleys of them that are ##overcome##note:tn[Heb. broken] with wine!
@ Behold, the Lord hath a mighty and strong one, __which__ ##as a tempest##note:x[<<Isa 30:30; Ezek 13:11; Nah 1:8>>] of hail __and__ a destroying storm, as a flood of mighty waters overflowing, shall cast down to the earth with the hand.
@ The crown of pride, the drunkards of Ephraim, shall be trodden under feet&#58;note:tn[__under…__: Heb. with feet]
@ And the glorious beauty, which __is__ on the head of the fat valley, shall be a fading flower, __and__ as ##the hasty##note:x[<<Mic 7:1; Nah 3:12>>] fruit before the summer; which __when__ he that looketh upon it seeth, while it is yet in his hand he ##eateth##note:tn[Heb. swalloweth] it up.

@ ¶ In that day ##shall the##note:x[<<Isa 60:19; 62:3; 1 Pet 5:4>>] LORD of hosts be for a crown of glory, and for a diadem of beauty, unto the residue of his people,
@ And ##for a spirit##note:x[<<Isa 11:2–4; John 5:30>>] of judgment to him that sitteth in judgment, ##and for strength##note:x[<<Ps 18:32–34>>] to them that turn the battle to the gate.

@ ¶ But they also have ##erred##note:x[<<Isa 19:14; 56:10–12; Luke 21:34>>] through wine, and through strong drink are out of the way; the priest and the prophet have erred through strong drink, they are swallowed up of wine, they are out of the way through strong drink; they err in vision, they stumble __in__ judgment.
@ ##For all tables are full of vomit __and__ filthiness, __so that there is__ no place __clean__.##note:x[<<Jer 48:26>>]

@ ¶ Whom ##shall he teach##note:x[<<Jer 6:10; John 12:38>>] knowledge? and whom shall he make to understand ##doctrine##note:tn[Heb. the hearing?]? __them that are__ ##weaned##note:x[<<Ps 131:2>>] from the milk, __and__ drawn from the breasts.
@ ##For precept##note:x[<<Isa 28:13; Jer 11:7; 2 Tim 3:7>>] ##__must be__##note:tn[or, hath been] upon precept, precept upon precept; line upon line, line upon line; here a little, __and__ there a little:
@ For ##with##note:x[<<Deut 28:49; Jer 5:15; 1 Cor 14:21>>] stammering lips and another tongue will he speak to this people.note:tn[__stammering…__: Heb. stammerings of lip]note:tn[__will…__: or, he hath spoken]
@ To whom he said, ##This##note:x[<<Isa 30:15; Jer 6:16; Matt 11:28–29>>] __is__ the rest __wherewith__ ye may cause the weary to rest; and this __is__ the refreshing: yet they would not hear.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Cornerstone in Zion

[.parallel]
(<<1 Corinthians 3:10–15; Ephesians 2:19–22; 1 Peter 2:1–8>>)

@ ¶ Wherefore hear the word of the LORD, ##ye##note:x[<<Isa 1:10; 28:22; 29:20>>] scornful men, that rule this people which __is__ in Jerusalem.
@ Because ye have said, ##We have##note:x[<<Eccl 8:8; Hos 2:18>>] made a covenant with death, and with hell are we at agreement; ##when##note:x[<<Isa 8:7–8>>] the overflowing scourge shall pass through, it shall not come unto us: for we have made lies our refuge, and under falsehood have we hid ourselves:

@ ¶ Therefore thus saith the Lord GOD, ##Behold##note:x[<<Ps 118:22; Acts 4:11–12; 1 Pet 2:6–8>>], I lay in Zion for a foundation a stone, a tried stone, a precious corner __stone__, a sure foundation: he that believeth shall not make haste.
@ ##Judgment##note:x[<<2 Kgs 21:13; Amos 7:7–9>>] also will I lay to the line, and righteousness to the plummet: ##and the hail##note:x[<<Rev 16:21>>] shall sweep away the refuge of lies, and the waters shall overflow the hiding place.

@ ¶ And ##your covenant##note:x[<<Isa 7:7; 8:10>>] with death shall be disannulled, and your agreement with hell shall not stand; when the overflowing scourge shall pass through, then ye shall be trodden down by it.note:tn[__trodden…__: Heb. a treading down to it]
@ From the time that it goeth forth it shall take you: for morning by morning shall it pass over, by day and by night: ##and it##note:x[<<Hab 3:16>>] shall be a vexation only __to__ understand the report.note:tn[__to…__: or, when he shall make you to understand doctrine]
@ For ##the bed##note:x[<<Isa 64:6; Rom 9:30–32; 1 Cor 1:18–31>>] is shorter than that __a man__ can stretch himself __on it:__ and the covering narrower than that he can wrap himself __in it__.
@ For the LORD shall rise up as ##__in__ mount Perazim##note:x[<<2 Sam 5:20; 1 Chr 14:11>>], he shall be wroth as __in__ ##the valley##note:x[<<Josh 10:12>>] of Gibeon, that he may do his work, his strange work; and bring to pass his act, his strange act.
@ Now therefore be ye not mockers, lest your bands be made strong: for I have heard from the Lord GOD of hosts ##a consumption##note:x[<<Isa 10:22–23; 24:1–23>>], even determined upon the whole earth.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Listen and Hear

@ ¶ Give ye ear, and hear my voice; hearken, and hear my speech.
@ Doth the plowman plow all day to sow? doth he open and break the clods of his ground?
@ When he hath made plain the face thereof, doth he not cast abroad the fitches, and scatter the cummin, and cast ##in the principal##note:x[<<Ezek 4:9>>] wheat and the appointed barley and the ##rie##note:tn[or, spelt] in their ##place##note:tn[Heb. border?]?note:tn[__the principal…__: or, the wheat in the principal place, and barley in the appointed place]
@ ##For his God##note:x[<<Exod 31:3–6; 36:2>>] doth instruct him to discretion, __and__ doth teach him.note:tn[__For…__: or, And he bindeth it in such sort as his God doth teach him]
@ For the fitches are not threshed with a threshing instrument, neither is a cart wheel turned about upon the cummin; but the fitches are beaten out with a staff, and the cummin with a rod.
@ Bread __corn__ is bruised; because he will not ever be threshing it, nor break __it with__ the wheel of his cart, nor bruise it __with__ his horsemen.
@ This also ##cometh##note:x[<<Job 5:9; Jer 32:19; Rom 11:33>>] forth from the LORD of hosts, __which__ is wonderful in counsel, __and__ excellent in working.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Woe to David’s City

[.parallel]
(<<Luke 19:41–44>>)

@ Woe to Ariel, to Ariel, ##the city##note:x[<<2 Sam 5:9>>] __where__ David dwelt! ##add##note:x[<<Amos 4:4–5>>] ye year to year; let them note:x[<<Mic 6:6–7>>]##kill##note:tn[Heb. cut off the heads of] sacrifices.note:tn[__Woe…__: or, O Ariel, that is, the lion of God]note:tn[__the city__: or, of the city]
@ Yet I will distress Ariel, and there shall be heaviness and sorrow: and it shall be unto me as Ariel.
@ ##And I will camp against thee round about, and will lay siege against thee with a mount, and I will raise forts against thee.##note:x[<<Luke 19:43–44>>]
@ And ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Ps 44:25; Isa 3:8>>] be brought down, __and__ shalt speak out of the ground, and thy speech shall be low out of the dust, and thy voice shall be, as of one that hath a familiar spirit, out of the ground, and thy speech shall note:x[<<Isa 8:19>>]##whisper##note:tn[Heb. peep, or, chirp] out of the dust.
@ Moreover ##the multitude##note:x[<<Isa 37:36>>] of thy strangers shall be like small dust, and the multitude of the terrible ones __shall be__ as chaff that passeth away: yea, it shall be ##at an##note:x[<<Isa 30:13; 1 Thess 5:3>>] instant suddenly.
@ ##Thou shalt be visited of the LORD of hosts with thunder, and with earthquake, and great noise, with storm and tempest, and the flame of devouring fire.##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:10; Matt 24:7; Mark 13:8>>]

@ ¶ And the multitude of all the nations that fight against Ariel, even all that fight against her and her munition, and that distress her, shall be ##as a dream##note:x[<<Job 20:8>>] of a night vision.
@ It shall even be as when an hungry __man__ dreameth, and, behold, he eateth; but he awaketh, and his soul is empty: or as when a thirsty man dreameth, and, behold, he drinketh; but he awaketh, and, behold, __he is__ faint, and his soul hath appetite: so shall the multitude of all the nations be, that fight against mount Zion.

@ ¶ Stay yourselves, ##and wonder##note:x[<<Isa 33:13–14; Rev 17:6>>]; cry ye out, and cry: ##they are##note:x[<<Isa 51:21–22>>] drunken, but not with wine; they stagger, but not with strong drink.note:tn[__cry ye…__: or, take your pleasure, and riot]
@ For ##the LORD##note:x[<<Rom 11:8>>] hath poured out upon you the spirit of deep sleep, and ##hath closed##note:x[<<Isa 44:18; 2 Thess 2:9–12>>] your eyes: the prophets and your ##rulers##note:tn[Heb. heads], the seers hath he covered.
@ And the vision of all is become unto you as the words of a ##book##note:tn[or, letter] ##that is sealed##note:x[<<Isa 8:16>>], which __men__ deliver to one that is learned, saying, Read this, I pray thee: and he saith, ##I cannot##note:x[<<Dan 12:4; Matt 13:11>>]; for it __is__ sealed:
@ And the book is delivered to him that is not learned, saying, Read this, I pray thee: and he saith, ##I am not##note:x[<<Isa 29:18; John 7:15–16>>] learned.

@ ¶ Wherefore the Lord said, ##Forasmuch##note:x[<<Jer 12:2; Ezek 33:31–33>>] as this people draw near __me__ with their mouth, and with their lips do honour me, but have removed their heart far from me, and ##their fear##note:x[<<Mark 7:2–13>>] toward me is taught by the precept of men:
@ Therefore, behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 28:21; Hab 1:5>>] ##proceed##note:tn[Heb. add] to do a marvellous work among this people, __even__ a marvellous work and a wonder: ##for the wisdom##note:x[<<1 Cor 1:19–24>>] of their wise __men__ shall perish, and the understanding of their prudent __men__ shall be hid.
@ Woe unto them that ##seek##note:x[<<Isa 30:1; Ezek 8:12>>] deep to hide their counsel from the LORD, and their works are in the dark, and they say, ##Who seeth##note:x[<<Isa 47:10>>] us? and who knoweth us?
@ note:x[<<Isa 45:9–10; Rom 9:19>>]Surely your turning of things upside down shall be esteemed as the potter’s clay: for shall the work say of him that made it, He made me not? ##or shall##note:x[<<Ps 94:8–9>>] the thing framed say of him that framed it, He had no understanding?

[discrete.pericope]
==== Sanctification for the Godly

@ __Is__ it not yet a very little while, and ##Lebanon##note:x[<<Isa 32:15; Matt 19:30>>] shall be turned into a fruitful field, and the fruitful field shall be esteemed as a forest?

@ ¶ And in that day shall ##the deaf##note:x[<<Prov 20:12; Isa 35:5; Matt 11:5>>] hear the words of the book, and the eyes of the blind shall see out of obscurity, and out of darkness.
@ The ##meek##note:x[<<Isa 61:1; Jas 2:5>>] also shall ##increase##note:tn[Heb. add] __their__ joy in the LORD, and ##the poor##note:x[<<Jas 2:5>>] among men shall rejoice in the Holy One of Israel.
@ For the terrible one is brought to nought, and ##the scorner##note:x[<<Isa 28:14–22>>] is consumed, ##and all##note:x[<<Mic 2:1>>] that watch for iniquity are cut off:
@ That make a man an offender for a word, ##and lay##note:x[<<Amos 5:10–12>>] a snare for him that reproveth in the gate, ##and turn##note:x[<<Jas 5:6>>] aside the just for a thing of nought.
@ Therefore thus saith the LORD, ##who redeemed##note:x[<<Isa 51:2>>] Abraham, concerning the house of Jacob, ##Jacob shall##note:x[<<Isa 45:17; Jer 30:10>>] not now be ashamed, neither shall his face now wax pale.
@ But when he seeth his children, ##the work##note:x[<<Isa 19:25; 45:11; Eph 2:10>>] of mine hands, in the midst of him, they shall sanctify my name, and sanctify the Holy One of Jacob, and shall fear the God of Israel.

=== @30

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Worthless Treaty with Egypt

@ Woe to ##the rebellious##note:x[<<Isa 1:2; 63:10; 65:2>>] children, saith the LORD, that take counsel, but not of me; and that cover with a covering, but not of my spirit, that they may add sin to sin:
@ That ##walk##note:x[<<Isa 36:6>>] to go down into Egypt, ##and have##note:x[<<Num 27:21>>] not asked at my mouth; to strengthen themselves in the strength of Pharaoh, and to trust in the shadow of Egypt!
@ Therefore shall ##the strength##note:x[<<Isa 20:5>>] of Pharaoh be your shame, and the trust in the shadow of Egypt ##__your__ confusion##note:x[<<Jer 17:5–6; Rom 10:11>>].
@ For his princes were at ##Zoan##note:x[<<Isa 19:11>>], and his ambassadors came to Hanes.
@ ##They were all ashamed of a people __that__ could not profit them, nor be an help nor profit, but a shame, and also a reproach.##note:x[<<Jer 2:36>>]
@ The ##burden##note:x[<<Isa 46:1–2>>] of the beasts of the south: ##into the land##note:x[<<Deut 8:15>>] of trouble and anguish, from whence __come__ the young and old lion, ##the viper##note:x[<<Deut 8:15>>] and fiery flying serpent, they will carry their riches upon the shoulders of young asses, and their treasures upon the bunches of camels, to a people __that__ shall not profit __them__.
@ For ##the Egyptians##note:x[<<Isa 31:1–5>>] shall help in vain, and to no purpose: therefore have I cried concerning this, ##Their##note:x[<<Isa 2:22; 7:4>>] strength __is__ to sit still.note:tn[__concerning…__: or, to her]

@ ¶ Now go, ##write##note:x[<<Deut 31:22; Isa 8:1; Hab 2:2>>] it before them in a table, and note it in a book, that it may be for the time to come for ever and ever&#58;note:tn[__the…__: Heb. the latter day]
@ That ##this __is__##note:x[<<Isa 30:1; Acts 7:51>>] a rebellious people, lying children, children __that__ will not hear the law of the LORD:
@ Which ##say##note:x[<<Jer 11:21>>] to the seers, See not; and to the prophets, Prophesy not unto us right things, ##speak##note:x[<<Ezek 13:7–10; Rom 16:18>>] unto us smooth things, prophesy deceits:
@ Get you out of the way, turn aside out of the path, cause the Holy One of Israel to cease from before us.
@ Wherefore thus saith the Holy One of Israel, ##Because##note:x[<<Isa 5:24; 31:1–3; Amos 2:4>>] ye despise this word, and trust in ##oppression##note:tn[or, fraud] and perverseness, and stay thereon:
@ Therefore this iniquity shall be to you ##as a breach##note:x[<<1 Kgs 20:30; Ezek 13:10–15>>] ready to fall, swelling out in a high wall, whose breaking ##cometh##note:x[<<Isa 29:5>>] suddenly at an instant.
@ And ##he shall break##note:x[<<Ps 2:9; Jer 19:10–11; Rev 2:27>>] it as the breaking of the potters’ vessel that is broken in pieces; he shall not spare: so that there shall not be found in the bursting of it a sherd to take fire from the hearth, or to take water __withal__ out of the pit.note:tn[__the potters’…__: Heb. the bottle of potters]
@ For thus saith the Lord GOD, the Holy One of Israel; ##In returning##note:x[<<Ps 125:1–2; Isa 26:3–4; 32:17>>] and rest shall ye be saved; in quietness and in confidence shall be your strength: and ye would not.
@ But ye said, No; ##for we will##note:x[<<Isa 31:1>>] flee upon horses; therefore shall ye flee: and, We will ride upon the swift; therefore shall they that pursue you be swift.
@ One ##thousand##note:x[<<Lev 26:36; Deut 28:25; 32:30>>] __shall flee__ at the rebuke of one; at the rebuke of five shall ye flee: till ye be left as a beacon upon the top of a mountain, and as an ensign on an hill.note:tn[__a beacon__: or, a tree bereft of branches, or, boughs: or, a mast]

[discrete.pericope]
==== God Will Be Gracious

@ ¶ And therefore will the LORD ##wait##note:x[<<2 Pet 3:9>>], that he may be gracious unto you, and therefore will he be exalted, that he may have mercy upon you: for the LORD __is__ a God of judgment: ##blessed##note:x[<<Prov 16:20; Lam 3:25–26>>] __are__ all they that wait for him.
@ For the people shall dwell in Zion at Jerusalem: ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Isa 60:20>>] weep no more: ##he will##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; Isa 65:24>>] be very gracious unto thee at the voice of thy cry; when he shall hear it, he will answer thee.
@ And __though__ the Lord give you ##the bread##note:x[<<1 Kgs 22:27; Ps 80:5>>] of adversity, and the water of ##affliction##note:tn[or, oppression], ##yet shall##note:x[<<Amos 8:11–12>>] not thy teachers be removed into a corner any more, but thine eyes shall see thy teachers:
@ And ##thine ears##note:x[<<Prov 3:5–6; Isa 58:11>>] shall hear a word behind thee, saying, This __is__ the way, walk ye in it, ##when ye turn to the right##note:x[<<Ps 32:8>>] hand, and when ye turn to the left.
@ Ye shall ##defile##note:x[<<Isa 31:7; Zech 13:2>>] also the covering of ##thy graven images of silver##note:x[<<Judg 17:3–4>>], and the ornament of thy molten images of gold: thou shalt ##cast##note:tn[Heb. scatter] them away as a menstruous cloth; thou shalt say unto it, Get thee hence.note:tn[__thy graven…__: Heb. the graven images of thy silver]
@ Then ##shall he##note:x[<<Ps 65:9–13; Isa 32:20; Zech 8:11–12>>] give the rain of thy seed, that thou shalt sow the ground withal; and bread of the increase of the earth, and it shall be fat and plenteous: in that day shall thy cattle feed in large pastures.
@ The oxen likewise and the young asses that ear the ground shall eat ##clean##note:tn[or, savoury: Heb. leavened] provender, which hath been winnowed with the shovel and with the fan.
@ And there shall be upon every high mountain, and upon every high hill, rivers __and__ streams of waters in the day of the great slaughter, when the towers fall.note:tn[__high hill__: Heb. lifted up, etc]
@ Moreover ##the light of the moon##note:x[<<Isa 60:19–20; Rev 22:5>>] shall be as the light of the sun, and the light of the sun shall be sevenfold, as the light of seven days, in the day that the LORD ##bindeth##note:x[<<Deut 32:39>>] up the breach of his people, and healeth the stroke of their wound.

@ ¶ Behold, the name of the LORD cometh from far, ##burning##note:x[<<2 Thess 2:8>>] __with__ his anger, and ##the burden __thereof__##note:x[<<Zeph 3:8>>] __is__ ##heavy##note:tn[Heb. heaviness]: his lips are full of indignation, and his tongue as a devouring fire&#58;note:tn[__burden…__: or, grievousness of flame]
@ And ##his breath##note:x[<<Isa 11:4; 2 Thess 2:8>>], as an overflowing stream, shall reach to the midst of the neck, to sift the nations with the sieve of vanity: and __there shall be__ ##a bridle##note:x[<<Isa 37:29>>] in the jaws of the people, causing __them__ to err.
@ ##Ye shall##note:x[<<Exod 15:1–21>>] have a song, as ##in the night##note:x[<<Ps 42:4>>] __when__ a holy solemnity is kept; and gladness of heart, as when one goeth ##with a pipe##note:x[<<Ps 42:4>>] to come into the mountain of the LORD, to the mighty One of Israel.note:tn[__mighty…__: Heb. Rock]
@ And ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 18:13–14; Isa 29:6>>] shall cause his glorious voice to be heard, and shall shew the lighting down of his arm, with the indignation of __his__ anger, and __with__ ##the flame##note:x[<<Ps 18:13–14>>] of a devouring fire, __with__ scattering, and tempest, and hailstones.note:tn[__his glorious…__: Heb. the glory of his voice]
@ For through the voice of the LORD shall the Assyrian be beaten down, __which__ smote with a rod.
@ And __in__ every place where the grounded staff shall pass, which the LORD shall lay upon him, __it__ shall be with tabrets and harps: and in battles of ##shaking##note:x[<<Isa 11:15>>] will he fight ##with it##note:tn[or, against them].note:tn[__in every…__: Heb. every passing of the rod founded]note:tn[__lay…__: Heb. cause to rest upon]
@ For ##Tophet##note:x[<<2 Kgs 23:10; Jer 19:6>>] __is__ ordained of old; yea, for the king it is prepared; he hath made __it__ deep __and__ large: the pile thereof __is__ fire and much wood; ##the breath##note:x[<<Gen 19:24>>] of the LORD, like a stream of brimstone, doth kindle it.note:tn[__of old__: Heb. from yesterday]

=== @31

[discrete.pericope]
==== Woe to Those Who Rely on Egypt

@ note:x[<<Jer 17:5>>]Woe to them that go down to Egypt for help; and ##stay on horses##note:x[<<Deut 17:16; Ps 20:7>>], and trust in chariots, because __they are__ many; and in horsemen, because they are very strong; but they look not unto the Holy One of Israel, neither seek the LORD!
@ Yet ##he also##note:x[<<Jer 10:12>>] __is__ wise, and ##will bring##note:x[<<Isa 45:7>>] evil, and ##will not##note:x[<<Num 23:19>>] call back his words: but will arise against the house of the evildoers, and against the help of them that work iniquity.note:tn[__call…__: Heb. remove]
@ Now ##the Egyptians##note:x[<<Ps 9:20>>] __are__ men, and not God; and their horses flesh, and not spirit. When the LORD shall ##stretch##note:x[<<Isa 9:17; Ezek 20:33–34>>] out his hand, both he that helpeth shall fall, and he that is holpen shall fall down, and they all shall fail together.
@ For thus hath the LORD spoken unto me, ##Like as##note:x[<<Hos 11:10; Amos 3:8>>] the lion and the young lion roaring on his prey, when a multitude of shepherds is called forth against him, __he__ will not be afraid of their voice, nor abase himself for the ##noise##note:tn[or, multitude] of them: ##so shall##note:x[<<Isa 42:13>>] the LORD of hosts come down to fight for mount Zion, and for the hill thereof.
@ As ##birds##note:x[<<Deut 32:11; Ps 91:4>>] flying, so will the LORD of hosts defend Jerusalem; ##defending##note:x[<<Ps 37:40>>] also he will deliver __it; and__ passing over he will preserve it.

@ ¶ ##Turn##note:x[<<Isa 55:7; Jer 3:14, 22>>] ye unto __him from__ whom the children of Israel have deeply revolted.
@ For ##in that##note:x[<<Isa 2:20; 30:22; Ezek 36:25>>] day every man shall cast away his idols of silver, and his idols of gold, which your own hands have made unto you __for__ a sin.note:tn[__his idols of gold__: Heb. the idols of his gold]

@ ¶ Then ##shall the##note:x[<<Isa 14:25>>] Assyrian fall with the sword, not of a mighty man; and the sword, not of a mean man, shall devour him: but he shall flee ##from##note:tn[or, for fear of] the sword, and his young men shall be ##discomfited##note:tn[or, tributary: Heb. for melting, or, tribute].
@ And he shall pass over to his strong hold for fear, and his princes shall be afraid of the ensign, saith the LORD, ##whose fire##note:x[<<Isa 29:6; Mal 4:1>>] __is__ in Zion, and his furnace in Jerusalem.note:tn[__he…__: Heb. his rock shall pass away for fear]note:tn[__his strong hold__: or, his strength]

=== @32

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Righteous King

@ Behold, a ##king##note:x[<<Isa 9:6–7; Jer 33:15; Heb 1:8–9>>] shall reign in righteousness, and princes shall rule in judgment.
@ And ##a man##note:x[<<Isa 9:6>>] shall be as ##an hiding##note:x[<<Isa 25:4; 44:3>>] place from the wind, and a covert from the tempest; as rivers of water in a dry place, as the shadow of a ##great##note:tn[Heb. heavy] rock in a weary land.
@ ##And the eyes of them that see shall not be dim, and the ears of them that hear shall hearken.##note:x[<<Isa 29:18; 35:5–6; Acts 26:18>>]
@ The ##heart##note:x[<<Isa 29:24>>] also of the ##rash##note:tn[Heb. hasty] shall understand knowledge, and the tongue of the stammerers shall be ready to speak ##plainly##note:tn[or, elegantly].
@ The ##vile##note:x[<<Isa 5:20>>] person shall be no more called liberal, nor the churl said __to be__ bountiful.
@ For ##the vile##note:x[<<Matt 15:19>>] person will speak villany, and his heart will work iniquity, to practise hypocrisy, and to utter error against the LORD, to make empty the soul of the hungry, and he will cause the drink of the thirsty to fail.
@ The ##instruments##note:x[<<Jer 5:26–28>>] also of the churl __are__ evil: he deviseth wicked devices to destroy the poor with lying words, even when the needy speaketh right.note:tn[__the needy…__: or, he speaketh against the poor in judgment]
@ But the liberal deviseth liberal things; and by liberal things shall he ##stand##note:tn[or, be established].

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Women of Jerusalem

@ ¶ Rise up, ##ye women##note:x[<<Isa 3:16>>] that are at ease; hear my voice, ye careless daughters; ##give ear##note:x[<<Isa 28:23; Matt 13:9>>] unto my speech.
@ Many days and years shall ye be troubled, ye careless women: for the vintage shall fail, the gathering shall not come.note:tn[__Many…__: Heb. Days above a year]
@ Tremble, ye women that are at ease; be troubled, ye careless ones: strip you, and make you bare, and gird __sackcloth__ upon __your__ loins.
@ They shall lament for the teats, for the pleasant fields, for the fruitful vine.note:tn[__pleasant…__: Heb. fields of desire]
@ Upon the land of my people shall ##come##note:x[<<Hos 9:6>>] up thorns __and__ briers; yea, upon all the houses of joy ##__in__ the##note:x[<<Isa 22:2>>] joyous city&#58;note:tn[__yea…__: or, burning upon]
@ Because the palaces shall be forsaken; the multitude of the city shall be left; the forts and towers shall be for dens for ever, a joy of wild asses, a pasture of flocks;note:tn[__forts…__: or, clifts and watchtowers]
@ Until ##the spirit##note:x[<<Isa 44:3; Ezek 39:29>>] be poured upon us from on high, and the ##wilderness##note:x[<<Isa 29:17>>] be a fruitful field, and the fruitful field be counted for a forest.
@ Then judgment shall dwell in the wilderness, and righteousness remain in the fruitful field.
@ And ##the work##note:x[<<Ps 119:165; Isa 26:3; Rom 14:17>>] of righteousness shall be peace; and the effect of righteousness quietness and assurance for ever.
@ ##And my people shall dwell in a peaceable habitation, and in sure dwellings, and in quiet resting places;##note:x[<<Jer 33:16; Hos 2:18–23; Zech 2:5>>]
@ When ##it shall##note:x[<<Isa 28:2, 17; 30:30>>] hail, coming down on the forest; and the city shall be low in a low place.note:tn[__low in…__: or, utterly abased]
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Eccl 11:1; Isa 30:23>>] __are__ ye that sow beside all waters, that send forth __thither__ the feet of the ox and the ass.

=== @33

[discrete.pericope]
==== The LORD Is Exalted

@ Woe to ##thee that##note:x[<<Isa 24:16>>] spoilest, and thou __wast__ not spoiled; and dealest treacherously, and they dealt not treacherously with thee! ##when thou shalt cease##note:x[<<Matt 7:2>>] to spoil, thou shalt be spoiled; __and__ when thou shalt make an end to deal treacherously, they shall deal treacherously with thee.
@ O LORD, ##be gracious##note:x[<<Ps 62:8; Isa 30:18–19>>] unto us; we have waited for thee: be thou their arm every morning, ##our salvation##note:x[<<2 Cor 1:3–4>>] also in the time of trouble.
@ At the noise of the tumult the people fled; at the lifting up of thyself the nations were scattered.
@ And your spoil shall be gathered __like__ the gathering of the caterpiller: as the running to and fro of locusts shall he run upon them.
@ ##The LORD##note:x[<<Isa 2:17>>] is exalted; for he dwelleth on high: he hath filled Zion with judgment and righteousness.
@ And wisdom and knowledge shall be the stability of thy times, __and__ strength of ##salvation##note:tn[Heb. salvations]: the ##fear##note:x[<<Ps 112:1–3; Prov 19:23; 1 Tim 4:8>>] of the LORD __is__ his treasure.
@ Behold, their valiant ones shall cry without: the ambassadors of peace shall weep bitterly.note:tn[__valiant…__: or, messengers]
@ The ##highways##note:x[<<Judg 5:6>>] lie waste, the wayfaring man ceaseth: he hath broken the covenant, he hath despised the cities, he regardeth no man.
@ The earth mourneth __and__ languisheth: Lebanon is ashamed __and__ hewn down: ##Sharon##note:x[<<Isa 35:2>>] is like a wilderness; and Bashan and Carmel shake off __their fruits__.note:tn[__hewn…__: or, withered away]
@ ##Now will I rise##note:x[<<Ps 12:5; 102:13–18; Isa 42:13–14>>], saith the LORD; now will I be exalted; now will I lift up myself.
@ Ye shall ##conceive##note:x[<<Ps 7:14; Isa 59:4; Jas 1:15>>] chaff, ye shall bring forth stubble: your breath, __as__ fire, shall devour you.
@ And the people shall be __as__ the burnings of lime: __as__ thorns cut up shall they be burned in the fire.

@ ¶ ##Hear##note:x[<<Ps 48:10; Eph 2:11–18>>], ye __that are__ far off, what I have done; and, ye __that are__ near, acknowledge my might.
@ The sinners in Zion are afraid; fearfulness hath surprised the hypocrites. ##Who among us shall dwell with the##note:x[<<Heb 12:29>>] devouring fire? who among us shall dwell with ##everlasting##note:x[<<Isa 66:24; Matt 25:46>>] burnings?
@ He ##that walketh##note:x[<<1 John 3:7>>] ##righteously##note:tn[Heb. in righteousnesses], and speaketh ##uprightly##note:tn[Heb. uprightnesses]; he that despiseth the gain of ##oppressions##note:tn[or, deceits], that shaketh his hands from holding of bribes, that ##stoppeth##note:x[<<Eph 5:11–13>>] his ears from hearing of ##blood##note:tn[Heb. bloods], and ##shutteth##note:x[<<Ps 119:37>>] his eyes from seeing evil;
@ He ##shall dwell##note:x[<<Prov 1:33; Isa 32:18>>] on ##high##note:tn[Heb. heights, or, high places]: his place of defence __shall be__ the munitions of rocks: ##bread##note:x[<<Luke 12:29–31>>] shall be given him; his waters __shall be__ sure.
@ Thine ##eyes##note:x[<<Zech 9:17; John 14:21; 1 John 3:2>>] shall see the king in his beauty: they shall behold the land that is very far off.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. of far distances]
@ Thine heart shall meditate terror. Where __is__ the scribe? where __is__ the ##receiver##note:tn[Heb. weigher?]? where __is__ he that counted the towers?
@ note:x[<<Deut 28:49–50>>]Thou shalt not see a fierce people, a people of a ##deeper##note:x[<<Isa 28:11; Jer 5:15>>] speech than thou canst perceive; of a ##stammering##note:tn[or, ridiculous] tongue, __that thou canst__ not understand.
@ Look upon Zion, the city of our solemnities: ##thine eyes##note:x[<<Ps 46:5>>] shall see Jerusalem a quiet habitation, a tabernacle __that__ shall not be taken down; not one of the stakes thereof shall ever be removed, neither shall any of the cords thereof be broken.
@ But there the glorious LORD __will be__ unto us ##a place##note:x[<<Ps 46:4–5>>] of broad rivers __and__ streams; wherein shall go no galley with oars, neither shall gallant ship pass thereby.note:tn[__of…__: Heb. broad of spaces, or, hands]
@ For the LORD __is__ our judge, the LORD __is__ our note:x[<<Jas 4:12>>]##lawgiver##note:tn[Heb. statutemaker], the LORD __is__ our king; ##he will##note:x[<<Zeph 3:15–17; Acts 5:31>>] save us.
@ Thy tacklings are loosed; they could not well strengthen their mast, they could not spread the sail: then is the prey of a great spoil divided; ##the lame##note:x[<<2 Kgs 7:8>>] take the prey.note:tn[__Thy…__: or, They have forsaken thy tacklings]
@ And ##the inhabitant##note:x[<<Exod 15:26; Rev 21:4>>] shall not say, I am sick: the people that dwell therein ##__shall be__ forgiven##note:x[<<Jer 50:20>>] __their__ iniquity.

=== @34

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on the Nations

@ ##Come##note:x[<<Isa 41:1; 43:9>>] near, ye nations, to hear; and hearken, ye people: ##let the##note:x[<<Isa 1:2>>] earth hear, and all that is therein; the world, and all things that come forth of it.note:tn[__all that…__: Heb. the fulness thereof]
@ For the indignation of the LORD __is__ upon all nations, and __his__ fury upon all their armies: he hath utterly destroyed them, he hath delivered them to the slaughter.
@ Their ##slain##note:x[<<Joel 2:20>>] also shall be cast out, and their stink shall come up out of their carcases, and the mountains shall be melted with their blood.
@ And ##all the##note:x[<<Ezek 32:7–8; Matt 24:29; Rev 6:13–14>>] host of heaven shall be dissolved, and the heavens shall be rolled together as a scroll: and all their host shall fall down, as the leaf falleth off from the vine, and as a falling __fig__ from the fig tree.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgment on Edom

[.parallel]
(<<Isaiah 21:11–12>>)

@ For ##my sword##note:x[<<Jer 46:10>>] shall be bathed in heaven: behold, it shall come down ##upon Idumea##note:x[<<Isa 63:1; Amos 1:11–12>>], and upon the people of my curse, to judgment.
@ The sword of the LORD is filled with blood, it is made fat with fatness, __and__ with the blood of lambs and goats, with the fat of the kidneys of rams: for ##the LORD hath##note:x[<<Isa 63:1>>] a sacrifice in Bozrah, and a great slaughter in the land of Idumea.
@ And the note:x[<<Num 23:22>>]##unicorns##note:tn[or, rhinocerots] shall come down with them, and ##the bullocks##note:x[<<Ps 68:30>>] with the bulls; and their land shall be ##soaked##note:tn[or, drunken] with blood, and their dust made fat with fatness.
@ For __it is__ the day of the LORD’s vengeance, ##__and__ the##note:x[<<Isa 35:4; 61:2; 63:4>>] year of recompences for the controversy of Zion.

@ ##And the streams thereof shall be turned into pitch, and the dust thereof into brimstone, and the land thereof shall become burning pitch.##note:x[<<Deut 29:23>>]
@ It ##shall not##note:x[<<Isa 66:24>>] be quenched night nor day; ##the smoke##note:x[<<Rev 19:3>>] thereof shall go up for ever: ##from##note:x[<<Isa 13:20>>] generation to generation it shall lie waste; none shall pass through it for ever and ever.

@ ¶ But the note:x[<<Zeph 2:14>>]##cormorant##note:tn[or, pelican] and the bittern shall possess it; the owl also and the raven shall dwell in it: and he shall ##stretch##note:x[<<2 Kgs 21:13; Lam 2:8>>] out upon it the line of confusion, and the stones of emptiness.
@ They shall call the nobles thereof to the kingdom, but none __shall be__ there, and all her princes shall be nothing.
@ And thorns shall come up in her palaces, nettles and brambles in the fortresses thereof: and it shall be ##an habitation##note:x[<<Jer 9:11; 10:22; Mal 1:3>>] of dragons, __and__ a court for ##owls##note:tn[or, ostriches: Heb. daughters of the owl].
@ The wild beasts of the desert shall also meet with the wild beasts of the island, and the satyr shall cry to his fellow; the screech owl also shall rest there, and find for herself a place of rest.note:tn[__The wild beasts of the desert__: Heb. Ziim]note:tn[__the wild beasts of the island__: Heb. Ijim]note:tn[__screech…__: or, night monster]
@ There shall the great owl make her nest, and lay, and hatch, and gather under her shadow: there shall the vultures also be gathered, every one with her mate.

@ ¶ ##Seek##note:x[<<Josh 1:8; Matt 5:18>>] ye out of the book of the LORD, and read: no one of these shall fail, none shall want her mate: for ##my mouth##note:x[<<Ps 33:9>>] it hath commanded, and his spirit it hath gathered them.
@ And he hath cast the lot for them, and his hand hath divided it unto them by line: ##they shall##note:x[<<Isa 34:10>>] possess it for ever, from generation to generation shall they dwell therein.

=== @35

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Glory of Zion

@ The ##wilderness##note:x[<<Isa 51:3; Ezek 36:35>>] and the solitary place shall be glad for them; and the ##desert##note:x[<<Isa 55:12–13>>] shall rejoice, and blossom as the rose.
@ It shall blossom abundantly, ##and rejoice##note:x[<<Rom 10:15>>] even with joy and singing: ##the glory##note:x[<<Isa 60:13>>] of Lebanon shall be given unto it, ##the excellency##note:x[<<Isa 60:13>>] of Carmel and Sharon, they shall see the glory of the LORD, __and__ the excellency of our God.

@ ¶ ##Strengthen ye the weak hands, and confirm the feeble knees.##note:x[<<Job 4:3–4; Luke 22:32; Heb 12:12>>]
@ Say to them __that are__ of a ##fearful##note:tn[Heb. hasty] heart, Be strong, ##fear not##note:x[<<Isa 41:10–14; Zeph 3:16–17>>]: ##behold##note:x[<<Jas 5:7–9>>], your God will come __with__ vengeance, __even__ God __with__ a recompence; he will come and save you.

@ Then ##the eyes##note:x[<<Isa 29:18; 42:6–7>>] of the blind shall be opened, and ##the ears##note:x[<<Luke 7:20–23>>] of the deaf shall be unstopped.
@ Then ##shall the lame##note:x[<<John 5:8–9>>] __man__ leap as an hart, and ##the tongue##note:x[<<Luke 11:14>>] of the dumb sing: ##for##note:x[<<John 7:37–39>>] in the wilderness shall waters break out, and streams in the desert.
@ And ##the parched##note:x[<<John 4:14>>] ground shall become a pool, and the thirsty land springs of water: ##in the##note:x[<<Isa 34:13; Acts 26:18>>] habitation of dragons, where each lay, __shall be__ grass with reeds and rushes.note:tn[__grass…__: or, a court for reeds, etc]
@ And ##an highway##note:x[<<Isa 42:16; John 14:6>>] shall be there, and a way, and it shall be called ##The way##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:14–15>>] of holiness; the unclean shall not pass over it; but it __shall be__ for those: the wayfaring men, though fools, shall not err __therein__.note:tn[__but…__: or, for he shall be with them]
@ No lion shall be there, nor __any__ ravenous beast shall go up thereon, it shall not be found there; but the redeemed shall walk __there:__
@ And the ransomed of the LORD shall return, and come to Zion with songs and everlasting joy upon their heads: they shall obtain joy and gladness, ##and sorrow##note:x[<<Isa 25:8; 30:19; Rev 21:4>>] and sighing shall flee away.

=== @36

[discrete.pericope]
==== Sennacherib Threatens Jerusalem

[.parallel]
(<<2 Kings 18:13–37; 2 Chronicles 32:1–8>>)

@ Now ##it came##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:13; 2 Chr 32:1>>] to pass in the fourteenth year of king Hezekiah, __that__ Sennacherib king of Assyria came up against all the defenced cities of Judah, and took them.
@ And the king of Assyria ##sent##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:17–37; 2 Chr 32:9–23>>] Rabshakeh from Lachish to Jerusalem unto king Hezekiah with a great army. And he stood by ##the conduit##note:x[<<Isa 7:3>>] of the upper pool in the highway of the fuller’s field.
@ Then came forth unto him Eliakim, Hilkiah’s son, which was over the house, and Shebna the ##scribe##note:tn[or, secretary], and Joah, Asaph’s son, the recorder.

@ ¶ And Rabshakeh said unto them, Say ye now to Hezekiah, Thus saith the great king, the king of Assyria, What confidence __is__ this wherein thou trustest?
@ I say, __sayest thou__, (but __they are but__ vain words) __I have__ counsel and strength for war: now on whom dost thou trust, ##that##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:7>>] thou rebellest against me?note:tn[__vain…__: Heb. a word of lips]note:tn[__I have…__: or, but counsel and strength are for the war]
@ ##Lo, thou trustest in the staff of this broken reed, on Egypt; whereon if a man lean, it will go into his hand, and pierce it: so __is__ Pharaoh king of Egypt to all that trust in him.##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:21; Isa 20:5–6; Ezek 29:6–7>>]
@ But if thou say to me, We trust in the LORD our God: ##__is it__ not##note:x[<<2 Kgs 18:4>>] he, whose high places and whose altars Hezekiah hath taken away, and said to Judah and to Jerusalem, Ye shall worship before this altar?
@ Now therefore give ##pledges##note:tn[or, hostages], I pray thee, to my master the king of Assyria, and I will give thee two thousand horses, if thou be able on thy part to set riders upon them.
@ How then wilt thou turn away the face of one captain of the least of my master’s servants, and put thy trust on Egypt for chariots and for horsemen?

@ ¶ But Rabshakeh said, Hath my master sent me to thy master and to thee to speak these words? __hath he__ not __sent me__ to the men that sit upon the wall, ##that they may##note:x[<<Lev 26:29; 2 Kgs 18:27>>] eat their own dung, and drink their own piss with you?

[.parallel]

@ Like ##a crane##note:x[<<Job 30:29>>] __or__ a swallow, so did I chatter: ##I did mourn##note:x[<<Isa 59:11>>] as a dove: ##mine eyes##note:x[<<Ps 69:3>>] fail __with looking__ upward: O LORD, I am oppressed; undertake for me.note:tn[__undertake…__: or, ease me]

@ For ##the grave##note:x[<<Ps 6:5; 30:9; Eccl 9:10>>] cannot praise thee, death can __not__ celebrate thee: they that go down into the pit cannot hope for thy truth.

@ He pursued them, __and__ passed ##safely##note:tn[Heb. in peace]; __even__ by the way __that__ he had not gone with his feet.

@ ¶ ##Fear##note:x[<<Josh 1:9; Isa 43:1; Rom 8:31>>] thou not; for I __am__ with thee: be not dismayed; for I __am__ thy God: I will strengthen thee; yea, I will help thee; yea, I will uphold thee with the right hand of my righteousness.
@ Behold, ##all they##note:x[<<Exod 23:22; Isa 45:24; Rev 3:9>>] that were incensed against thee shall be ashamed and confounded: they shall be as nothing; and they that strive with thee shall perish.note:tn[__they that strive…__: Heb. the men of thy strife]
@ Thou ##shalt seek##note:x[<<Job 20:7–9; Ps 37:35–36>>] them, and shalt not find them, __even__ them that contended with thee: they that war against thee shall be as nothing, and as a thing of nought.note:tn[__them that…__: Heb. the men of thy contention]note:tn[__they…__: Heb. the men of thy war]
@ For I the LORD thy God ##will hold##note:x[<<Ps 63:8; 73:23; 109:31>>] thy right hand, saying unto thee, Fear not; I will help thee.

@ Thou ##shalt fan##note:x[<<Jer 51:2>>] them, and the wind shall carry them away, and the whirlwind shall scatter them: and ##thou shalt rejoice##note:x[<<Isa 45:24–25; 61:10–11>>] in the LORD, __and__ shalt glory in the Holy One of Israel.
