==== Meaningless Idols

@ note:x[<<Job 23:3–4>>]##Produce##note:tn[Heb. Cause to come near] your cause, saith the LORD; bring forth your strong __reasons__, saith the King of Jacob.
@ Let them bring __them__ forth, ##and shew##note:x[<<Isa 45:21>>] us what shall happen: let them shew the former things, what they __be__, that we may consider them, and know the latter end of them; or declare us things for to come.note:tn[__consider…__: Heb. set our heart upon them]
@ Shew the things that are to come hereafter, ##that we may know##note:x[<<Isa 42:9; John 13:19>>] that ye __are__ gods: yea, ##do good##note:x[<<Jer 10:5>>], or do evil, that we may be dismayed, and behold __it__ together.
@ Behold, ##ye __are__##note:x[<<Ps 115:8; Isa 41:29; 1 Cor 8:4>>] of nothing, and your work of nought: an abomination __is he that__ chooseth you.note:tn[__of nothing__: or, worse than nothing]note:tn[__of nought__: or, worse than of a viper]
@ I have raised up __one__ from the north, and he shall come: from the rising of the sun shall he call upon my name: and he shall ##come upon##note:x[<<2 Sam 22:43; Isa 10:6; 41:2>>] princes as __upon__ morter, and as the potter treadeth clay.
@ Who hath ##declared##note:x[<<Isa 41:22; 44:7; 45:21>>] from the beginning, that we may know? and beforetime, that we may say, __He is__ righteous? yea, __there is__ none that sheweth, yea, __there is__ none that declareth, yea, __there is__ none that heareth your words.
@ The first __shall say__ to Zion, Behold, behold them: and ##I will give##note:x[<<Isa 40:9; 44:28; 52:7>>] to Jerusalem one that bringeth good tidings.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Israel’s Only Savior

@ But now thus saith the LORD that ##created##note:x[<<Ps 100:3; Isa 43:7>>] thee, O Jacob, and he that formed thee, O Israel, Fear not: for I have redeemed thee, I have called __thee__ by thy name; ##thou __art__ mine##note:x[<<Zech 13:9>>].
@ When thou ##passest##note:x[<<Ps 66:12>>] through the waters, ##I __will be__##note:x[<<Josh 1:9; Isa 41:10>>] with thee; and through the rivers, they shall not overflow thee: when thou walkest through the fire, thou shalt not be burned; neither shall the flame kindle upon thee.

@ Fear not: for I __am__ with thee: ##I will##note:x[<<Deut 30:3; Isa 49:12; Luke 13:29>>] bring thy seed from the east, and gather thee from the west;

@ __Even__ every one that is called by my name: ##for I##note:x[<<Eph 2:10>>] have created him ##for my##note:x[<<Isa 43:21; Eph 1:12>>] glory, I have formed him; yea, I have made him.

@ Ye __are__ my witnesses, saith the LORD, and my servant whom I have chosen: ##that ye##note:x[<<Isa 45:6; 46:8–9>>] may know and believe me, and understand that ##I __am__ he##note:x[<<Isa 41:4>>]: before me there was no God formed, neither shall there be after me.note:tn[__no…__: or, nothing formed of God]
@ ##I, __even__ I, __am__ the LORD; and beside me __there is__ no saviour.##note:x[<<Isa 43:3; 45:21–22; Hos 13:4>>]
@ I have declared, and have saved, and I have shewed, when __there was__ ##no strange##note:x[<<Deut 32:12; Ps 81:9–10>>] __god__ among you: therefore ye __are__ ##my witnesses##note:x[<<Isa 44:8>>], saith the LORD, that I __am__ God.

@ ¶ They that ##make##note:x[<<Isa 41:24, 29; Jer 10:14–15>>] a graven image __are__ all of them vanity; and their ##delectable##note:tn[Heb. desirable] things shall not profit; and they __are__ their own witnesses; they see not, nor know; that they may be ashamed.

@ ##The smith with##note:x[<<Isa 40:19; 41:6–7>>] the tongs both worketh in the coals, and fashioneth it with hammers, and worketh it with the strength of his arms: yea, he is hungry, and his strength faileth: he drinketh no water, and is faint.note:tn[__with the tongs__: or, with an axe]
@ The carpenter stretcheth out __his__ rule; he marketh it out with a line; he fitteth it with planes, and he marketh it out with the compass, and maketh it after the figure of a man, according to the beauty of a man; ##that it may##note:x[<<Judg 17:4–5>>] remain in the house.
@ He heweth him down cedars, and taketh the cypress and the oak, which he ##strengtheneth##note:tn[or, taketh courage] for himself among the trees of the forest: he planteth an ash, and the rain doth nourish __it__.
@ Then shall it be for a man to burn: for he will take thereof, and warm himself; yea, he kindleth __it__, and baketh bread; yea, ##he maketh a god##note:x[<<2 Chr 25:14>>], and worshippeth __it;__ he maketh it a graven image, and falleth down thereto.

==== Jerusalem to Be Restored

==== God Calls Cyrus

[.parallel]
(<<2 Chronicles 36:22–23; Ezra 1:1–4>>)

@ Thus saith the LORD ##to his##note:x[<<Isa 44:28>>] anointed, to Cyrus, ##whose##note:x[<<Isa 42:6>>] right hand I have holden, ##to subdue##note:x[<<Jer 51:20–24>>] nations before him; and I will loose the loins of kings, to open before him the two leaved gates; and the gates shall not be shut;note:tn[__have…__: or, strengthened]
@ I will go before thee, and ##make##note:x[<<Isa 40:4; 42:16; Luke 3:5>>] the crooked places straight: I will break in pieces the gates of brass, and cut in sunder the bars of iron:
@ And I will give thee the treasures of darkness, and hidden riches of secret places, that thou mayest know that I, the LORD, ##which call##note:x[<<Exod 33:17; Isa 43:1; 48:15>>] __thee__ by thy name, __am__ the God of Israel.
@ For ##Jacob##note:x[<<Isa 41:8–9>>] my servant’s sake, and Israel mine elect, I have even called thee by thy name: I have surnamed thee, though thou hast not known me.

@ ¶ I __am__ ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 44:8; 46:9; Joel 2:27>>], and __there is__ none else, __there is__ no God beside me: I girded thee, though thou hast not known me:
@ ##That they may know from the rising of the sun, and from the west, that __there is__ none beside me. I __am__ the LORD, and __there is__ none else.##note:x[<<Ps 46:10; Isa 37:20; Mal 1:11>>]
@ I form the light, and create darkness: ##I make peace##note:x[<<Ps 75:7; Eccl 7:13–14; Amos 3:6>>], and create evil: I the LORD do all these __things__.
@ ##Drop down##note:x[<<Ps 72:6; Hos 10:12>>], ye heavens, from above, and let the skies pour down righteousness: ##let the earth##note:x[<<Isa 61:11>>] open, and let them bring forth salvation, and let righteousness spring up together; I the LORD have created it.
@ Woe ##unto him##note:x[<<Isa 64:8>>] that striveth with his Maker! __Let__ the potsherd __strive__ with the potsherds of the earth. ##Shall the clay##note:x[<<Isa 10:15; Rom 9:20–21>>] say to him that fashioneth it, What makest thou? or thy work, He hath no hands?
@ Woe unto him that saith unto __his__ father, What begettest thou? or to the woman, What hast thou brought forth?

@ Thus saith the LORD, ##the Holy One##note:x[<<Isa 48:17>>] of Israel, and his Maker, ##Ask##note:x[<<Jer 33:3; Ezek 36:37>>] me of things to come concerning my sons, and concerning the work of my hands command ye me.
@ I have ##made the earth##note:x[<<Isa 42:5>>], and created man upon it: I, __even__ ##my hands##note:x[<<Isa 44:24; Jer 27:5>>], have stretched out the heavens, and all their host have I commanded.
@ I have ##raised him##note:x[<<Isa 41:2, 25>>] up in righteousness, and I will ##direct##note:tn[or, make straight] all his ways: ##he shall build##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:22–23>>] my city, and he shall let go my captives, not for price nor reward, saith the LORD of hosts.
@ Thus saith the LORD, ##The labour##note:x[<<Isa 49:23>>] of Egypt, and merchandise of Ethiopia and of the Sabeans, men of stature, shall come over unto thee, and they shall be thine: they shall come after thee; ##in chains##note:x[<<Isa 49:23>>] they shall come over, and they shall fall down unto thee, they shall make supplication unto thee, __saying__, ##Surely##note:x[<<Jer 16:19>>] God __is__ in thee; and __there is__ none else, __there is__ no God.
@ Verily thou __art__ ##a God##note:x[<<Ps 44:24; Isa 8:17; 57:17>>] that hidest thyself, O God of Israel, the Saviour.
@ ##They shall be ashamed, and also confounded, all of them: they shall go to confusion together __that are__ makers of idols.##note:x[<<Isa 42:17; 44:9, 11>>]
@ __But__ Israel shall be saved in the LORD with an everlasting salvation: ##ye shall not##note:x[<<Ps 25:3; Isa 54:4; Rom 10:11>>] be ashamed nor confounded world without end.
@ For thus saith the LORD ##that created##note:x[<<Isa 42:5; Jer 10:12>>] the heavens; God himself that formed the earth and made it; he hath established it, ##he created##note:x[<<Ps 115:16>>] it not in vain, he formed it to be inhabited: I __am__ the LORD; and __there is__ none else.
@ I have not ##spoken##note:x[<<Isa 48:16>>] in secret, in a dark place of the earth: I said not unto the seed of Jacob, ##Seek##note:x[<<Amos 5:4>>] ye me in vain: I the LORD ##speak righteousness##note:x[<<Num 23:19–20>>], I declare things that are right.

@ ¶ Assemble ##yourselves##note:x[<<Isa 43:9>>] and come; draw near together, ye __that are__ ##escaped##note:x[<<Isa 4:2>>] of the nations: ##they##note:x[<<Isa 44:17–20>>] have no knowledge that set up the wood of their graven image, and pray unto a god __that__ cannot save.
@ Tell ye, and bring __them__ near; yea, let them take counsel together: ##who hath declared##note:x[<<Isa 46:9–10>>] this from ancient time? __who__ hath told it from that time? __have__ not I the LORD? ##and __there is__##note:x[<<Isa 45:5>>] no God else beside me; ##a just##note:x[<<Isa 43:11>>] God and a Saviour; __there is__ none beside me.
@ ##Look##note:x[<<Mic 7:7; John 6:40; Heb 12:2>>] unto me, and be ye saved, all the ends of the earth: for I __am__ God, and __there is__ none else.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Babylon’s Idols

@ ##Bel##note:x[<<Isa 21:9; Jer 50:2; 51:44>>] boweth down, Nebo stoopeth, their idols were upon the beasts, and upon the cattle: your carriages __were__ heavy loaden; __they are__ a burden to the weary __beast__.
@ They stoop, they bow down together; they could not deliver the burden, ##but##note:x[<<Judg 18:17–18; Jer 48:7>>] ##themselves##note:tn[Heb. their soul] are gone into captivity.

@ ¶ ##Hearken##note:x[<<Isa 51:1, 7>>] unto me, O house of Jacob, and all the remnant of the house of Israel, which are ##borne##note:x[<<Deut 1:31>>] __by me__ from the belly, which are carried from the womb:
@ And ##__even__ to __your__##note:x[<<Ps 92:14>>] old age I __am__ he; and ##__even__ to hoar##note:x[<<Ps 48:14; 71:18>>] hairs will I carry __you:__ I have made, and I will bear; even I will carry, and will deliver __you__.

@ ¶ ##To whom will ye liken me, and make __me__ equal, and compare me, that we may be like?##note:x[<<Ps 89:6; Isa 40:18, 25>>]
@ They ##lavish##note:x[<<Acts 17:29>>] gold out of the bag, and weigh silver in the balance, __and__ hire a goldsmith; and he maketh it a god: they fall down, yea, they worship.
@ They bear him upon the shoulder, ##they carry him##note:x[<<Jer 10:5>>], and set him in his place, and he standeth; from his place shall he not remove: yea, ##__one__ shall cry##note:x[<<Isa 45:20>>] unto him, yet can he not answer, nor save him out of his trouble.
@ Remember this, and shew yourselves men: bring __it__ again to mind, O ye transgressors.
@ Remember ##the former##note:x[<<Ps 111:4; Isa 42:9>>] things of old: for ##I __am__ God##note:x[<<Isa 45:21–22>>], and __there is__ none else; __I am__ God, and __there is__ none like me,
@ Declaring the end from the beginning, and from ancient times __the things__ that are not __yet__ done, saying, ##My counsel##note:x[<<Ps 33:11; Prov 19:21; Eph 1:9–11>>] shall stand, and I will do all my pleasure:
@ Calling a ravenous bird from the east, the man that executeth my counsel from a far country: yea, ##I have spoken##note:x[<<Num 23:19; Job 23:13; Isa 14:24–27>>] __it__, I will also bring it to pass; I have purposed __it__, I will also do it.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. of my counsel]

@ ¶ ##Hearken##note:x[<<Isa 46:3>>] unto me, ye stouthearted, ##that##note:x[<<Jer 2:5; Eph 2:13>>] __are__ far from righteousness:
@ I ##bring##note:x[<<Isa 51:5>>] near my righteousness; it shall not be far off, and my ##salvation##note:x[<<Isa 12:2; 62:11>>] shall not tarry: and I will place salvation in Zion for Israel my glory.

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Humiliation of Babylon

@ Come down, and sit in the dust, ##O virgin##note:x[<<Jer 46:11>>] ##daughter##note:x[<<Ps 137:8; Jer 51:33>>] of Babylon, sit on the ground: __there is__ no throne, O daughter of the Chaldeans: for thou shalt no more be called tender and delicate.
@ Take ##the millstones##note:x[<<Exod 11:5; Judg 16:21; Matt 24:41>>], and grind meal: uncover thy locks, make bare the leg, uncover the thigh, pass over the rivers.
@ Thy nakedness shall be uncovered, yea, thy shame shall be seen: ##I will take##note:x[<<Jer 51:20–24; Nah 3:5>>] vengeance, and I will not meet __thee as__ a man.
@ __As for__ our redeemer, the LORD of hosts __is__ his name, the Holy One of Israel.
@ Sit thou silent, and get thee into darkness, O daughter of the Chaldeans: for thou shalt no more be called, The lady of kingdoms.

@ ¶ I was ##wroth##note:x[<<2 Chr 28:9; Zech 1:15>>] with my people, I have polluted mine inheritance, and given them into thine hand: thou didst shew them no mercy; ##upon##note:x[<<Deut 28:50>>] the ancient hast thou very heavily laid thy yoke.

@ ¶ And ##thou saidst##note:x[<<Isa 47:5>>], I shall be a lady for ever: ##__so__ that##note:x[<<Deut 32:29>>] thou didst not lay these __things__ to thy heart, neither didst remember the latter end of it.
@ Therefore hear now this, __thou that art__ ##given##note:x[<<Isa 32:9; Zeph 2:15>>] to pleasures, that dwellest carelessly, that sayest in thine heart, ##I __am__##note:x[<<Isa 47:10>>], and none else beside me; I shall not sit __as__ a widow, neither shall I know the loss of children:
@ But these two __things__ shall come to thee ##in a moment##note:x[<<Ps 73:19; 1 Thess 5:3; Rev 18:8–10>>] in one day, the loss of children, and widowhood: they shall come upon thee in their perfection for the multitude of thy sorceries, __and__ for the great abundance of thine enchantments.

@ ¶ For thou hast trusted in thy wickedness: ##thou hast said##note:x[<<Isa 29:15>>], None seeth me. ##Thy wisdom##note:x[<<Isa 5:21; Rom 1:22>>] and thy knowledge, it hath perverted thee; and thou hast said in thine heart, I __am__, and none else beside me.note:tn[__perverted…__: or, caused thee to turn away]

@ ¶ Therefore shall evil come upon thee; thou shalt not know from whence it riseth: and mischief shall fall upon thee; ##thou shalt not be##note:x[<<1 Thess 5:3>>] able to ##put##note:tn[Heb. expiate] it off: and desolation shall come upon thee suddenly, __which__ thou shalt not know.note:tn[__from…__: Heb. the morning thereof]
@ ##Stand now with thine enchantments, and with the multitude of thy sorceries, wherein thou hast laboured from thy youth; if so be thou shalt be able to profit, if so be thou mayest prevail.##note:x[<<Acts 13:8–12>>]
@ Thou art wearied in the multitude of thy counsels. ##Let now##note:x[<<Isa 44:25>>] the ##astrologers##note:tn[Heb. viewers of the heavens], the stargazers, the monthly prognosticators, stand up, and save thee from __these things__ that shall come upon thee.note:tn[__the monthly…__: Heb. that give knowledge concerning the months]
@ Behold, ##they shall##note:x[<<Ezek 15:7; Nah 1:10; Mal 4:1>>] be as stubble; the fire shall burn them; they shall not deliver ##themselves##note:tn[Heb. their souls] from the power of the flame: __there shall__ not __be__ a coal to warm at, __nor__ fire to sit before it.
@ Thus shall they be unto thee with whom thou hast laboured, __even__ thy merchants, from thy youth: they shall wander every one to his quarter; none shall save thee.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Israel’s Stubbornness

@ Hear ye this, O house of Jacob, which are called by the name of Israel, and are ##come##note:x[<<Ps 68:26>>] forth out of the waters of Judah, which swear by the name of the LORD, and make mention of the God of Israel, __but__ ##not in truth##note:x[<<Jer 4:2>>], nor in righteousness.
@ For ##they call##note:x[<<Isa 52:1>>] themselves of the holy city, ##and stay##note:x[<<Isa 10:20; Mic 3:11>>] themselves upon the God of Israel; The LORD of hosts __is__ his name.
@ I have ##declared##note:x[<<Isa 41:22; 42:9>>] the former things from the beginning; and they went forth out of my mouth, ##and I##note:x[<<Josh 21:45>>] shewed them; I did __them__ suddenly, and they came to pass.
@ Because ##I knew##note:x[<<Zech 7:11–12>>] that thou __art__ ##obstinate##note:tn[Heb. hard], and ##thy neck##note:x[<<Exod 32:9; Acts 7:51>>] __is__ an iron sinew, and thy brow brass;
@ I have ##even##note:x[<<Isa 48:3; Luke 1:70>>] from the beginning declared __it__ to thee; before it came to pass I shewed __it__ thee: lest thou shouldest say, Mine idol hath done them, and my graven image, and my molten image, hath commanded them.
@ Thou hast heard, see all this; and will not ye declare __it__? I have ##shewed##note:x[<<Isa 42:9; Rom 16:25–26; Rev 4:1>>] thee new things from this time, even hidden things, and thou didst not know them.
@ They are created now, and not from the beginning; even before the day when thou heardest them not; lest thou shouldest say, Behold, I knew them.
@ Yea, thou heardest not; yea, thou knewest not; yea, from that time __that__ thine ear was not opened: for ##I knew##note:x[<<Hos 5:7>>] that thou wouldest deal very treacherously, and wast called ##a transgressor##note:x[<<Ps 58:3>>] from the womb.

@ ¶ note:x[<<Ps 79:9; 106:8; Isa 37:35>>]For my name’s sake will I defer mine anger, and for my praise will I refrain for thee, that I cut thee not off.
@ Behold, ##I have refined##note:x[<<Job 23:10; Prov 17:3; 1 Pet 4:12>>] thee, but not with silver; I have chosen thee in the furnace of affliction.note:tn[__with…__: or, for silver]
@ For mine own sake, __even__ for mine own sake, will I do __it:__ ##for how##note:x[<<Num 14:15–16; Ezek 20:9>>] should __my name__ be polluted? and ##I will not##note:x[<<Isa 42:8>>] give my glory unto another.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Deliverance Promised to Israel

@ ¶ Hearken unto me, O Jacob and Israel, my called; ##I __am__ he##note:x[<<Isa 41:4; Rev 1:8; 22:13>>]; I __am__ the first, I also __am__ the last.
@ Mine ##hand##note:x[<<Exod 20:11; Ps 102:25; Heb 1:10–12>>] also hath laid the foundation of the earth, and my right hand hath spanned the heavens: __when__ I call unto them, they stand up together.note:tn[__my…__: or, the palm of my right hand hath spread out]
@ All ye, ##assemble##note:x[<<Isa 43:9>>] yourselves, and hear; which among them hath declared these __things__? The LORD hath loved him: ##he will do##note:x[<<Isa 13:17–18; Jer 50:21–29>>] his pleasure on Babylon, and his arm __shall be on__ the Chaldeans.
@ ##I, __even__ I, have spoken; yea, I have called him: I have brought him, and he shall make his way prosperous.##note:x[<<Josh 1:8; Ps 45:4>>]

@ ¶ Come ye near unto me, hear ye this; ##I have not##note:x[<<Isa 45:19; John 18:20>>] spoken in secret from the beginning; from the time that it was, there __am__ I: and now ##the Lord GOD##note:x[<<John 20:21–22>>], and his Spirit, hath sent me.
@ Thus saith the LORD, thy Redeemer, the Holy One of Israel; I __am__ the LORD thy God ##which teacheth##note:x[<<Job 22:21–22; Ps 25:12>>] thee to profit, ##which leadeth##note:x[<<Ps 32:8>>] thee by the way __that__ thou shouldest go.
@ O ##that thou##note:x[<<Deut 5:29>>] hadst hearkened to my commandments! ##then had##note:x[<<Ps 119:165; Isa 66:12>>] thy peace been as a river, and thy righteousness as the waves of the sea:
@ Thy ##seed##note:x[<<Gen 22:17; Isa 10:22>>] also had been as the sand, and the offspring of thy bowels like the gravel thereof; his name should not have been cut off nor destroyed from before me.

@ ¶ Go ##ye forth##note:x[<<Jer 50:8; 51:45>>] of Babylon, flee ye from the Chaldeans, ##with a voice##note:x[<<Isa 52:9>>] of singing declare ye, tell this, utter it __even__ to the end of the earth; say ye, The LORD hath redeemed his servant Jacob.
@ And ##they thirsted##note:x[<<Isa 41:17–18; 43:19–20>>] not __when__ he led them through the deserts: ##he caused##note:x[<<Ps 105:41>>] the waters to flow out of the rock for them: he clave the rock also, and the waters gushed out.
@ ##__There is__ no peace, saith the LORD, unto the wicked.##note:x[<<Job 15:20–24; Isa 57:21; Luke 19:42>>]

==== The Servant and Light to the Gentiles

[.parallel]
(<<Acts 13:42–52>>)

@ Listen, O isles, unto me; and hearken, ye people, from far; ##The LORD##note:x[<<Isa 49:5; Jer 1:5; Gal 1:15>>] hath called me from the womb; from the bowels of my mother hath he made mention of my name.
@ And ##he hath made##note:x[<<Heb 4:12; Rev 1:16>>] my mouth like a sharp sword; in the shadow of his hand hath he hid me, and ##made me##note:x[<<Isa 50:4>>] a polished shaft; in his quiver hath he hid me;
@ ##And said unto me, Thou __art__ my servant, O Israel, in whom I will be glorified.##note:x[<<Isa 42:1; 44:23; 1 Pet 2:9>>]
@ Then I said, ##I have laboured##note:x[<<Gal 4:11>>] in vain, I have spent my strength for nought, and in vain: __yet__ surely my judgment __is__ with the LORD, and my work with my God.note:tn[__my work__: or, my reward]

@ ¶ And now, saith the LORD that formed me from the womb __to be__ his servant, to bring Jacob again to him, Though Israel be not gathered, ##yet##note:x[<<Matt 17:5; John 3:35>>] shall I be glorious in the eyes of the LORD, and my God shall be my strength.note:tn[__Though…__: or, That Israel may be gathered to him, and I may, etc]
@ And he said, It is a light thing that thou shouldest be my servant to raise up the tribes of Jacob, and to restore the ##preserved##note:tn[or, desolations] of Israel: ##I will also##note:x[<<Isa 42:6; Luke 2:32; Acts 13:47>>] give thee for a light to the Gentiles, that thou mayest be my salvation unto the end of the earth.note:tn[__It is…__: or, Art thou lighter than that thou shouldest, etc]

@ Thus saith the LORD, the Redeemer of Israel, __and__ his Holy One, ##to him whom man despiseth##note:x[<<Isa 53:3; Matt 26:67>>], to him whom the nation abhorreth, to a servant of rulers, Kings shall see and arise, princes also shall worship, because of the LORD that is faithful, __and__ the Holy One of Israel, ##and he##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:4>>] shall choose thee.note:tn[__whom man…__: or, that is despised in soul]
@ Thus saith the LORD, ##In an##note:x[<<Ps 69:13; 2 Cor 6:2>>] acceptable time have I heard thee, and in a day of salvation have I helped thee: and I will preserve thee, and ##give thee##note:x[<<Isa 42:6>>] for a covenant of the people, to ##establish##note:tn[or, raise up] the earth, to cause to inherit the desolate heritages;
@ That thou mayest say ##to the##note:x[<<Isa 42:7; 1 Pet 2:9>>] prisoners, Go forth; ##to them##note:x[<<Isa 42:16>>] that __are__ in darkness, Shew yourselves. They shall feed in the ways, and their pastures __shall be__ in all high places.
@ They ##shall not##note:x[<<Matt 5:6; Rev 7:16–17>>] hunger nor thirst; neither shall the heat nor sun smite them: for ##he that##note:x[<<Ezek 34:23>>] hath mercy on them shall lead them, even by the springs of water shall he guide them.
@ ##And I will make all my mountains a way, and my highways shall be exalted.##note:x[<<Isa 11:16>>]
@ Behold, ##these shall##note:x[<<Isa 43:5–6>>] come from far: and, lo, these from the north and from the west; and these from the land of Sinim.

@ ¶ Sing, ##O heavens##note:x[<<Ps 96:11–13; Isa 44:23>>]; and be joyful, O earth; and break forth into singing, O mountains: for ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 51:3>>] hath comforted his people, and will have mercy upon his afflicted.
@ But Zion said, ##The LORD##note:x[<<Isa 40:27>>] hath forsaken me, and my Lord hath forgotten me.
@ Can ##a woman##note:x[<<Ps 103:13; Matt 7:11>>] forget her sucking child, that she should not have compassion on the son of her womb? yea, they may forget, ##yet##note:x[<<Isa 44:21>>] will I not forget thee.note:tn[__that…__: Heb. from having compassion]
@ Behold, ##I have##note:x[<<Song 8:6; Hag 2:23>>] graven thee upon the palms of __my__ hands; ##thy walls##note:x[<<Isa 60:18>>] __are__ continually before me.
@ Thy ##children##note:x[<<Isa 62:5>>] shall make haste; ##thy destroyers##note:x[<<Isa 51:13>>] and they that made thee waste shall go forth of thee.

@ ¶ Lift up thine eyes round about, and behold: ##all these##note:x[<<Isa 54:1–3; 66:12–13>>] gather themselves together, __and__ come to thee. __As__ I live, saith the LORD, ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Isa 61:10>>] surely clothe thee with them all, as with an ornament, and bind them __on thee__, as a bride __doeth__.
@ For ##thy waste##note:x[<<Isa 54:1–2; Jer 30:18–19; Zech 10:10>>] and thy desolate places, and the land of thy destruction, shall even now be too narrow by reason of the inhabitants, and they that swallowed thee up shall be far away.

@ Then shalt thou say in thine heart, Who hath begotten me these, seeing I have lost my children, and am desolate, a captive, and removing to and fro? and who hath brought up these? Behold, I was left alone; these, where __had__ they __been__?
@ Thus saith the Lord GOD, Behold, I will lift up mine hand to the Gentiles, and set up my standard to the people: and they shall bring thy sons in __their__ ##arms##note:tn[Heb. bosom], and thy daughters shall be carried upon __their__ shoulders.
@ And ##kings##note:x[<<Isa 60:3, 16>>] shall be thy nursing fathers, and their ##queens##note:tn[Heb. princesses] thy nursing mothers: they shall ##bow##note:x[<<Isa 60:14>>] down to thee with __their__ face toward the earth, and lick up the dust of thy feet; and thou shalt know that I __am__ the LORD: for they shall not be ashamed that wait for me.note:tn[__nursing fathers__: Heb. nourishers]

@ ¶ Shall the ##prey##note:x[<<Ps 124:6–7; Matt 12:29; Luke 11:21–22>>] be taken from the mighty, or the lawful captive delivered?note:tn[__lawful…__: Heb. captivity of the just]
@ But thus saith the LORD, ##Even##note:x[<<Jer 50:33–34>>] the ##captives##note:tn[Heb. captivity] of the mighty shall be taken away, and the prey of the terrible shall be delivered: for ##I will contend##note:x[<<Isa 41:11–12; 54:15–17>>] with him that contendeth with thee, and I will save thy children.
@ And ##I will feed##note:x[<<Isa 9:20>>] them that oppress thee with their own flesh; and they shall be ##drunken##note:x[<<Rev 16:6>>] with their own blood, as with ##sweet##note:tn[or, new] wine: ##and all##note:x[<<Isa 41:14–20>>] flesh shall know that I the LORD __am__ thy Saviour and thy Redeemer, the mighty One of Jacob.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Israel’s Sin

@ Thus saith the LORD, Where __is__ ##the bill##note:x[<<Jer 3:8>>] of your mother’s divorcement, whom I have put away? ##or which##note:x[<<Deut 32:30>>] of my creditors __is it__ to whom I have sold you? Behold, ##for your iniquities##note:x[<<Jer 3:8>>] have ye sold yourselves, and for your transgressions is your mother put away.
@ Wherefore, ##when I came##note:x[<<Isa 66:4>>], __was there__ no man? when I called, __was there__ none to answer? ##Is my##note:x[<<Num 11:23; Isa 59:1>>] hand shortened at all, that it cannot redeem? or have I no power to deliver? behold, at my rebuke I dry up the sea, I make the rivers a wilderness: their fish stinketh, because __there is__ no water, and dieth for thirst.
@ ##I clothe the heavens with blackness, and I make sackcloth their covering.##note:x[<<Ps 18:11–12; Rev 6:12>>]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Servant’s Obedience

[.parallel]
(<<Matthew 27:27–31; Mark 15:16–20; Luke 22:63–65; John 19:1–15>>)

@ The Lord ##GOD##note:x[<<Exod 4:11–12; Jer 1:9; Luke 21:15>>] hath given me the tongue of the learned, that I should know how to speak a word in season to __him that is__ weary: he wakeneth morning by morning, he wakeneth mine ear to hear as the learned.

@ ¶ ##The Lord GOD hath opened mine ear, and I was not rebellious, neither turned away back.##note:x[<<John 14:31; Phil 2:8; Heb 5:8>>]
@ I ##gave##note:x[<<Matt 26:67; Mark 14:65; 15:19>>] my back to the smiters, and my cheeks to them that plucked off the hair: I hid not my face from shame and spitting.

@ ¶ For ##the Lord##note:x[<<John 16:33; Heb 13:6>>] GOD will help me; therefore shall I not be confounded: therefore have ##I set##note:x[<<Ezek 3:8–9>>] my face like a flint, and I know that I shall not be ashamed.
@ __He is__ ##near that##note:x[<<Rom 8:32–34>>] justifieth me; who will contend with me? ##let us##note:x[<<Isa 41:1>>] stand together: who __is__ ##mine adversary##note:x[<<Rev 12:10>>]? let him come near to me.note:tn[__mine…__: Heb. the master of my cause?]
@ Behold, the Lord GOD will help me; who __is__ he __that__ shall condemn me? lo, ##they all##note:x[<<Job 13:28; Ps 102:26; Heb 1:11–12>>] shall wax old as a garment; the moth shall eat them up.

@ ¶ Who __is__ among you that feareth the LORD, that obeyeth the voice of his servant, ##that walketh##note:x[<<John 12:46>>] __in__ darkness, and hath no light? ##let##note:x[<<Isa 26:3–4; Mic 7:7–9>>] him trust in the name of the LORD, and stay upon his God.
@ Behold, ##all ye##note:x[<<Jer 17:5–7; Rom 1:21–22; 10:3>>] that kindle a fire, that compass __yourselves__ about with sparks: walk in the light of your fire, and in the sparks __that__ ye have kindled. This shall ye have of mine hand; ye shall lie down in sorrow.

=== @51

[discrete.pericope]
==== Salvation for Zion

@ ##Hearken##note:x[<<Isa 51:7>>] to me, ##ye that follow##note:x[<<Ps 94:15; Isa 51:7>>] after righteousness, ye that seek the LORD: look unto the rock __whence__ ye are hewn, and to the hole of the pit __whence__ ye are digged.
@ Look ##unto Abraham##note:x[<<Rom 4:1–5>>] your father, and unto Sarah __that__ bare you: ##for##note:x[<<Ezek 33:24; Gal 3:9–14>>] I called him alone, and blessed him, and increased him.
@ For ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 49:13>>] shall comfort Zion: he will comfort ##all##note:x[<<Isa 52:9>>] her waste places; and he will make her wilderness like Eden, and her desert ##like the##note:x[<<Joel 2:3>>] garden of the LORD; joy and gladness shall be found therein, thanksgiving, and the voice of melody.

@ ¶ Hearken unto me, my people; and give ear unto me, ##O my##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:9>>] nation: for ##a law##note:x[<<Isa 2:3; 1 Cor 9:21>>] shall proceed from me, and I will make my judgment to rest for a light of the people.
@ My ##righteousness##note:x[<<Isa 46:13; Rom 10:6–10>>] __is__ near; ##my salvation##note:x[<<Rom 10:17–18>>] is gone forth, and mine arms shall judge the people; the isles shall wait upon me, and on mine arm shall they trust.
@ Lift up your eyes to ##the heavens##note:x[<<Isa 34:4; Matt 24:35; Rev 6:12–14>>], and look upon the earth beneath: for the heavens shall vanish away like smoke, and the earth shall wax old like a garment, and they that dwell therein shall die in like manner: but my salvation shall be for ever, and my righteousness shall not be abolished.

@ ¶ Hearken unto me, ye that know righteousness, the people ##in whose##note:x[<<Ps 37:31>>] heart __is__ my law; ##fear##note:x[<<Ezek 2:6; Matt 5:11>>] ye not the reproach of men, neither be ye afraid of their revilings.
@ For ##the moth##note:x[<<Isa 50:9; 66:24>>] shall eat them up like a garment, and the worm shall eat them like wool: but ##my righteousness##note:x[<<Isa 51:6>>] shall be for ever, and my salvation from generation to generation.

@ ¶ Awake, awake, ##put##note:x[<<Ps 74:13–14; Isa 52:1>>] on strength, O arm of the LORD; awake, as in the ancient days, in the generations of old. __Art__ thou not it that hath cut Rahab, __and__ wounded ##the dragon##note:x[<<Ps 74:13–14>>]?
@ __Art__ thou not it which hath ##dried##note:x[<<Isa 43:16; 50:2; 63:11–12>>] the sea, the waters of the great deep; that hath made the depths of the sea a way for the ransomed to pass over?
@ Therefore the redeemed of the LORD shall return, and come with singing unto Zion; and everlasting joy __shall be__ upon their head: they shall obtain gladness and joy; ##__and__ sorrow##note:x[<<Isa 25:8; Rev 7:17; 21:4>>] and mourning shall flee away.
@ I, __even__ I, __am__ he that comforteth you: who __art__ thou, ##that thou##note:x[<<Ps 118:6; Isa 2:22>>] shouldest be afraid of a man __that__ shall die, and of the son of ##man __which__##note:x[<<1 Pet 1:24>>] shall be made __as__ grass;
@ And ##forgettest##note:x[<<Isa 17:10>>] the LORD thy maker, ##that hath##note:x[<<Isa 40:22; 45:12>>] stretched forth the heavens, and laid the foundations of the earth; and hast feared continually every day because of the fury of the oppressor, as if he were ready to destroy? and where __is__ the fury of the oppressor?note:tn[__were…__: or, made himself ready]
@ The ##captive##note:x[<<Isa 48:20; 52:2>>] exile hasteneth that he may be loosed, and that he should not ##die##note:x[<<Zech 9:11>>] in the pit, nor that his bread should fail.
@ But I __am__ ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 54:5>>] thy God, ##that divided##note:x[<<Jer 31:35; Amos 9:5–6>>] the sea, whose waves roared: The LORD of hosts __is__ his name.
@ And ##I have put##note:x[<<Isa 59:21; John 17:8>>] my words in thy mouth, and ##I have covered##note:x[<<Isa 49:2>>] thee in the shadow of mine hand, that I may plant the heavens, and lay the foundations of the earth, and say unto Zion, Thou __art__ my people.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Fury Removed

@ ¶ ##Awake##note:x[<<Isa 52:1; 1 Cor 15:34>>], awake, stand up, O Jerusalem, ##which hast##note:x[<<Rev 14:10>>] drunk at the hand of the LORD the cup of his fury; thou hast drunken the dregs of the cup of trembling, __and__ wrung __them__ out.
@ __There is__ ##none##note:x[<<Ps 88:18; Isa 49:21>>] to guide her among all the sons __whom__ she hath brought forth; neither __is there any__ that taketh her by the hand of all the sons __that__ she hath brought up.
@ These ##two __things__##note:x[<<Isa 47:9>>] are come unto thee; ##who shall##note:x[<<Amos 7:2>>] be sorry for thee? desolation, and ##destruction##note:tn[Heb. breaking], and the famine, and the sword: ##by whom##note:x[<<Amos 7:2>>] shall I comfort thee?note:tn[__are…__: Heb. happened]
@ Thy ##sons##note:x[<<Lam 2:11–12>>] have fainted, they lie at the head of all the streets, as ##a wild##note:x[<<Rev 16:9–11>>] bull in a net: they are full of the fury of the LORD, the rebuke of thy God.

@ ¶ Therefore hear now this, thou afflicted, and drunken, but not with wine:
@ Thus saith thy Lord the LORD, and thy God __that__ ##pleadeth##note:x[<<Prov 22:23; Jer 50:34>>] the cause of his people, Behold, ##I have##note:x[<<Isa 51:17>>] taken out of thine hand the cup of trembling, __even__ the dregs of the cup of my fury; thou shalt no more drink it again:
@ But ##I will##note:x[<<Zech 12:2>>] put it into the hand of them that afflict thee; which have said to thy soul, ##Bow##note:x[<<Josh 10:24>>] down, that we may go over: and thou hast laid thy body as the ground, and as the street, to them that went over.

=== @52

[discrete.pericope]
==== Deliverance for Jerusalem

@ ##Awake##note:x[<<Isa 51:17>>], awake; put on thy strength, O Zion; put on thy beautiful garments, O Jerusalem, ##the holy##note:x[<<Rev 21:27>>] city: for henceforth ##there shall##note:x[<<Rev 21:27>>] no more come into thee the uncircumcised and the unclean.
@ ##Shake##note:x[<<Jer 51:45, 50>>] thyself from the dust; arise, __and__ sit down, O Jerusalem: ##loose##note:x[<<Isa 51:14>>] thyself from the bands of thy neck, O captive daughter of Zion.
@ ##For thus saith the LORD, Ye have sold yourselves for nought; and ye shall be redeemed without money.##note:x[<<Ps 44:12; Isa 45:13; 1 Pet 1:18>>]
@ For thus saith the Lord GOD, ##My people##note:x[<<Gen 46:6>>] went down aforetime into Egypt to sojourn there; and the Assyrian oppressed them ##without##note:x[<<Ps 69:4>>] cause.
@ Now therefore, what have I here, saith the LORD, that my people is taken away for nought? they that rule over them make them to howl, saith the LORD; and ##my name##note:x[<<Rom 2:24>>] continually every day __is__ blasphemed.
@ Therefore ##my people##note:x[<<Ezek 20:44; 37:13–14; Zech 10:9–12>>] shall know my name: therefore __they shall know__ in that day that I __am__ he that doth speak: behold, __it is__ I.

@ ¶ ##How beautiful##note:x[<<Isa 61:1–3; Nah 1:15; Rom 10:12–15>>] upon the mountains are the feet of him that bringeth good tidings, that publisheth peace; that bringeth good tidings of good, that publisheth salvation; that saith unto Zion, Thy God reigneth!
@ ##Thy##note:x[<<Isa 62:6; Ezek 33:7>>] watchmen shall lift up the voice; with the voice together shall they sing: for they shall ##see##note:x[<<Jer 32:39>>] eye to eye, when the LORD shall bring again Zion.

@ ¶ ##Break##note:x[<<Isa 14:7; 42:10–11>>] forth into joy, sing together, ##ye waste##note:x[<<Isa 51:3>>] places of Jerusalem: for the LORD hath comforted his people, he hath redeemed Jerusalem.
@ The LORD hath ##made##note:x[<<Isa 51:9>>] bare his holy arm in the eyes of ##all##note:x[<<Luke 3:6; Rev 14:6>>] the nations; and all the ends of the earth shall see the salvation of our God.

@ ¶ ##Depart ye##note:x[<<Isa 48:20; Zech 2:6–7; 2 Cor 6:17>>], depart ye, go ye out from thence, touch no unclean __thing;__ go ye out of the midst of her; be ye clean, that bear the vessels of the LORD.
@ ##For##note:x[<<Exod 14:19–20; Deut 20:4>>] ye shall not go out with haste, nor go by flight: for the LORD will go before you; and ##the God##note:x[<<Isa 58:8>>] of Israel __will be__ your rereward.note:tn[__be…__: Heb. gather you up]

@ ¶ Behold, ##my servant##note:x[<<Isa 42:1; Phil 2:7–8>>] shall deal prudently, ##he shall##note:x[<<Matt 28:18>>] be exalted and extolled, and be very high.note:tn[__deal…__: or, prosper]

==== The Suffering Servant

[.parallel]
(<<Acts 8:26–40; 1 Peter 2:21–25>>)

@ ##Who##note:x[<<John 12:38; Rom 10:16–17>>] hath believed our ##report##note:tn[or, doctrine?: Heb. hearing?]? and to whom is ##the##note:x[<<1 Cor 1:18>>] arm of the LORD revealed?
@ For ##he shall grow##note:x[<<Isa 11:1; Phil 2:6–7>>] up before him as a tender plant, and as a root out of a dry ground: ##he hath no##note:x[<<Isa 52:14>>] form nor comeliness; and when we shall see him, __there is__ no beauty that we should desire him.
@ He is ##despised##note:x[<<Matt 26:67; Heb 12:2–3>>] and rejected of men; a man of sorrows, and acquainted with grief: and we hid as it were __our__ faces from him; he was despised, and ##we esteemed##note:x[<<John 1:10–11>>] him not.note:tn[__we hid…__: or, he hid as it were his face from us: Heb. as an hiding of faces from him, or, from us]

@ ¶ Surely ##he hath##note:x[<<Matt 8:17; Gal 3:13; 1 Pet 2:24>>] borne our griefs, and carried our sorrows: yet we did esteem him stricken, smitten of God, and afflicted.
@ ##But he __was__##note:x[<<Matt 20:28; 1 Pet 3:18>>] ##wounded##note:tn[or, tormented] for our transgressions, __he was__ bruised for our iniquities: ##the chastisement##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:24>>] of our peace __was__ upon him; and with his ##stripes##note:tn[Heb. bruise] we are healed.
@ ##All we##note:x[<<Ps 119:176; 1 Pet 2:25>>] like sheep have gone astray; we have turned every one to ##his own##note:x[<<1 Pet 3:18>>] way; and the LORD hath laid on him the iniquity of us all.note:tn[__laid…__: Heb. made the iniquity of us all to meet on him]
@ He was oppressed, and he was afflicted, ##yet##note:x[<<Matt 26:63; 1 Pet 2:23>>] he opened not his mouth: ##he is##note:x[<<Acts 8:32–33>>] brought as a lamb to the slaughter, and as a sheep before her shearers is dumb, so he openeth not his mouth.

(<<Matthew 27:57–61; Mark 15:42–47; Luke 23:50–56; John 19:38–42>>)

@ And he ##made##note:x[<<Matt 27:57–60; Mark 15:43–46>>] his grave with the wicked, and with the rich in his ##death##note:tn[Heb. deaths]; because he had done no violence, neither __was any__ ##deceit##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:22>>] in his mouth.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Future Blessings for Zion

@ Sing, ##O barren##note:x[<<Isa 62:4; Gal 4:27>>], thou __that__ didst not bear; break forth into singing, and cry aloud, thou __that__ didst not travail with child: ##for more##note:x[<<1 Sam 2:5>>] __are__ the children of the desolate than the children of the married wife, saith the LORD.
@ ##Enlarge the place of thy tent, and let them stretch forth the curtains of thine habitations: spare not, lengthen thy cords, and strengthen thy stakes;##note:x[<<Isa 33:20; 49:19–20; Jer 10:20>>]
@ For ##thou shalt##note:x[<<Ps 72:8–11; Isa 43:5–6; 60:3–11>>] break forth on the right hand and on the left; and thy seed shall inherit the Gentiles, and make the desolate cities to be inhabited.
@ ##Fear not##note:x[<<Isa 41:10; 61:7; 1 Pet 2:6>>]; for thou shalt not be ashamed: neither be thou confounded; for thou shalt not be put to shame: for thou shalt forget the shame of thy youth, and shalt not remember the reproach of thy widowhood any more.
@ For note:x[<<Hos 2:19–20; 2 Cor 11:2–3; Eph 5:25–27>>]thy ##Maker##note:tn[Heb. Makers] __is__ thine husband; the LORD of hosts __is__ his name; and thy Redeemer the Holy One of Israel; The God of the whole earth shall he be called.

@ For the LORD hath called thee as ##a woman##note:x[<<Isa 62:4; Hos 2:1–2, 14–15>>] forsaken and grieved in spirit, and a wife of youth, when thou wast refused, saith thy God.
@ For ##a small##note:x[<<Ps 30:5; Isa 26:20; 2 Pet 3:8>>] moment have I forsaken thee; but with great mercies will I gather thee.
@ In a little wrath ##I hid##note:x[<<Ezek 39:23–24>>] my face from thee for a moment; ##but##note:x[<<Jer 31:3; 1 Tim 1:16>>] with everlasting kindness will I have mercy on thee, saith the LORD thy Redeemer.
@ ##For this __is as__ the waters of Noah unto me: for __as__ I have sworn that the waters of Noah should no more go over the earth; so have I sworn that I would not be wroth with thee, nor rebuke thee.##note:x[<<Gen 8:21; Isa 12:1>>]
@ For ##the mountains##note:x[<<Ps 46:2>>] shall depart, and the hills be removed; but my kindness shall not depart from thee, neither shall ##the covenant##note:x[<<Ps 89:33–34>>] of my peace be removed, saith the LORD ##that hath##note:x[<<Eph 2:4–5>>] mercy on thee.

@ ¶ O ##thou afflicted##note:x[<<Jer 30:17; Acts 14:22>>], tossed with tempest, __and__ not comforted, behold, ##I will lay##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:4–6>>] thy stones with fair colours, and lay thy foundations with sapphires.
@ And I will make thy windows of agates, and thy gates of carbuncles, and all thy borders of pleasant stones.
@ And ##all##note:x[<<John 6:45>>] thy children __shall be__ taught of the LORD; and ##great##note:x[<<Ps 119:165; Phil 4:7>>] __shall be__ the peace of thy children.
@ In righteousness shalt thou be established: ##thou shalt be##note:x[<<Zech 9:8>>] far from oppression; ##for thou##note:x[<<Prov 3:25–26; Jer 30:10>>] shalt not fear: and from terror; for it shall not come near thee.
@ Behold, they shall surely gather together, __but__ not by me: whosoever shall gather together against thee ##shall fall##note:x[<<Ps 37:12–13; Zech 2:8; 12:9>>] for thy sake.
@ Behold, ##I have##note:x[<<Exod 9:16; Prov 16:4; Isa 46:11>>] created the smith that bloweth the coals in the fire, and that bringeth forth an instrument for his work; and I have created the waster to destroy.

@ ¶ No ##weapon##note:x[<<Isa 54:15; John 10:28–30; Rom 8:28–39>>] that is formed against thee shall prosper; and every tongue __that__ shall rise against thee in judgment thou shalt condemn. This __is__ the heritage of the servants of the LORD, and their righteousness __is__ of me, saith the LORD.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Invitation to the Needy

@ Ho, ##every##note:x[<<John 7:37–38; Rev 21:6; 22:17>>] one that thirsteth, come ye to the waters, and he that hath no money; come ye, buy, and eat; yea, come, buy wine and milk without money and without price.
@ Wherefore ##do ye##note:x[<<Jer 2:13>>] ##spend##note:tn[Heb. weigh] money for __that which is__ not bread? and your labour for __that which__ satisfieth not? hearken diligently unto me, and ##eat##note:x[<<Ps 22:26; John 6:48–58>>] ye __that which is__ good, and let your soul delight itself in fatness.
@ ##Incline##note:x[<<Prov 4:20>>] your ear, and ##come##note:x[<<Matt 11:28>>] unto me: hear, and your soul shall live; ##and I will##note:x[<<Jer 32:40>>] make an everlasting covenant with you, __even__ the sure mercies of David.

@ Let ##the wicked##note:x[<<Prov 28:13; Ezek 18:21–23; Jas 4:8–10>>] forsake his way, and the unrighteous man his thoughts: and let him return unto the LORD, and he will have mercy upon him; and to our God, for he will abundantly pardon.note:tn[__the unrighteous…__: Heb. the man of iniquity]note:tn[__abundantly…__: Heb. multiply to pardon]

@ ¶ ##For my thoughts __are__ not your thoughts, neither __are__ your ways my ways, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Ps 40:5; 92:5; Hos 14:9>>]
@ ##For __as__ the heavens are higher than the earth, so are my ways higher than your ways, and my thoughts than your thoughts.##note:x[<<Ps 103:11; Matt 11:25; Rom 11:31–36>>]
@ For ##as the rain##note:x[<<Deut 32:2; Isa 30:23>>] cometh down, and the snow from heaven, and returneth not thither, but watereth the earth, and maketh it bring forth and bud, that it may ##give##note:x[<<2 Cor 9:9–11>>] seed to the sower, and bread to the eater:
@ So ##shall my##note:x[<<Matt 24:35; Rom 10:17>>] word be that goeth forth out of my mouth: it shall not return unto me void, but ##it shall accomplish##note:x[<<Isa 46:10>>] that which I please, and it shall prosper __in the thing__ whereto I sent it.
@ For ##ye shall##note:x[<<Rom 15:13; Gal 5:22>>] go out with joy, and be led forth with peace: ##the mountains##note:x[<<Isa 44:23>>] and the hills shall break forth before you into singing, and all the trees of the field shall clap __their__ hands.
@ Instead ##of the thorn##note:x[<<Isa 41:19>>] shall come up the fir tree, and instead of the brier shall come up the myrtle tree: and it shall be to the LORD ##for a##note:x[<<Eph 3:20–21; 1 Pet 2:9–10>>] name, for an everlasting sign __that__ shall not be cut off.

=== @56

[discrete.pericope]
==== Salvation for Foreigners

@ Thus saith the LORD, ##Keep##note:x[<<Isa 1:16–19>>] ye ##judgment##note:tn[or, equity], and do justice: ##for##note:x[<<Ps 85:9; Rom 13:11–14>>] my salvation __is__ near to come, and my righteousness to be revealed.
@ ##Blessed##note:x[<<Ps 112:1>>] __is__ the man __that__ doeth this, and the son of man __that__ layeth hold on it; that ##keepeth the##note:x[<<Exod 31:13–16; Isa 58:13>>] sabbath from polluting it, and keepeth his hand from doing any evil.

@ ¶ ##Neither##note:x[<<Matt 19:12–30>>] let ##the son##note:x[<<Acts 10:34>>] of the stranger, that hath ##joined##note:x[<<1 Cor 6:17>>] himself to the LORD, speak, saying, The LORD hath utterly separated me from his people: neither let the eunuch say, Behold, I __am__ a dry tree.
@ For thus saith the LORD unto the eunuchs that keep my sabbaths, and ##choose##note:x[<<Josh 24:15; Ps 119:111; Luke 10:42>>] __the things__ that please me, and take hold of my covenant;
@ Even unto them will I give in mine house and within my walls a place ##and a##note:x[<<1 John 3:1; Rev 3:12>>] name better than of sons and of daughters: I will give them an everlasting name, ##that shall##note:x[<<Rev 3:5>>] not be cut off.
@ Also the sons of the stranger, that ##join##note:x[<<Jer 50:5>>] themselves to the LORD, to serve him, and to love the name of the LORD, to be his servants, ##every##note:x[<<Isa 56:2; 58:13>>] one that keepeth the sabbath from polluting it, and taketh hold of my covenant;
@ Even ##them will##note:x[<<Isa 2:2–3; Mal 1:11>>] I bring to my holy mountain, and make them joyful in my house of prayer: their burnt offerings and their sacrifices __shall be__ accepted upon mine altar; ##for mine##note:x[<<Mark 11:17>>] house shall be called an house of prayer for all people.
@ The Lord GOD ##which##note:x[<<Isa 54:7>>] gathereth the outcasts of Israel saith, ##Yet##note:x[<<Isa 60:3–11; John 10:16>>] will I gather __others__ to him, beside those that are gathered unto him.note:tn[__beside…__: Heb. to his gathered]

@ ¶ ##All ye beasts of the field, come to devour, __yea__, all ye beasts in the forest.##note:x[<<Jer 12:9>>]
@ His watchmen ##__are__ blind##note:x[<<Isa 29:10; Matt 15:14>>]: they are all ignorant, ##they __are__ all dumb##note:x[<<Ezek 33:6>>] dogs, they cannot bark; ##sleeping##note:tn[or, dreaming, or, talking in their sleep], lying down, loving to slumber.
@ ##Yea, __they are__##note:x[<<Ezek 13:19; 34:2–3; Mic 3:11>>] ##greedy##note:tn[Heb. strong of appetite] dogs __which__ can never have enough, and they __are__ shepherds __that__ cannot understand: they all look to their own way, every one for his gain, from his quarter.note:tn[__can…__: Heb. know not to be satisfied]

[discrete.pericope]
==== The Blessed Death of the Righteous

@ ##The righteous##note:x[<<2 Kgs 22:20; 2 Chr 34:28>>] perisheth, and no man layeth __it__ to heart: and ##merciful men##note:x[<<Ps 12:1>>] __are__ taken away, none considering that the righteous is taken away from the evil __to come__.note:tn[__merciful…__: Heb. men of kindness, or, godliness]note:tn[__from…__: or, from that which is evil]

[discrete.pericope]
==== God Condemns Idolatry

@ ¶ But draw near hither, ye ##sons##note:x[<<Hos 1:2; Matt 16:4; 1 John 3:10>>] of the sorceress, the seed of the adulterer and the whore.

@ Enflaming yourselves ##with idols##note:x[<<Isa 1:29>>] ##under##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:3–4>>] every green tree, ##slaying##note:x[<<2 Kgs 16:3>>] the children in the valleys under the clifts of the rocks?note:tn[__with…__: or, among the oaks]
@ Among ##the smooth##note:x[<<Jer 3:9>>] __stones__ of the stream __is__ thy portion; they, they __are__ thy lot: even ##to them##note:x[<<Jer 7:18>>] hast thou poured a drink offering, thou hast offered a meat offering. Should I receive comfort in these?
@ ##Upon a lofty and high mountain hast thou set thy bed: even thither wentest thou up to offer sacrifice.##note:x[<<Jer 2:20; Ezek 16:16; 23:41>>]
@ Behind the doors also and the posts hast thou set up thy remembrance: for thou hast discovered __thyself to another__ than me, and art gone up; thou hast enlarged thy bed, and made thee __a covenant__ with them; thou lovedst their bed where thou sawest __it__.note:tn[__made…__: or, hewed it for thyself larger than theirs]note:tn[__where…__: or, thou providedst room]
@ And ##thou wentest to the king##note:x[<<Ezek 23:16>>] with ointment, and didst increase thy perfumes, and didst send thy messengers far off, and didst debase __thyself even__ unto hell.note:tn[__thou…__: or, thou respectedst]
@ Thou art wearied in the greatness of thy way; __yet__ saidst thou not, ##There is##note:x[<<Jer 2:25>>] no hope: thou hast found the ##life##note:tn[or, living] of thine hand; therefore thou wast not grieved.
@ And ##of whom##note:x[<<Prov 29:25; Isa 51:12–13>>] hast thou been afraid or feared, that thou hast lied, and hast not remembered me, nor laid __it__ to thy heart? ##have not##note:x[<<Ps 50:21>>] I held my peace even of old, and thou fearest me not?

@ And shall say, ##Cast##note:x[<<Isa 35:8; 62:10; Luke 3:5–6>>] ye up, cast ye up, prepare the way, take up the stumblingblock out of the way of my people.

@ For ##I will not##note:x[<<Ps 85:5; Jer 10:24; Mic 7:18>>] contend for ever, neither will I be always wroth: for the spirit should fail before me, and the souls __which__ I have made.

[discrete.pericope]
==== True Fasts and Sabbaths

@ Cry ##aloud##note:tn[Heb. with the throat], ##spare##note:x[<<Acts 20:26–27; Titus 2:15>>] not, lift up thy voice ##like##note:x[<<Hos 8:1>>] a trumpet, and shew my people their transgression, and the house of Jacob their sins.
@ Yet ##they seek##note:x[<<Isa 29:13; Matt 15:7–9; Titus 1:16>>] me daily, and delight to know my ways, as a nation that did righteousness, and forsook not the ordinance of their God: they ask of me the ordinances of justice; they take delight in approaching to God.

@ ¶ Wherefore ##have we fasted##note:x[<<Mal 3:14; Luke 18:9–12>>], __say they__, and thou seest not? __wherefore__ have we afflicted our soul, and thou takest no knowledge? Behold, in the day of your fast ye find pleasure, and ##exact##note:x[<<Prov 28:9>>] all your ##labours##note:tn[or, things wherewith ye grieve others: Heb. griefs].
@ Behold, ##ye fast##note:x[<<1 Kgs 21:9–13>>] for strife and debate, and to smite with the fist of wickedness: ye shall not fast as __ye do this__ day, to make your voice to be heard on high.note:tn[__ye shall…__: or, ye fast not as this day]
@ Is ##it such##note:x[<<Dan 9:3–19; Zech 7:5>>] a fast that I have chosen? a day for a man to afflict his soul? __is it__ to bow down his head as a bulrush, and to spread sackcloth and ashes __under him__? wilt thou call this a fast, and ##an acceptable##note:x[<<Isa 49:8>>] day to the LORD?note:tn[__a day…__: or, to afflict his soul for a day?]
@ __Is__ not this the fast that I have chosen? ##to loose##note:x[<<Neh 5:10–12; Jer 34:8–11>>] the bands of wickedness, to undo the ##heavy burdens##note:x[<<1 Tim 6:1>>], and to let the ##oppressed##note:tn[Heb. broken] go free, and that ye break every yoke?note:tn[__the heavy…__: Heb. the bundles of the yoke]
@ __Is it__ not ##to deal##note:x[<<Ezek 18:7; Matt 25:35–40>>] thy bread to the hungry, and that thou bring the poor that are cast out to thy house? when thou seest ##the naked##note:x[<<Matt 25:35–45>>], that thou cover him; and that thou hide not thyself from thine own flesh?note:tn[__cast…__: or, afflicted]

@ ¶ Then shall ##thy light##note:x[<<Ps 37:6>>] break forth as the morning, ##and thine##note:x[<<Jer 33:6>>] health shall spring forth speedily: and thy righteousness shall go before thee; ##the glory##note:x[<<Isa 52:12>>] of the LORD shall be thy rereward.note:tn[__be…__: Heb. gather thee up]
@ Then ##shalt thou##note:x[<<Ps 50:15; Isa 65:24; Jer 29:12–13>>] call, and the LORD shall answer; thou shalt cry, and he shall say, Here I __am__. If thou take away from the midst of thee the yoke, the putting forth of the finger, and speaking vanity;
@ And __if__ ##thou draw##note:x[<<Prov 11:24–25; Isa 58:7>>] out thy soul to the hungry, and satisfy the afflicted soul; ##then##note:x[<<Ps 37:6>>] shall thy light rise in obscurity, and thy darkness __be__ as the noonday:
@ And ##the LORD##note:x[<<Ps 32:8; Isa 49:10>>] shall guide thee continually, and satisfy thy soul in ##drought##note:tn[Heb. droughts], and make fat thy bones: and thou shalt ##be like##note:x[<<Jer 31:12>>] a watered garden, and like a spring of water, whose waters ##fail##note:tn[Heb. lie, or, deceive] not.
@ And __they that shall be__ of thee shall ##build##note:x[<<Isa 61:4; Ezek 36:33; Amos 9:14>>] the old waste places: thou shalt raise up the foundations of many generations; and thou shalt be called, The repairer of the breach, The restorer of paths to dwell in.

@ ¶ If thou ##turn##note:x[<<Isa 56:2–6; Jer 17:21–27>>] away thy foot from the sabbath, __from__ doing thy pleasure on my holy day; and ##call##note:x[<<Ps 92:1–2>>] the sabbath a delight, the holy of the LORD, honourable; and shalt honour him, not doing thine own ways, nor finding thine own pleasure, nor speaking __thine own__ words:
@ Then shalt thou ##delight##note:x[<<Job 22:26>>] thyself in the LORD; and I will cause thee ##to ride##note:x[<<Deut 32:13>>] upon the high places of the earth, ##and feed##note:x[<<Isa 1:19>>] thee with the heritage of Jacob thy father: for the mouth of the LORD hath spoken __it__.

==== Sin Separates Us from God

[.parallel]
(<<Psalms 14:1–7; Psalms 53:1–6; Romans 3:9–20>>)

@ note:x[<<Num 11:23; Isa 50:2; Jer 32:17>>]Behold, the LORD’s hand is not shortened, that it cannot save; neither his ear heavy, that it cannot hear:
@ But ##your iniquities##note:x[<<Prov 15:29; Jer 5:25>>] have separated between you and your God, and your sins have ##hid##note:x[<<Mic 3:4>>] __his__ face from you, that he will not hear.note:tn[__have hid__: or, have made him hide]
@ For ##your hands##note:x[<<Isa 1:15; Jer 2:34>>] are defiled with blood, and your fingers with iniquity; ##your lips##note:x[<<Ezek 13:8>>] have spoken lies, your tongue hath muttered perverseness.
@ None ##calleth##note:x[<<Mic 7:2–5>>] for justice, nor __any__ pleadeth for truth: they trust in vanity, and speak lies; ##they conceive##note:x[<<Job 15:35; Jas 1:15>>] mischief, and bring forth iniquity.
@ They hatch note:x[<<Prov 23:32; Isa 14:29>>]##cockatrice’##note:tn[or, adder’s] eggs, and weave the spider’s web: he that eateth of their eggs dieth, and that which is ##crushed breaketh out into a viper##note:x[<<Matt 3:7>>].note:tn[__crushed…__: or, sprinkled is as if there brake out a viper]
@ Their webs shall not become garments, neither shall they cover themselves with their works: their works __are__ works of iniquity, and the act of violence __is__ in their hands.
@ Their ##feet##note:x[<<Prov 1:16; 6:17; Rom 3:15>>] run to evil, and they make haste to shed innocent blood: their thoughts __are__ thoughts of iniquity; wasting and ##destruction##note:tn[Heb. breaking] __are__ in their paths.
@ The ##way##note:x[<<Luke 1:79; Rom 3:17>>] of peace they know not; and __there is__ no ##judgment##note:tn[or, right] in their goings: they have made them crooked paths: ##whosoever##note:x[<<Isa 57:20–21>>] goeth therein shall not know peace.

@ ¶ Therefore is judgment far from us, neither doth justice overtake us: ##we wait##note:x[<<Isa 5:30>>] for light, but behold obscurity; for brightness, __but__ we walk in darkness.
@ We ##grope##note:x[<<Deut 28:29; Job 5:14>>] for the wall like the blind, and we grope as if __we had__ no eyes: we stumble at noonday as in the night; __we are__ in desolate places as dead __men__.
@ We roar all like bears, and ##mourn##note:x[<<Isa 38:14; Ezek 7:16>>] sore like doves: we look for judgment, but __there is__ none; for salvation, __but__ it is far off from us.
@ For ##our transgressions##note:x[<<Ezra 9:6>>] are multiplied before thee, and ##our sins##note:x[<<Jer 14:7; Hos 5:5>>] testify against us: for our transgressions __are__ with us; and __as for__ our iniquities, we know them;
@ In transgressing and lying against the LORD, and departing away from our God, ##speaking##note:x[<<Mark 7:21–22>>] oppression and revolt, conceiving and uttering from the heart words of falsehood.
@ ##And judgment is turned away backward, and justice standeth afar off: for truth is fallen in the street, and equity cannot enter.##note:x[<<Eccl 3:16; Isa 5:23; Hab 1:4>>]
@ Yea, ##truth##note:x[<<Jer 7:28; Mic 7:2>>] faileth; and ##he __that__##note:x[<<Heb 11:36–38>>] departeth from evil maketh himself a prey: and the LORD saw __it__, and it displeased him that __there was__ no judgment.note:tn[__maketh…__: or, is accounted mad]note:tn[__it displeased…__: Heb. it was evil in his eyes]

@ ¶ And ##he saw##note:x[<<Ps 106:23; Isa 64:7; Ezek 22:30>>] that __there was__ no man, and wondered that __there was__ no intercessor: therefore his arm brought salvation unto him; and his righteousness, it sustained him.

@ And ##the sons##note:x[<<Isa 61:5>>] of strangers shall build up thy walls, and ##their kings##note:x[<<Isa 49:23; Rev 21:26>>] shall minister unto thee: for in my wrath I smote thee, but in my favour have I had mercy on thee.

@ ##The glory##note:x[<<Isa 35:2>>] of Lebanon shall come unto thee, the fir tree, the pine tree, and the box together, to beautify ##the place##note:x[<<1 Chr 28:2; Ps 96:6>>] of my sanctuary; and I will make the place of my feet glorious.

@ For ##brass##note:x[<<Isa 30:26; Zech 12:8; Heb 11:40>>] I will bring gold, and for iron I will bring silver, and for wood brass, and for stones iron: I will also make thy officers peace, and thine exactors righteousness.

=== @63

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Vengeance on the Nations

@ Who __is__ this that cometh ##from Edom##note:x[<<Ps 137:7; Isa 34:5–6>>], with ##dyed##note:x[<<Rev 19:13>>] garments from Bozrah? this __that is__ ##glorious##note:tn[Heb. decked] in his apparel, travelling in the greatness of his strength? I that speak in righteousness, mighty to save.
@ Wherefore __art thou__ red in thine apparel, and thy garments like him that treadeth in the winefat?
@ I have ##trodden##note:x[<<Mal 4:3; Rev 14:19–20; 19:13–15>>] the winepress alone; and of the people __there was__ none with me: for I will tread them in mine anger, and trample them in my fury; and their blood shall be sprinkled upon my garments, and I will stain all my raiment.
@ ##For the day of vengeance __is__ in mine heart, and the year of my redeemed is come.##note:x[<<Isa 34:8; 35:4; 61:2>>]
@ And I ##looked##note:x[<<Isa 41:28; 50:2>>], and __there was__ none to help; and I wondered that __there was__ none to uphold: therefore ##mine own##note:x[<<Ps 44:3>>] arm brought salvation unto me; and my fury, it upheld me.
@ And I will tread down the people in mine anger, and make them drunk in my fury, and I will bring down their strength to the earth.

[discrete.pericope]
==== God’s Mercies Recalled

@ ¶ I will mention the lovingkindnesses of the LORD, __and__ the praises of the LORD, according to all that the LORD hath bestowed on us, and the great goodness toward the house of Israel, which he hath bestowed on them ##according to his##note:x[<<Ps 86:5, 15; Eph 2:4>>] mercies, and according to the multitude of his lovingkindnesses.
@ For he said, ##Surely##note:x[<<Gen 17:7>>] they __are__ my people, children __that__ will not lie: so he was their Saviour.
@ In all their affliction he was afflicted, and ##the angel##note:x[<<Exod 23:20–21; 33:14; Mal 3:1>>] of his presence saved them: in his love and in his pity he redeemed them; and he bare them, and carried them all the days of old.

@ ¶ But they rebelled, and ##vexed##note:x[<<Ps 78:56; Acts 7:51; Eph 4:30>>] his holy Spirit: therefore he was turned to be their enemy, __and__ he fought against them.
@ Then he remembered the days of old, Moses, __and__ his people, __saying__, Where __is__ he that brought them up out of the sea with the note:x[<<Ps 77:20>>]##shepherd##note:tn[or, shepherds] of his flock? ##where __is__ he that put##note:x[<<Num 11:17, 25>>] his holy Spirit within him?
@ That led __them__ by the right hand of Moses ##with##note:x[<<Exod 15:16>>] his glorious arm, ##dividing##note:x[<<Josh 3:16; Ps 78:13>>] the water before them, to make himself an everlasting name?
@ That led them through the deep, as an horse in the wilderness, __that__ they should not stumble?
@ As a beast goeth down into the valley, ##the Spirit##note:x[<<Josh 23:1>>] of the LORD caused him to rest: so didst thou lead thy people, ##to make##note:x[<<Isa 63:12>>] thyself a glorious name.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Prayer for Mercy

[.parallel]
(<<Jeremiah 14:19–22>>)

@ ¶ Look ##down##note:x[<<Deut 26:15; Ps 80:14>>] from heaven, and behold from the habitation of thy holiness and of thy glory: where __is__ thy zeal and thy strength, the sounding of ##thy bowels##note:x[<<Jer 31:20>>] and of thy mercies toward me? are they restrained?note:tn[__the sounding__: or, the multitude]
@ Doubtless ##thou __art__##note:x[<<Deut 32:6; Isa 64:8; Mal 2:10>>] our father, though Abraham be ignorant of us, and Israel acknowledge us not: thou, O LORD, __art__ our father, our redeemer; thy name __is__ from everlasting.note:tn[__our redeemer…__: or, our redeemer from everlasting is thy name]

@ ¶ O LORD, ##why##note:x[<<Ezek 14:7–9>>] hast thou made us to err from thy ways, ##__and__ hardened##note:x[<<Josh 11:20; Isa 6:10>>] our heart from thy fear? Return for thy servants’ sake, the tribes of thine inheritance.
@ The people of thy holiness have possessed __it__ but a little while: ##our##note:x[<<Ps 74:3–7>>] adversaries have trodden down thy sanctuary.
@ We ##are __thine__##note:x[<<Ps 79:6; 135:4; Rom 9:4>>]__:__ thou never barest rule over them; they were not called by thy name.note:tn[__they…__: Heb. thy name was not called upon them]

=== @64

[discrete.pericope]
==== A Prayer for God’s Power

@ ##Oh that##note:x[<<Mark 1:10>>] thou wouldest rend the heavens, ##that thou wouldest come##note:x[<<Exod 19:18–19; Mic 1:3–4>>] down, that the mountains might flow down at thy presence,
@ As __when__ the melting fire burneth, the fire causeth the waters to boil, to make thy name known to thine adversaries, ##__that__ the nations##note:x[<<Ps 99:1; Jer 33:9>>] may tremble at thy presence!note:tn[__the melting…__: Heb. the fire of meltings]
@ When ##thou didst##note:x[<<Exod 34:10>>] terrible things __which__ we looked not for, thou camest down, the mountains flowed down at thy presence.
@ For since the beginning of the world __men__ ##have not##note:x[<<Ps 31:19; 1 Cor 2:9–10>>] heard, nor perceived by the ear, neither hath the eye seen, O God, beside thee, __what__ he hath ##prepared##note:x[<<Ps 31:19>>] for him that waiteth for him.note:tn[__seen…__: or, seen a God beside thee, which doeth so for him, etc]
@ Thou meetest him that rejoiceth and worketh righteousness, __those that__ remember thee in thy ways: behold, thou art wroth; for we have sinned: in those is continuance, and we shall be saved.

@ But we ##are all##note:x[<<Rom 7:18, 24; Eph 2:1–2>>] as an unclean __thing__, and all our righteousnesses __are__ as filthy rags; and we all do fade as a leaf; and our iniquities, like the wind, have taken us away.
@ And ##__there is__##note:x[<<Isa 50:2; 59:16; Ezek 22:30>>] none that calleth upon thy name, that stirreth up himself to take hold of thee: for thou hast hid thy face from us, and hast ##consumed##note:tn[Heb. melted] us, ##because##note:tn[Heb. by the hand] of our iniquities.
@ But now, O LORD, thou __art__ our father; we ##__are__ the clay##note:x[<<Jer 18:2–6>>], and thou our potter; and we ##all __are__##note:x[<<Ps 119:73; Eph 2:10>>] the work of thy hand.

@ ¶ Be not wroth very sore, O LORD, neither remember iniquity for ever: behold, see, we beseech thee, we __are__ all thy people.
@ ##Thy holy cities are a wilderness, Zion is a wilderness, Jerusalem a desolation.##note:x[<<Isa 1:7>>]
@ Our ##holy##note:x[<<2 Kgs 25:9; 2 Chr 36:19; Ps 74:5–7>>] and our beautiful house, where our fathers praised thee, is burned up with fire: and all our pleasant things are laid waste.
@ ##Wilt thou refrain thyself for these __things__, O LORD? wilt thou hold thy peace, and afflict us very sore?##note:x[<<Ps 74:10–11; Isa 42:14>>]

=== @65

[discrete.pericope]
==== Judgments and Promises

[.parallel]
(<<Romans 10:1–21>>)

@ ##I am sought##note:x[<<Rom 9:24–26; 10:20>>] of __them that__ asked not __for me;__ I am found of __them that__ sought me not: I said, Behold me, behold me, ##unto##note:x[<<1 Pet 2:10>>] a nation __that__ was not called by my name.
@ I have ##spread##note:x[<<Prov 1:24; Rom 10:21>>] out my hands all the day unto a rebellious people, which walketh in a way __that was__ not good, ##after##note:x[<<Jer 4:14>>] their own thoughts;
@ ##A people##note:x[<<Deut 32:21>>] that provoketh me to anger continually to my face; ##that sacrificeth##note:x[<<Isa 1:29; 66:17>>] in gardens, and burneth incense upon altars of brick;note:tn[__upon…__: Heb. upon bricks]
@ Which remain among the graves, and lodge in the monuments, ##which eat##note:x[<<Lev 11:7; Isa 66:17>>] swine’s flesh, and ##broth##note:tn[or, pieces] of abominable __things is in__ their vessels;
@ Which say, ##Stand##note:x[<<Luke 7:39; 18:9–12>>] by thyself, come not near to me; for I am holier than thou. ##These##note:x[<<Prov 16:5>>] __are__ a smoke in my ##nose##note:tn[or, anger], a fire that burneth all the day.
@ Behold, __it is__ written before me: ##I will##note:x[<<Ps 50:3, 21>>] not keep silence, ##but##note:x[<<Jer 16:18>>] will recompense, even recompense into their bosom,
@ ##Your iniquities##note:x[<<Exod 20:5>>], and the iniquities of your fathers together, saith the LORD, which have ##burned##note:x[<<Isa 57:7>>] incense upon the mountains, and ##blasphemed##note:x[<<Ezek 20:27–28>>] me upon the hills: therefore will I measure their former work into their bosom.

@ ¶ note:x[<<Isa 6:13; Amos 9:8–9; Rom 9:27–29>>]Thus saith the LORD, As the new wine is found in the cluster, and __one__ saith, Destroy it not; for a blessing __is__ in it: so will I do for my servants’ sakes, that I may not destroy them all.
@ And ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 27:6; Amos 9:11–15>>] bring forth a seed out of Jacob, and out of Judah an inheritor of my mountains: and ##mine elect##note:x[<<Rom 11:5–7>>] shall inherit it, and my servants shall dwell there.
@ And ##Sharon##note:x[<<Isa 33:9; 35:2>>] shall be a fold of flocks, ##and the##note:x[<<Hos 2:15>>] valley of Achor a place for the herds to lie down in, for my people that have sought me.

@ ¶ But ye __are__ ##they that##note:x[<<Isa 1:28>>] forsake the LORD, that forget ##my holy##note:x[<<Isa 2:2>>] mountain, that ##prepare##note:x[<<Deut 32:17>>] a table for that ##troop##note:tn[or, Gad], and that furnish the drink offering unto that ##number##note:tn[or, Meni, a pagan god].
@ Therefore will I number you to the sword, and ye shall all bow down to the slaughter: ##because##note:x[<<2 Chr 36:15–16; Jer 7:13>>] when I called, ye did not answer; when I spake, ye did not hear; but did evil before mine eyes, and did choose __that__ wherein I delighted not.
@ Therefore thus saith the Lord GOD, Behold, ##my servants shall eat##note:x[<<Mal 3:18; Luke 16:24–25>>], but ye shall be hungry: behold, my servants shall drink, but ye shall be thirsty: behold, ##my servants shall rejoice##note:x[<<Isa 66:14>>], but ye shall be ashamed:
@ Behold, ##my servants##note:x[<<Ps 66:4>>] shall sing for joy of heart, but ##ye shall##note:x[<<Matt 8:12>>] cry for sorrow of heart, and shall howl for ##vexation##note:tn[Heb. breaking] of spirit.
@ And ##ye shall##note:x[<<Zech 8:13>>] leave your name for a curse unto my chosen: for the Lord GOD shall slay thee, and call ##his servants##note:x[<<Isa 62:2; Rom 9:26>>] by another name:
@ That ##he who##note:x[<<Jer 4:2>>] blesseth himself in the earth shall bless himself ##in the God##note:x[<<Ps 31:5>>] of truth; and ##he that##note:x[<<Isa 19:18>>] sweareth in the earth shall swear by the God of truth; because the former troubles are forgotten, and because they are hid from mine eyes.

[discrete.pericope]
==== A New Heaven and a New Earth

[.parallel]
(<<Revelation 21:1–8>>)

@ ¶ For, behold, ##I create##note:x[<<Isa 66:22; 2 Pet 3:13; Rev 21:1–5>>] new heavens and a new earth: and the former shall not be remembered, nor come into mind.note:tn[__come…__: Heb. come upon the heart]
@ But be ye glad and rejoice for ever __in that__ which I create: ##for, behold,##note:x[<<Ps 98:1–9; Isa 66:10–14; Rev 11:15–18>>] I create Jerusalem a rejoicing, and her people a joy.
@ And I will rejoice in Jerusalem, and joy in my people: and ##the voice of weeping##note:x[<<Isa 25:8; Rev 7:17; 21:4>>] shall be no more heard in her, nor the voice of crying.
@ ##There shall##note:x[<<Deut 4:40; Job 5:26>>] be no more thence an infant of days, nor an old man that hath not filled his days: for the child shall die an hundred years old; ##but##note:x[<<Eccl 8:12>>] the sinner __being__ an hundred years old shall be accursed.
@ ##And they shall build houses, and inhabit __them;__ and they shall plant vineyards, and eat the fruit of them.##note:x[<<Isa 62:8–9; Jer 31:4–5; Amos 9:14>>]
@ They shall not build, and another inhabit; they shall not plant, and another eat: ##for as##note:x[<<Ps 92:12–14; Isa 65:9, 15>>] the days of a tree __are__ the days of my people, and mine elect shall long enjoy the work of their hands.note:tn[__shall long…__: Heb. shall make them continue long, or, shall wear out]
@ They ##shall##note:x[<<Isa 61:9>>] not labour in vain, nor bring forth ##for##note:x[<<Isa 61:9; Jer 32:38–39>>] trouble; for they __are__ the seed of the blessed of the LORD, and their offspring with them.
@ ##And it shall come to pass, that before they call, I will answer; and while they are yet speaking, I will hear.##note:x[<<Dan 10:12; Mark 11:24; 1 John 5:14–15>>]
@ The ##wolf##note:x[<<Isa 11:6–9; Acts 9:19–21>>] and the lamb shall feed together, and the lion shall eat straw like the bullock: and dust __shall be__ the serpent’s meat. They ##shall not##note:x[<<Mic 4:3>>] hurt nor destroy in all my holy mountain, saith the LORD.

=== @66

[discrete.pericope]
==== Heaven Is My Throne

@ Thus saith the LORD, ##The heaven##note:x[<<Matt 5:34–35; Acts 17:24>>] __is__ my throne, and the earth __is__ my footstool: ##where __is__ the house##note:x[<<Acts 7:48–50>>] that ye build unto me? and where __is__ the place of my rest?
@ For all those __things__ hath mine hand made, and all those __things__ have been, saith the LORD: but ##to this##note:x[<<Ps 34:18; 51:17; Isa 57:15>>] __man__ will I look, __even__ to __him that is__ poor and of a contrite spirit, and trembleth at my word.
@ He that ##killeth##note:x[<<Prov 15:8; Isa 1:11–15; Amos 5:21–22>>] an ox __is as if__ he slew a man; he that sacrificeth a ##lamb##note:tn[or, kid], __as if__ he cut off a dog’s neck; he that offereth an oblation, __as if he offered__ swine’s blood; he that ##burneth##note:tn[Heb. maketh a memorial of] incense, __as if__ he blessed an idol. Yea, they have chosen their own ways, and their soul delighteth in their abominations.
@ I also will choose their ##delusions##note:tn[or, devices], and ##will bring##note:x[<<Prov 10:24>>] their fears upon them; because ##when I called##note:x[<<Prov 1:24; Isa 65:12>>], none did answer; when I spake, they did not hear: but they did evil before mine eyes, and chose __that__ in which I delighted not.

@ ¶ Hear the word of the LORD, ##ye that##note:x[<<Isa 66:2>>] tremble at his word; ##Your##note:x[<<Matt 5:10–12; John 15:18–20>>] brethren that hated you, that cast you out for my name’s sake, said, Let the LORD be glorified: but he shall appear to your joy, and they shall be ashamed.
@ A voice of noise from the city, a voice from the temple, a voice of the LORD that rendereth recompence to his enemies.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Rejoice with Jerusalem

@ ##Before she travailed, she brought forth; before her pain came, she was delivered of a man child.##note:x[<<Rev 12:1–5>>]
@ Who ##hath heard##note:x[<<Isa 64:4>>] such a thing? who hath seen such things? Shall the earth be made to bring forth in one day? __or__ shall a nation be born at once? for as soon as Zion travailed, she brought forth her children.
@ Shall I ##bring to##note:x[<<Gen 18:14; Isa 37:3>>] the birth, and not cause to bring forth? saith the LORD: shall I cause to bring forth, and shut __the womb__? saith thy God.note:tn[__not…__: or, not beget?]
@ ##Rejoice ye##note:x[<<Deut 32:43>>] with Jerusalem, and be glad with her, ##all ye that love##note:x[<<Ps 26:8; 137:6>>] her: rejoice for joy with her, all ye that mourn for her:
@ That ##ye may suck##note:x[<<Isa 60:16; Joel 3:18>>], and be satisfied with the breasts of her consolations; that ye may milk out, and be delighted with the ##abundance##note:tn[or, brightness] of her glory.
@ For thus saith the LORD, Behold, ##I will##note:x[<<Isa 48:18>>] extend peace to her like a river, and ##the glory##note:x[<<Isa 45:14>>] of the Gentiles like a flowing stream: ##then##note:x[<<Isa 60:16>>] shall ye suck, ye shall be borne upon __her__ sides, and be dandled upon __her__ knees.
@ As ##one##note:x[<<Isa 51:3; 1 Thess 2:7>>] whom his mother comforteth, so will I comfort you; and ##ye shall##note:x[<<Isa 66:10>>] be comforted in Jerusalem.
@ And when ye see __this__, ##your heart##note:x[<<Zech 10:7>>] shall rejoice, and ##your bones##note:x[<<Prov 3:8; 17:22>>] shall flourish like an herb: and the hand of the LORD shall be known toward his servants, and __his__ indignation toward his enemies.

[discrete.pericope]
==== Final Judgments against the Wicked

@ For, behold, ##the LORD##note:x[<<Isa 30:33; 2 Thess 1:6–9; 2 Pet 3:10–12>>] will come with fire, and with his chariots like a whirlwind, to render his anger with fury, and his rebuke with flames of fire.
@ ##For by fire and by his sword will the LORD plead with all flesh: and the slain of the LORD shall be many.##note:x[<<Ezek 38:21–22; 39:2–10>>]
@ They that ##sanctify##note:x[<<Isa 65:3–4>>] themselves, and purify themselves in the gardens behind one __tree__ in the midst, ##eating##note:x[<<Lev 11:2–8; Deut 14:3–8>>] swine’s flesh, and the abomination, and the mouse, shall be consumed together, saith the LORD.note:tn[__behind…__: or, one after another]
@ For ##I __know__##note:x[<<John 5:42>>] their works and their thoughts: it shall come, ##that I##note:x[<<Ps 86:9; Rev 11:15>>] will gather all nations and tongues; and they shall come, and see my glory.
@ And I will set a sign among them, and I will send those that escape of them unto the nations, __to__ ##Tarshish##note:x[<<Ezek 27:10>>], Pul, and Lud, that draw the bow, __to__ Tubal, and Javan, __to__ the isles afar off, ##that have##note:x[<<Isa 52:15; Rom 15:21>>] not heard my fame, neither have seen my glory; and they shall declare my glory among the Gentiles.
@ And they shall ##bring all##note:x[<<Isa 60:3–14>>] your brethren __for__ an offering unto the LORD out of all nations upon horses, and in chariots, and in ##litters##note:tn[or, coaches], and upon mules, and upon swift beasts, to my holy mountain Jerusalem, saith the LORD, as the children of Israel bring an offering in a clean vessel into the house of the LORD.
@ ##And I will also take of them for priests __and__ for Levites, saith the LORD.##note:x[<<Exod 19:6; Isa 61:6; 1 Pet 2:9>>]
@ For as ##the new##note:x[<<Isa 65:17; 2 Pet 3:13; Rev 21:1>>] heavens and the new earth, which I will make, shall remain before me, saith the LORD, so shall your seed and your name remain.
@ And it shall come to pass, ##__that__ from##note:x[<<Ezek 46:1, 6>>] one new moon to another, and from one sabbath to another, ##shall all##note:x[<<Ps 86:9>>] flesh come to worship before me, saith the LORD.note:tn[__from one new…__: Heb. from new moon to his new moon, and from sabbath to his sabbath]
@ ##And they##note:x[<<Dan 12:2>>] shall go forth, and look upon the carcases of the men that have transgressed against me: for ##their worm##note:x[<<Isa 14:11; Rev 14:10–11>>] shall not die, neither shall their fire be quenched; and they shall be an abhorring unto all flesh.
